Public PayPal.me payment link, not an account-settings URL. const IFWL_PAYPAL_LINK = "https://paypal.me/IFWL"; const MKT_PART_LABEL = { tyre: "Tyres", engine: "Engine parts", suspension: "Suspension parts", damper: "Damper parts", aero: "Aero parts", fuel: "Race Fuel" }; function discordSafe(v) { return String(v ?? "").trim().replaceAll("@everyone", "@\u200beveryone").replaceAll("@here", "@\u200bhere") || "-"; } function discordClip(v, maxLen = 1200) { const text = discordSafe(v); return text.length <= maxLen ? text : text.slice(0, maxLen - 1) + "..."; } function buildStatementHubLink(ticketId) { const url = new URL(OPEN_STATEMENTS_URL); url.searchParams.set("ticket", ticketId); url.searchParams.set("ticketId", ticketId); return url.toString(); } function buildStewardDeliberationLink(ticketId) { const url = new URL(HEAD_STEWARD_DASHBOARD_URL); url.searchParams.set("ticket", ticketId); url.searchParams.set("ticketId", ticketId); return url.toString(); } async function publishNewTicketToDiscord(ticketId, ticket) { const webhook = NEW_TICKET_DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL; if (!webhook || !webhook.startsWith("https://discord.com/api/webhooks/")) { console.warn("New-ticket Discord webhook is not configured. throw new Error(`Discord new-ticket publish failed: ${resp.status} ${resp.statusText} ${txt}`); } } function isAllowedRedirect(redirectUri) { try { const u = new URL(redirectUri); // Add/remove allowed pages here const allowedPaths = [ "/ACC-driver-classifier/RT.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/ticketportal.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/teamstest.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/appcallback.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/eventsignup.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/tunebuilder.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/livetimings.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/licence.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/raceteamnew.html", "/ACC-driver-classifier/IFWL_ACC_Tune_Image_Builder_v1_8_Patreon_Gate.html", ]; return ( u.origin === "https://ifwlowner.github.io" && allowedPaths.includes(u.pathname) ); } catch { return false; } } function isAllowedRole(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_ASSESSOR_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_RACE_TEAM_ACC_ID) ); } function isAssessor(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && roles.includes(ROLE_ASSESSOR_ID); } function hasRulesAccess(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && roles.includes(ROLE_SIMGRID_EVENT_MEMBER_ID) && roles.includes(ROLE_IFWL_ACTIVE_RACER_ID); } function isActiveRacer(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && roles.includes(ROLE_IFWL_ACTIVE_RACER_ID); } const ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID = "1285573242534363177"; const ROLE_MODERATOR_ID = "1310178152143917127"; const ROLE_OWNER_ID = "1164536220387385364"; const ROLE_STEWARD_ID = "1165343817294172160"; // NEW: grants Owner/Ops-level Live Timings staff access // ✅ ADDED: Commentator - a standalone permission, deliberately independent // of staff/Gold/anything else. Anyone holding this Discord role gets the // Commentator screen regardless of what other roles they do or don't have. const ROLE_COMMENTATOR_ID = "1295114710085271644"; function isCommentator(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && roles.includes(ROLE_COMMENTATOR_ID); } function canPostDiscordSignupList(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_MODERATOR_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) ); } function getPatreonAccess(roles) { if (!Array.isArray(roles)) { return { patreonGranted: false, patreonTier: "none", downloadLimit: 0, staffBypass: false, deniedMessage: PATREON_DENIED_MESSAGE }; } // Owner/Ops bypass Patreon limits if (roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Owner", downloadLimit: PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT, staffBypass: true }; } if (roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Ops Team", downloadLimit: PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT, staffBypass: true }; } // NEW: Steward role gets the same staff-level access as Owner/Ops if (roles.includes(ROLE_STEWARD_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Steward", downloadLimit: PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT, staffBypass: true }; } // Highest Patreon tier wins if a user has multiple roles if (roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_GOLD_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Gold", downloadLimit: PATREON_GOLD_LIMIT, staffBypass: false }; } if (roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_SILVER_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Silver", downloadLimit: PATREON_SILVER_LIMIT, staffBypass: false }; } if (roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_BRONZE_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Bronze", downloadLimit: PATREON_BRONZE_LIMIT, staffBypass: false }; } // Every IFWL ACTIVE RACER gets 1 free community tune download per 30 days. // Patreon roles above override this because their limits are higher. if (roles.includes(ROLE_IFWL_ACTIVE_RACER_ID)) { return { patreonGranted: true, patreonTier: "Active Racer Free", downloadLimit: ACTIVE_RACER_FREE_LIMIT, staffBypass: false }; } return { patreonGranted: false, patreonTier: "none", downloadLimit: 0, staffBypass: false, deniedMessage: PATREON_DENIED_MESSAGE }; } // LIVE TIMINGS detailed access is intentionally stricter than Tune Share access. // Active Racer Free may use Tune Share, but does NOT unlock detailed LIVE TIMINGS. function hasLiveTimingsDetailedAccess(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_STEWARD_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_GOLD_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_SILVER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_BRONZE_ID) ); } async function getPatreonDownloadStatus(userId, username, roles) { const access = getPatreonAccess(roles); if (!access.patreonGranted) { return { ok: true, ...access, userId: String(userId || ""), username: String(username || "user"), downloadsUsed: 0, downloadsRemaining: 0, windowDays: 30 }; } if (access.staffBypass) { return { ok: true, ...access, userId: String(userId || ""), username: String(username || "user"), downloadsUsed: 0, downloadsRemaining: PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT, windowDays: 30 }; } const sinceMs = Date.now() - PATREON_DOWNLOAD_WINDOW_MS; const downloadsRef = db .collection("patreon_tune_usage") .doc(String(userId)) .collection("downloads"); const snap = await downloadsRef.where("createdAtMs", ">=", sinceMs).get(); const used = snap.size; const remaining = Math.max(0, Number(access.downloadLimit || 0) - used); return { ok: true, ...access, userId: String(userId || ""), username: String(username || "user"), downloadsUsed: used, downloadsRemaining: remaining, windowDays: 30 }; } async function recordPatreonDownload(user, downloadType) { const userId = String(user?.sub || user?.userId || ""); const username = String(user?.username || "user"); const roles = Array.isArray(user?.roles) ? user.roles : []; if (!userId) { return { ok: false, allowed: false, error: "Missing user id" }; } const status = await getPatreonDownloadStatus(userId, username, roles); if (!status.patreonGranted) { return { ok: true, allowed: false, patreonGranted: false, message: PATREON_DENIED_MESSAGE, ...status }; } if (status.staffBypass) { return { ok: true, allowed: true, downloadGranted: true, patreonDownloadGranted: true, ...status }; } if (Number(status.downloadsRemaining || 0) <= 0) { return { ok: true, allowed: false, downloadGranted: false, patreonDownloadGranted: false, message: `You have used your ${status.downloadLimit} ${status.patreonTier} tune download(s) for this 30 day period. Upgrade your IFWL Patreon tier to unlock more Tune Share downloads: https://www.patreon.com/c/IFWL`, upgradePrompt: true, upgradeUrl: "https://www.patreon.com/c/IFWL", ...status }; } const nowMs = Date.now(); const downloadsRef = db .collection("patreon_tune_usage") .doc(userId) .collection("downloads"); await downloadsRef.add({ userId, username, patreonTier: status.patreonTier, downloadType: String(downloadType || "tune_png"), createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), createdAtMs: nowMs }); const usedAfter = Number(status.downloadsUsed || 0) + 1; const remainingAfter = Math.max(0, Number(status.downloadLimit || 0) - usedAfter); return { ok: true, allowed: true, downloadGranted: true, patreonDownloadGranted: true, ...status, downloadsUsed: usedAfter, downloadsRemaining: remainingAfter }; } function canManagePatreonTunes(roles) { const access = getPatreonAccess(roles); return !!access.patreonGranted; } function canUploadPatreonTunes(roles) { return canManagePatreonTunes(roles) || isActiveRacer(roles); } function canViewTuneDatabase(roles) { return canManagePatreonTunes(roles) || isActiveRacer(roles); } function canReviewTune(roles) { return canManagePatreonTunes(roles) || isActiveRacer(roles); } function canModeratePatreonTunes(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) ); } // Race Team roster management: Owner, Ops Managers and Assessors can all fully // create/edit/view every team roster (both PC and Console), regardless of which // platform they personally manage. Race Team ACC role is intentionally NOT // included here - that role currently only tags drivers, it is not a management // permission, and nothing in this feature reads it yet. function canManageRaceTeams(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_ASSESSOR_ID) ); } // Race Team ACC is the driver-side tag - up to now it wasn't used for anything. // This is the first feature built on it: any driver holding it can view team // events and submit their own stint availability/acknowledgement, but cannot // create events, allocate stints or finalize a plan - that stays captain-only. function hasRaceTeamAccRole(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && roles.includes(ROLE_RACE_TEAM_ACC_ID); } function canAccessRaceTeamEvents(roles) { return canManageRaceTeams(roles) || hasRaceTeamAccRole(roles); } // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED FOR LICENCE CENTRE — Gold/Silver-plus helpers and staff permission, // built on top of the existing getPatreonAccess() so tier logic stays in one place. // ======================================================= function isGoldAccess(roles) { const access = getPatreonAccess(roles); return !!(access.staffBypass || access.patreonTier === "Gold"); } function isSilverPlusAccess(roles) { const access = getPatreonAccess(roles); return !!(access.staffBypass || access.patreonTier === "Gold" || access.patreonTier === "Silver"); } // ✅ ADDED: "Career Unlock" - a beta-period staff switch (Staff > Season // Window > Career Unlock) that opens Career Shop Gold-locked Shop/Factory // ownership to every IFWL Active Racer, not just Gold Patreon - so the // whole driver base can test the Marketplace economy during beta without // needing to subscribe. Deliberately narrow: it only ever affects the // Gold-lock on owning a production Shop/Factory (see careerShopPurchase // below) and the client-side Season Progression stat-tier reveal - Career // Perks (saveMyCareerPerks) are untouched and stay real-Patreon-tier-gated // no matter what this switch is set to, per explicit instruction. Only // applies to Active Racers, never to a random logged-in Discord member. async function isCareerUnlockedForUser(user) { if (!user?.activeRacer) return false; const snap = await db.doc("league_meta/careerUnlock").get(); return snap.exists ? !!(snap.data() || {}).enabled : false; } // ✅ ADDED: Career System master on/off (Staff > Season Window > Career // System) - deliberately separate from Career Unlock above, which only ever // WIDENS access (Gold-locked stuff opened to everyone). This is a full // stop: while closed, NOBODY buys/sells/lists on the Career Shop, actions a // Backing offer, or locks in Career Perks - Gold Patreon and staffBypass // included. Built for closing the whole Career system during Qualification // and reopening once drivers are placed for the new season. Defaults to // OPEN when the doc doesn't exist yet, so this never silently closes // something that was working before this switch was added. Read-only views // (catalog, your own ledger/perks, marketplace listings, backing overview) // deliberately stay reachable even while closed - see staffCareerSystemStatus // below - so pages can render a "closed" state instead of hard-erroring. async function isCareerSystemOpen() { const snap = await db.doc("league_meta/careerSystemStatus").get(); if (!snap.exists) return true; return (snap.data() || {}).enabled !== false; } async function careerSystemClosedMessage() { const snap = await db.doc("league_meta/careerSystemStatus").get(); const data = snap.exists ? (snap.data() || {}) : {}; return data.closedMessage || "The Career system is closed right now while IFWL preps for the next season."; } async function requireCareerSystemOpen(res) { if (await isCareerSystemOpen()) return true; json(res, 403, { error: await careerSystemClosedMessage(), careerSystemClosed: true }); return false; } // Owner/Ops/Steward manage the Licence Centre - the same staff-level roles // that already get staffBypass treatment everywhere else in this file. function canManageLicences(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_STEWARD_ID) ); } // ✅ ADDED: two tighter staff tiers, layered on top of canManageLicences // ("all staff") for endpoints the nav restructure specifically calls out as // Ops-only or Owner-only - e.g. removing a driver's profile images, or // managing the trophy cabinet/calling cards/system status. Steward is // deliberately excluded from canManageLicencesOpsOnly, and both Steward and // Ops Manager are excluded from canManageLicencesOwnerOnly. function canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) ); } function canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID); } function licenceRef(userId) { return db.collection("licences").doc(String(userId)); } // --------------------------- // ENTRYLIST SERIES CATALOG - the fixed six series IFWL runs. Kept // server-side (not trusted from the client) so a bad/typo'd seriesId from // the ELC tool can never create a stray series or silently misapply the // wrong car-change rule. // --------------------------- const ENTRYLIST_SERIES_CATALOG = [ { id: "pc_pro_am", label: "PC Pro-Am", platform: "pc", allowCarChanges: false }, { id: "pc_beginner", label: "PC Beginner", platform: "pc", allowCarChanges: true }, { id: "pc_monday_funday", label: "PC Monday Funday", platform: "pc", allowCarChanges: true }, { id: "console_pro_am", label: "Console Pro-Am", platform: "console", allowCarChanges: false }, { id: "console_beginner", label: "Console Beginner", platform: "console", allowCarChanges: true }, { id: "console_monday_funday", label: "Console Monday Funday", platform: "console", allowCarChanges: true } ]; function entrylistSeriesById(id) { return ENTRYLIST_SERIES_CATALOG.find((s) => s.id === String(id || "")) || null; } // Latest snapshot for a series without an orderBy+where composite query // (which would need a Firestore composite index that isn't deployed here) - // a plain equality where() only needs Firestore's automatic single-field // index, so this fetches the (small, weekly-cadence) set and sorts in JS. async function latestEntrylistSnapshotDoc(seriesId) { const snap = await db.collection("entrylist_snapshots").where("seriesId", "==", seriesId).limit(200).get(); if (snap.empty) return null; return snap.docs.sort((a, b) => (b.data().capturedAtMs || 0) - (a.data().capturedAtMs || 0))[0]; } // Steam64 -> patreonOutlineTier map, built from every licence profile that // has a self-reported Steam64 on file (licences.steamId). Same full-scan // pattern as ?linkedDriverNames=1 - there's no way to query "who has this // Steam64" any other way, since steamId isn't the doc ID (discordId is). async function buildSteamIdPatreonMap() { const snap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); const map = new Map(); snap.docs.forEach((d) => { const x = d.data() || {}; const sid = String(x.steamId || "").trim(); if (sid) map.set(sid, String(x.patreonOutlineTier || "")); }); return map; } // licences.steamId is a self-reported Steam64 ONLY - there's no PSN/Xbox // equivalent stored anywhere in this system yet, so this only ever resolves // "S"-prefixed (Steam) SimGrid/ACC player IDs. Returns null for anything // else (console P/M ids) so callers can tell "checked, and it's fine" apart // from "can't be checked at all". function bareSteamIdFromPlayerId(playerId) { const m = String(playerId || "").trim().match(/^S(\d{10,20})$/); return m ? m[1] : null; } // ✅ ADDED: shared by every tier-change endpoint that should post a Discord // "licence upgrade" notification - only fires for a genuine promotion (new // tier's order strictly higher than the old one), not a lateral move, // correction, or demotion. async function isTierUpgrade(fromTierName, toTierName) { if (fromTierName === toTierName) return false; const tierListSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/licenceTiers").get(); const tierList = tierListSnap.exists ? (tierListSnap.data()?.tiers || DEFAULT_LICENCE_TIERS) : DEFAULT_LICENCE_TIERS; const fromDef = tierList.find((t) => t.name === fromTierName); const toDef = tierList.find((t) => t.name === toTierName); const fromOrder = fromDef ? Number(fromDef.order) : -1; const toOrder = toDef ? Number(toDef.order) : -1; return toOrder > fromOrder; } async function nextLicenceNumber() { const counterRef = db.doc("league_meta/licenceCounter"); return db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(counterRef); const next = snap.exists ? Number(snap.data()?.next || 1) : 1; tx.set(counterRef, { next: next + 1 }, { merge: true }); return `IFWL-${new Date().getFullYear()}-${String(next).padStart(5, "0")}`; }); } // ✅ ADDED: shared by ?myTrophies=1 (list) and ?myTrophyShowcaseSave=1 // (validation) so both always agree on exactly which trophies a driver // actually won, straight from the trophy_cabinet collection itself - never // trust a driver-submitted trophyId without checking it against this. async function getWonTrophiesForDriver(userId) { const won = []; const cabinetSnap = await db.collection("trophy_cabinet").limit(500).get(); cabinetSnap.docs.forEach((d) => { const x = d.data() || {}; if (x.active === false) return; for (const place of ["gold", "silver", "bronze"]) { const p = x[place]; if (p && p.discordId === userId) { won.push({ trophyId: `${d.id}:${place}`, entryId: d.id, place, eventName: x.eventName || "Unnamed Event", createdAtMs: x.createdAtMs || 0, tierColor: p.tierColor || "#93a1a9", trophyImageUrl: p.trophyImageUrl || "" }); } } }); // ✅ ADDED: individually-awarded "achievement" trophies - not tied to a // top-3 event placement, just staff recognising a driver for whatever // reason they choose (e.g. "Most Improved", "Clean Race Award"). Module-level (not inside rtGate's // closure) so it stays reachable if any other export ever needs it too. // \u2705 ADDED: staff-editable message templates - starting with the two // pieces of copy directly tied to a licence refusal (the Discord post and // the on-page gate a refused driver actually sees). Please head to https://www.ifwl.net and complete driver sign up.", licenceRefusedPageMessage: "Your IFWL profile isn't complete yet, so a licence can't be issued. Please head to www.ifwl.net and complete driver sign up - once that's done, refresh this page and your licence will be issued automatically.", // \u2705 ADDED: a staff-toggleable red banner shown above "Your ACC driver // name(s)" on every driver's own My Licence page - built to answer the // "why haven't I been given a tier yet" question that keeps coming up on // Discord, without staff having to repeat themselves every time. licenceBetaNoticeEnabled: false, licenceBetaNoticeText: "The system is in BETA - no licences are issued apart from PC Beginner series as the system is under stress testing." }; async function logRaceTeamEventAudit({ teamId, eventId, action, actor, details }) { try { await db.collection("race_team_event_audit").add({ teamId: String(teamId || ""), eventId: String(eventId || ""), action: String(action || ""), // "created" | "updated" | "finalized" | "deleted" actorUserId: actor?.userId || "", actorName: actor?.nick || actor?.username || "Unknown", details: details || {}, atMs: Date.now(), at: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }); } catch (e) { // Audit logging must never block or fail the actual action it's // recording - a logging hiccup shouldn't stop a real team action. logger.warn("logRaceTeamEventAudit failed", e); } } // Resolves an event's preview image straight from SimGrid's own og:image meta // tag - confirmed present on real SimGrid event pages. Browsers can't fetch // this directly (CORS), so it's resolved here server-side, once, at save // time, and cached on the event document. Only ever fetches thesimgrid.com // itself - this deliberately does not become a general-purpose URL fetcher. // ✅ ADDED: without this, a link pasted without "https://" (e.g. staff typing // "thesimgrid.com/events/xyz" straight from a browser address bar) gets saved // verbatim, and when later used as an , the browser treats it as a // RELATIVE path and silently prepends the current page's own address in // front of it - producing exactly the "GitHub link before the link we add" // bug. Normalizing here, server-side, means every consumer of a saved // simgridLink (Available Races, Race Team Events, wherever else) is // guaranteed a real absolute URL, regardless of which form saved it. function normalizeUrlScheme(url) { const trimmed = String(url || "").trim(); if (!trimmed) return ""; if (/^https?:\/\//i.test(trimmed)) return trimmed; return "https://" + trimmed.replace(/^\/+/, ""); } async function fetchSimgridOgImage(url) { try { const u = new URL(url); const host = u.hostname.toLowerCase(); if (host !== "thesimgrid.com" && !host.endsWith(".thesimgrid.com")) return ""; const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; IFWLBot/1.0)" } }); if (!res.ok) return ""; const html = await res.text(); const m1 = html.match(/]+property=["']og:image["'][^>]+content=["']([^"']+)["']/i); if (m1) return m1[1]; const m2 = html.match(/]+content=["']([^"']+)["'][^>]+property=["']og:image["']/i); return m2 ? m2[1] : ""; } catch (e) { logger.warn("fetchSimgridOgImage failed", e); return ""; } } // ✅ ADDED: best-effort round/schedule detection for a SimGrid championship. // SimGrid has no public API - this scrapes the plain visible text of a // championship's own "/races" sub-page, which (confirmed by fetching a real // example championship directly) consistently lists each round with a // "Track" label and a "Date" label right after it, e.g. "Track Nurburgring // ... Date Sep 28, '21 18:30 Ended". This is inherently fragile - it relies // on SimGrid's page text staying in this shape, not on any documented // contract - so it's built to degrade gracefully: a round it can't fully // parse still comes back with whatever it DID find, rather than silently // dropping it, so staff can see what needs filling in by hand. function parseSimgridDateText(text) { const s = String(text || "").trim(); const months = { jan:0,feb:1,mar:2,apr:3,may:4,jun:5,jul:6,aug:7,sep:8,oct:9,nov:10,dec:11 }; // ✅ FIXED: confirmed against a real private invite link that SimGrid // sometimes omits the year entirely - "Aug 3, 14:00" with no year, not // "Sep 28, '21 18:30" like the public example this was first built // against. Try WITH a year first, then fall back to inferring one. let m = s.match(/([A-Za-z]{3})\s+(\d{1,2}),?\s*'?(\d{4}|\d{2})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); if (m) { const [, mon, day, yy, hh, mi] = m; const year = yy.length === 2 ? 2000 + Number(yy) : Number(yy); const monthIdx = months[mon.toLowerCase().slice(0,3)]; if (monthIdx === undefined) return 0; const ms = Date.UTC(year, monthIdx, Number(day), Number(hh), Number(mi)); return Number.isFinite(ms) ? ms : 0; } m = s.match(/([A-Za-z]{3})\s+(\d{1,2}),?\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/); if (m) { const [, mon, day, hh, mi] = m; const monthIdx = months[mon.toLowerCase().slice(0,3)]; if (monthIdx === undefined) return 0; const now = Date.now(); const thisYear = new Date().getUTCFullYear(); let ms = Date.UTC(thisYear, monthIdx, Number(day), Number(hh), Number(mi)); // If that lands more than ~4 months in the past, it's almost certainly // meant to be next year rather than an event that already happened. if (ms < now - (120 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)) { ms = Date.UTC(thisYear + 1, monthIdx, Number(day), Number(hh), Number(mi)); } return Number.isFinite(ms) ? ms : 0; } return 0; } async function fetchSimgridRounds(championshipUrl) { const u = new URL(championshipUrl); const host = u.hostname.toLowerCase(); if (host !== "thesimgrid.com" && !host.endsWith(".thesimgrid.com")) { throw new Error("Only thesimgrid.com links are supported"); } // ✅ FIXED: this was appending "/races" to the raw URL STRING, which lands // after any query string (e.g. "...26328?s=qIJjYXo6/races" - invalid). // Private invite links always carry a "?s=..." token, so this broke every // single one of them. Build it from the parsed URL's own pathname/search // instead, so /races is correctly inserted into the path, before the // query string. const racesPath = u.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "").endsWith("/races") ? u.pathname : u.pathname.replace(/\/+$/, "") + "/races"; const racesUrl = `${u.protocol}//${u.host}${racesPath}${u.search}`; const res = await fetch(racesUrl, { headers: { "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; IFWLBot/1.0)" } }); if (!res.ok) { // ✅ ADDED: SimGrid started fronting (at least some of) their pages with // Cloudflare's bot-check ("Performing security verification...") some // time around August 2026 - a plain server-side fetch like this one gets // served that challenge page instead of the real content and can never // pass it (it requires a real browser running Cloudflare's JS, which is // the whole point of the check). That's a completely different failure // from "this is a private link" and staff were being told the wrong // thing, so this sniffs the response body for Cloudflare's own markers // and says so plainly instead of guessing "private/invite-only" every // time. Either way, "Preview rounds" is the only thing affected - the // manual per-event fields on this same panel don't depend on this fetch // at all and still work. const isCloudflareChallenge = res.headers.get("server") === "cloudflare" && [403, 503].includes(res.status); let bodySnippet = ""; if (isCloudflareChallenge) { try { bodySnippet = (await res.text()).slice(0, 500); } catch (e) { /* ignore - just skip the extra sniff */ } } if (isCloudflareChallenge && /cloudflare|security verification|cf-browser-verification/i.test(bodySnippet)) { throw new Error(`SimGrid's own bot-protection (Cloudflare) blocked this automated request - "Preview rounds" can't get past that from our server, this isn't something wrong with the link. Add this event's round(s) manually using the fields below instead.`); } throw new Error(`SimGrid returned ${res.status} for ${racesUrl} - the link may be private/invite-only and not reachable without a logged-in session`); } const html = await res.text(); // Strip tags down to plain visible text - more robust than guessing exact // class names/markup, since the label text itself ("Track", "Date") is // what's stable, not the surrounding HTML structure. const text = html .replace(//gi, " ") .replace(//gi, " ") .replace(/<[^>]+>/g, " ") .replace(/ /g, " ") .replace(/\s+/g, " ") .trim(); const roundChunks = text.split(/(?=Round\s+\d+)/i).filter((c) => /^Round\s+\d+/i.test(c)); if (!roundChunks.length) { // ✅ ADDED: a debug snippet of what was actually fetched, so a failure // here is diagnosable rather than a dead end - if the page structure // genuinely doesn't match what this was built against, staff (or a // follow-up fix) can see exactly what came back instead of guessing. return { rounds: [], warning: "Couldn't find any rounds on this page - it may be a private/invite-only championship, a single-event link rather than a championship, or SimGrid's page layout may not match what this was built against.", debugFetchedUrl: racesUrl, debugTextSnippet: text.slice(0, 1500) }; } const rounds = roundChunks.slice(0, 60).map((chunk) => { const roundNumMatch = chunk.match(/^Round\s+(\d+)/i); // ✅ FIXED: strip known leading noise tokens one at a time rather than // trying to match them as one fixed literal phrase - confirmed against // a real link that which tabs appear (Info/Schedule/Admin) varies per // round, and a single rigid pattern either missed rounds entirely or // swallowed the noise into the captured "name". let rest = chunk.replace(/^Round\s+\d+\s*/i, ""); let stripped = true; while (stripped) { const before = rest; rest = rest.replace(/^(?:DLC|Info|Schedule|Admin)\b\s*/i, ""); stripped = rest !== before; } const nameMatch = rest.match(/^([^-]{2,80}?)\s*-\s*Track/i); const trackMatch = chunk.match(/Track\s+([A-Za-z0-9 .'-]{3,50}?)\s+-\s*Date/i); // Year is now optional in this capture too - matches both "Sep 28, '21 // 18:30" and the year-less "Aug 3, 14:00" real-world format. const dateTextMatch = chunk.match(/Date\s+([A-Za-z]{3}\s+\d{1,2},?\s*'?(?:\d{2,4})?\s*\d{1,2}:\d{2})/i); const dateText = dateTextMatch ? dateTextMatch[1].trim() : ""; return { roundNumber: roundNumMatch ? Number(roundNumMatch[1]) : null, name: nameMatch ? nameMatch[1].trim() : `Round ${roundNumMatch ? roundNumMatch[1] : "?"}`, track: trackMatch ? trackMatch[1].trim() : "", dateText, dateMs: parseSimgridDateText(dateText), ended: /\bEnded\b/i.test(chunk.split("Date")[1]?.slice(0, 60) || "") }; }); return { rounds, warning: rounds.some((r) => !r.track || !r.dateMs) ? "Some rounds parsed only partially - double-check them against the SimGrid page itself before relying on the dates shown." : "" }; } // Silver/Gold Patreon (and Owner/Ops) get the same full sector/consistency/ // Session Risk breakdown on ANY session type they click - not just races. // Deliberately excludes Bronze, matching the requested tier split exactly. function hasFullSessionBreakdownAccess(roles) { return Array.isArray(roles) && ( roles.includes(ROLE_OWNER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_OPS_MANAGER_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_GOLD_ID) || roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_SILVER_ID) ); } // Roster drivers were originally saved as plain name strings. This now also // carries an optional SimGrid link per driver, so a driver entry can be either // a legacy plain string OR an {name, simgridLink} object. Normalizing here // means older saved teams (like any created before this change) keep working // exactly as before - they just show an empty SimGrid link until someone adds one. function normalizeRosterDriver(d) { if (typeof d === "string") { const name = d.slice(0, 80); return name ? { name, simgridLink: "" } : null; } if (d && typeof d === "object") { const name = String(d.name || "").slice(0, 80); const simgridLink = String(d.simgridLink || d.simgrid || "").slice(0, 300); return name ? { name, simgridLink } : null; } return null; } function hasProhibitedTuneName(value) { return /(jadier|jardier|jlopez|cda)/i.test(String(value || "")); } function tuneHasProhibitedName(tune, meta = {}) { const safeTune = typeof tune === "object" && tune ? tune : {}; const checks = [ meta.fileName, meta.title, safeTune.name, safeTune.title, safeTune.fileName, safeTune.raw?.fileName, safeTune.raw?.name ]; return checks.some(hasProhibitedTuneName); } function tuneMetaFromPayload(tune) { const safeTune = typeof tune === "object" && tune ? tune : {}; return { title: String(safeTune.title || safeTune.name || "IFWL Tune").slice(0, 120), car: String(safeTune.car || safeTune.raw?.carName || "").slice(0, 120), track: String(safeTune.track || "").slice(0, 120), session: String(safeTune.session || "").slice(0, 80), platform: String(safeTune.platform || "").slice(0, 80), notes: String(safeTune.notes || "").slice(0, 500), fileName: String(safeTune.name || safeTune.title || safeTune.raw?.fileName || "").slice(0, 180) }; } function cleanTuneDisplayName(value) { if (value === null || value === undefined) return value; let out = String(value); // Remove ACC temperature tokens such as "30c", "38C", "30 C" or "30°C". // Catches names like "BARCELONA\30C IFWL.NET..." and "BARCELONA/30C...". out = out.replace(/(^|[\s_\-\\/]+)\d{1,3}\s*°?\s*[cC]\b/gi, " "); out = out.replace(/\b\d{1,3}\s*°?\s*[cC]\b/gi, " "); // Remove shorthand session variants that should not be shown in public names. out = out.replace(/\b(RF|QF|RS)\b/gi, " "); // Expand valid single-letter session tokens. out = out.replace(/\bQ\b/gi, "Qually"); out = out.replace(/\bR\b/gi, "Race"); out = out.replace(/\bP\b/gi, "Practice"); // The library wording standard is RAIN, not WET. Use a word boundary so Watkins etc is untouched. out = out.replace(/\bWET\b/gi, "RAIN"); // Tidy spacing while leaving IFWL.NET and other names untouched. out = out.replace(/[\\/]+/g, " "); out = out.replace(/[_\-]{2,}/g, " "); out = out.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim(); out = out.replace(/\s+([.)\]])/g, "$1"); out = out.replace(/([([\]])\s+/g, "$1"); return out; } function collectTuneNameCleanupUpdates(x) { const updates = {}; const beforeAfter = []; const fields = [ "title", "displayTitle", "fileName", "sourceFileName", "originalFileName", "name", "tune.title", "tune.displayTitle", "tune.name", "tune.fileName", "tune.sourceFileName", "tune.originalFileName", "tune.raw.fileName", "tune.raw.name", "tune.raw.title" ]; for (const path of fields) { const parts = path.split("."); let cur = x; for (const part of parts) cur = cur && typeof cur === "object" ? cur[part] : undefined; if (typeof cur !== "string" || !cur.trim()) continue; const cleaned = cleanTuneDisplayName(cur); if (cleaned && cleaned !== cur) { updates[path] = cleaned; beforeAfter.push({ field: path, before: cur, after: cleaned }); } } if (Object.keys(updates).length) { updates.nameCleanedAt = admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(); updates.nameCleanedAtMs = Date.now(); updates.nameCleanRules = "name_cleanup_temp_wet_session_tokens_v3"; } return { updates, beforeAfter }; } function getSessionAuthorName(user) { return String(user?.nick || user?.globalName || user?.username || "IFWL").trim(); } function tuneEntitlementRef(userId, tuneId) { return db .collection("patreon_tune_entitlements") .doc(String(userId)) .collection("tunes") .doc(String(tuneId)); } async function hasTuneEntitlement(userId, tuneId) { if (!userId || !tuneId) return false; const snap = await tuneEntitlementRef(userId, tuneId).get(); return snap.exists; } async function saveTuneEntitlement(user, tuneId, tuneData) { const userId = String(user?.sub || user?.userId || ""); if (!userId || !tuneId) return; const nowMs = Date.now(); const x = tuneData || {}; await tuneEntitlementRef(userId, tuneId).set({ tuneId: String(tuneId), userId, username: String(user?.username || "user"), globalName: String(user?.globalName || ""), nick: String(user?.nick || ""), title: String(x.title || x.tune?.title || "IFWL Tune"), car: String(x.car || x.tune?.car || ""), track: String(x.track || x.tune?.track || ""), session: String(x.session || x.tune?.session || ""), platform: String(x.platform || x.tune?.platform || ""), authorName: String(x.authorName || x.createdBy?.authorName || x.createdBy?.nick || x.createdBy?.username || "IFWL"), firstDownloadedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), firstDownloadedAtMs: nowMs, lastLoadedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), lastLoadedAtMs: nowMs, reloadCount: 0 }, { merge: true }); } async function touchTuneEntitlement(userId, tuneId) { if (!userId || !tuneId) return; await tuneEntitlementRef(userId, tuneId).set({ lastLoadedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), lastLoadedAtMs: Date.now(), reloadCount: admin.firestore.FieldValue.increment(1) }, { merge: true }); } function renderStarsNumber(n) { const v = Number(n || 0); return Math.max(0, Math.min(5, Math.round(v * 10) / 10)); } async function recalcTuneRating(tuneId) { const reviewsSnap = await db.collection("patreon_tunes").doc(String(tuneId)).collection("reviews").get(); let total = 0; let count = 0; reviewsSnap.docs.forEach((doc) => { const stars = Number(doc.data()?.stars || 0); if (stars >= 1 && stars <= 5) { total += stars; count += 1; } }); const avg = count ? Math.round((total / count) * 10) / 10 : 0; const aggregate = { ratingAvg: avg, ratingCount: count, ratingTotal: total, ratingUpdatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), ratingUpdatedAtMs: Date.now() }; await db.collection("patreon_tunes").doc(String(tuneId)).set(aggregate, { merge: true }); return aggregate; } // base64url helpers function b64url(input) { const buf = Buffer.isBuffer(input) ? input : Buffer.from(String(input)); return buf.toString("base64").replace(/=/g, "").replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_"); } function signSession(payloadObj, secret) { const crypto = require("crypto"); const payload = b64url(JSON.stringify(payloadObj)); const sig = b64url(crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest()); return `${payload}.${sig}`; } function verifySession(token, secret) { const crypto = require("crypto"); if (!token || typeof token !== "string") return { ok: false, error: "Missing token" }; const parts = token.split("."); if (parts.length !== 2) return { ok: false, error: "Bad token format" }; const [payload, sig] = parts; const expected = b64url(crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload).digest()); if (sig !== expected) return { ok: false, error: "Bad signature" }; let obj = null; try { obj = JSON.parse( Buffer.from(payload.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/"), "base64").toString("utf8") ); } catch { return { ok: false, error: "Bad payload" }; } if (!obj?.exp || Date.now() > obj.exp) return { ok: false, error: "Token expired" }; return { ok: true, user: obj }; } function requireAuth(req, secret) { const auth = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const token = auth.startsWith("Bearer ") ? auth.slice(7) : ""; return verifySession(token, secret); } // Discord OAuth helpers async function tokenExchange(code, redirectUri, clientSecret) { const params = new URLSearchParams(); params.set("client_id", DISCORD_CLIENT_ID); params.set("client_secret", clientSecret); params.set("grant_type", "authorization_code"); params.set("code", code); params.set("redirect_uri", redirectUri); const out = await fetchJson("https://discord.com/api/oauth2/token", { method: "POST", headers: { "Content-Type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" }, body: params }); if (!out.ok) throw new Error(`Token exchange failed (status ${out.status}): ${out.text}`); return out.json; } async function getUser(accessToken) { const out = await fetchJson("https://discord.com/api/v10/users/@me", { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } }); if (!out.ok) throw new Error(`User fetch failed (status ${out.status}): ${out.text}`); return out.json; } async function getMember(userId, botToken) { const out = await fetchJson( `https://discord.com/api/v10/guilds/${encodeURIComponent(GUILD_ID)}/members/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}`, { headers: { Authorization: `Bot ${botToken}` } } ); if (out.status === 404) return { member: null, status: 404, raw: out.json || out.text }; if (!out.ok) throw new Error(`Member fetch failed (status ${out.status}): ${out.text}`); return { member: out.json, status: out.status, raw: out.json }; } // ✅ ADDED: grant/revoke a single Discord role on a guild member - the // write-side counterpart to getMember's read-only role checks above. Used // by the per-event "unlock this area on IFWL Discord" feature (see // unlockEventDiscordRole and syncEventDiscordRoleGrants below). Both are // idempotent on Discord's side (adding a role you already have, or // removing one you don't, both just return 204), so callers never need to // pre-check membership before calling either. async function addDiscordMemberRole(userId, roleId, botToken) { return fetchJson( `https://discord.com/api/v10/guilds/${encodeURIComponent(GUILD_ID)}/members/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}/roles/${encodeURIComponent(roleId)}`, { method: "PUT", headers: { Authorization: `Bot ${botToken}` } } ); } async function removeDiscordMemberRole(userId, roleId, botToken) { return fetchJson( `https://discord.com/api/v10/guilds/${encodeURIComponent(GUILD_ID)}/members/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}/roles/${encodeURIComponent(roleId)}`, { method: "DELETE", headers: { Authorization: `Bot ${botToken}` } } ); } // ✅ ADDED: keeps a driver's per-event Discord role grants in sync with // their CURRENT tier, called every time they open Available Races - same // "live, not a snapshot" philosophy already used for the race list itself // (see myAvailableRaces below). If they were promoted/demoted since they // last unlocked a race's Discord area, or staff changed/removed that // event's gated role entirely, this quietly revokes any grant that's no // longer valid on Discord and deletes its record. Returns the set of // eventIds whose grant is still valid, so the caller can mark those // events as already-unlocked for the driver. async function syncEventDiscordRoleGrants(db, userId, myTier, botToken) { const grantsSnap = await db.collection("eventRoleGrants").where("discordId", "==", userId).get(); if (grantsSnap.empty) return new Set(); const stillValid = new Set(); await Promise.all(grantsSnap.docs.map(async (g) => { const grant = g.data() || {}; try { const eventSnap = await db.collection("simgrid_events").doc(String(grant.eventId || "")).get(); const ev = eventSnap.exists ? eventSnap.data() || {} : null; const stillEligible = !!ev && ev.active !== false && ev.discordRoleId === grant.roleId && Array.isArray(ev.tiers) && ev.tiers.includes(myTier); if (stillEligible) { stillValid.add(grant.eventId); return; } if (grant.roleId) await removeDiscordMemberRole(userId, grant.roleId, botToken); await g.ref.delete(); } catch (e) { logger.warn(`syncEventDiscordRoleGrants: failed to reconcile grant ${g.id}`, e); } })); return stillValid; } // ======================================================= // rtGate — Discord OAuth + role checks + session issuing // FIXED: returns nick (guild nickname) + globalName // UPDATED: issues sessionToken also for IFWL ACTIVE RACER (needed for image upload auth) // ======================================================= exports.rtGate = onRequest( { region: "europe-west2", secrets: [RT_SESSION_SECRET, DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET, DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN, LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK] }, (req, res) => c(req, res, async () => { try { const debug = req.query.debug === "1"; const issue = req.query.issue === "1"; const secret = RT_SESSION_SECRET.value(); const clientSecret = DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET.value(); const botToken = DISCORD_BOT_TOKEN.value(); const licenceBotWebhook = LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK.value(); // --------------------------- // PUBLIC TUNE DATABASE COUNT // --------------------------- if (req.query.patreonTuneCount === "1") { const snap = await db.collection("patreon_tunes").where("active", "==", true).get(); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, tuneCount: snap.size }); } // --------------------------- // PUBLIC TUNE DATABASE STATS // Top rated, most downloaded, and top contributors. const activeTunes = tunesSnap.docs .map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...(d.data() || {}) })) .filter((x) => x && x.active !== false && x.deleted !== true && x.removed !== true); const downloadDocMap = new Map(); try { const groupSnap = await db.collectionGroup("downloads").where("createdAtMs", ">=", sinceMs).limit(PATREON_TUNE_LIST_LIMIT).get(); for (const d of groupSnap.docs || []) downloadDocMap.set(d.ref.path, d); } catch (e) { logger.error("admin overview downloads collectionGroup failed; using per-user fallback", e); } // Fallback/backup: walk patreon_tune_usage/{userId}/downloads so the overview still works // even if collectionGroup indexes are not ready or behave differently in the project. try { const usageUsers = await db.collection("patreon_tune_usage").listDocuments(); for (const userRef of usageUsers.slice(0, 600)) { const userDownloads = await userRef.collection("downloads").where("createdAtMs", ">=", sinceMs).limit(500).get(); for (const d of userDownloads.docs || []) downloadDocMap.set(d.ref.path, d); } } catch (e) { logger.error("admin overview per-user downloads fallback failed", e); } const downloadDocs = [...downloadDocMap.values()]; let entitlementSnap = { docs: [] }; try { entitlementSnap = await db.collectionGroup("tunes").limit(PATREON_TUNE_LIST_LIMIT).get(); } catch (e) { logger.error("admin overview entitlements collectionGroup failed", e); } const knownUsers = new Map(); for (const d of entitlementSnap.docs || []) { const x = d.data() || {}; const userId = safeStr(x.userId); if (!userId) continue; const name = safeStr(x.nick || x.globalName || x.username || userId, userId); const current = knownUsers.get(userId) || { userId, name, username: safeStr(x.username), nick: safeStr(x.nick), globalName: safeStr(x.globalName), lastSeenAtMs: 0 }; current.name = current.name || name; current.username = current.username || safeStr(x.username); current.nick = current.nick || safeStr(x.nick); current.globalName = current.globalName || safeStr(x.globalName); current.lastSeenAtMs = Math.max(safeNum(current.lastSeenAtMs), safeNum(x.lastLoadedAtMs || x.firstDownloadedAtMs || x.createdAtMs)); knownUsers.set(userId, current); } const downloads = (downloadDocs || []).map((d) => { const x = d.data() || {}; const name = knownUsers.get(userId)?.name || safeStr(x.nick || x.globalName || x.username || userId, userId || "Unknown user"); return { id: d.id, userId, name, username: safeStr(x.username), patreonTier: tier, downloadType: safeStr(x.downloadType), tuneId: parsedTuneId, createdAtMs: safeNum(x.createdAtMs) }; }).sort((a, b) => b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs); const downloadUsersMap = new Map(); for (const d of downloads) { if (!d.userId) continue; const cur = downloadUsersMap.get(d.userId) || { userId: d.userId, name: d.name, username: d.username, patreonTier: d.patreonTier, downloadsUsed: 0, downloadLimit: tierLimit(d.patreonTier), downloadsRemaining: 0, lastDownloadAtMs: 0, downloadTypes: {} }; cur.name = cur.name || d.name; cur.username = cur.username || d.username; cur.patreonTier = d.patreonTier || cur.patreonTier; cur.downloadLimit = tierLimit(cur.patreonTier); cur.downloadsUsed += 1; cur.lastDownloadAtMs = Math.max(cur.lastDownloadAtMs, d.createdAtMs); cur.downloadTypes[d.downloadType || "download"] = (cur.downloadTypes[d.downloadType || "download"] || 0) + 1; downloadUsersMap.set(d.userId, cur); } const downloadUsers = [...downloadUsersMap.values()].map((u) => ({ ...u, downloadsRemaining: u.downloadLimit >= PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT ? PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT : Math.max(0, u.downloadLimit - u.downloadsUsed), maxedOut: u.downloadLimit > 0 && u.downloadLimit < PATREON_STAFF_BYPASS_LIMIT && u.downloadsUsed >= u.downloadLimit })).sort((a, b) => b.downloadsUsed - a.downloadsUsed || b.lastDownloadAtMs - a.lastDownloadAtMs); uploadersMap.set(key, cur); } const uploaders = [...uploadersMap.values()].sort((a, b) => b.uploadCount - a.uploadCount || b.lastUploadAtMs - a.lastUploadAtMs); const recentUploads = activeTunes .map((t) => ({ id: t.id, title: safeStr(t.title || t.tune?.title || "IFWL Tune"), car: safeStr(t.car || t.tune?.car), track: safeStr(t.track || t.tune?.track), session: safeStr(t.session || t.tune?.session), authorName: safeStr(t.authorName || t.createdBy?.authorName || t.createdBy?.nick || t.createdBy?.username || "IFWL"), createdAtMs: safeNum(t.createdAtMs || t.updatedAtMs), downloadCount: safeNum(t.downloadCount), ratingAvg: renderStarsNumber(t.ratingAvg), ratingCount: safeNum(t.ratingCount) })) .sort((a, b) => b.createdAtMs - a.createdAtMs) .slice(0, 20); Safe to call on every // login: welcomeBonusPaidAtMs guards it to exactly once, ever. // ======================================================= const DEFAULT_WELCOME_BONUS = { gold: 60000, silver: 40000, bronze: 25000, enabled: true }; async function maybePayWelcomeBonus(driverId, priorData, outlineTier) { if (!outlineTier) return; if (priorData?.welcomeBonusPaidAtMs) return; const cfgSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/welcomeBonus").get(); const cfg = cfgSnap.exists ? cfgSnap.data() : {}; if (cfg.enabled === false) return; const amount = outlineTier === "gold" ? "both" : body.platformAdd; } // ✅ ADDED: SimGrid username + Steam64 ID - self-reported, not // tier-gated (same reasoning as accDriverNames/platform above: this // helps STAFF match drivers faster, e.g. the entrylist // cross-reference tool, so it should be as easy as possible for // every Active Racer to fill in, not locked behind Gold). Steam64 // IDs are always a run of digits - loosely validated as 10-20 // digits rather than a hardcoded exact length, since Valve doesn't // publicly guarantee the length never changes. if (typeof body.simgridUsername === "string") { updates.simgridUsername = body.simgridUsername.trim().slice(0, 80); } if (typeof body.steamId === "string") { const cleaned = body.steamId.trim(); updates.steamId = /^\d{10,20}$/.test(cleaned) ? cleaned : ""; } // ✅ ADDED: name pronunciation guide - purely for commentators, never // shown anywhere public. Same non-gated tier as everything else on // this driver-identity block. if (typeof body.namePronunciation === "string") { updates.namePronunciation = body.namePronunciation.trim().slice(0, 200); } // Silver/Gold only if (isSilverPlusAccess(roles)) { if (typeof body.bio === "string") updates.bio = body.bio.slice(0, 800); if (typeof body.callingCardUrl === "string") updates.callingCardUrl = body.callingCardUrl.slice(0, 1000); // ✅ ADDED: Dark Mode - Silver+ perk, same pattern as the Gold-only // Site Accent Theme just above (a purely cosmetic reskin of the // driver's OWN view, boolean not free text, never visible to // anyone else). Gated one tier lower than the accent theme // because it's a full theme swap rather than a curated colour // pick, so there's no "unreadable combination" risk to guard // against - a plain boolean is safe to trust directly. if (typeof body.siteDarkTheme === "boolean") updates.siteDarkTheme = body.siteDarkTheme; } // Gold only - stores the URL returned by ?licenceImageUpload=1, never raw bytes if (isGoldAccess(roles)) { if (typeof body.photoUploadUrl === "string") updates.photoUploadUrl = body.photoUploadUrl.slice(0, 1000); if (typeof body.photoUploadRef === "string") updates.photoUploadRef = body.photoUploadRef.slice(0, 20); if (typeof body.headerUploadUrl === "string") updates.headerUploadUrl = body.headerUploadUrl.slice(0, 1000); if (typeof body.headerUploadRef === "string") updates.headerUploadRef = body.headerUploadRef.slice(0, 20); // ✅ ADDED: Site Accent Theme - Gold cosmetic perk. Purely a CSS // reskin of the driver's OWN view of the whole Licence Centre // (not visible to anyone else) - curated palette only, never a // free-text hex value, so nobody can end up with an unreadable // colour combination on their own site. if (typeof body.accentTheme === "string") { const validThemes = ["teal", "crimson", "ocean", "royal", "sunset", "emerald", "hotpink", "chrome"]; updates.accentTheme = validThemes.includes(body.accentTheme) ? body.accentTheme : "teal"; } } // ✅ ADDED: My Shop customization - a driver's shop name (shown as a // big title on their public shop page, visited via "Visit Shop for // Offers" links on the Marketplace) and background image. Gated on // owning at least one Shop/Factory (not on Patreon tier) - the whole // My Shop area on career.html only exists once a driver owns one of // tyreFactory/fuelDepot/engineShop/tuningShop/aeroShop, so editing // these without owning one would just be dead data nobody could // ever see. Background comes from ?rtLicenceImageUpload=1&kind= // careerShopBackground (never raw bytes), same pattern as photo/header. // ✅ ADDED: careerShopBio - a short free-text blurb shown under the // shop name on the public shop page (careerShopPublicView), so an // owner can tell visitors what they stock / their vibe, same idea as // the "bio" field on the driver's own licence profile but scoped to // the shop. Same gating and same block as name/background. if (typeof body.careerShopName === "string" || typeof body.careerShopBackgroundUrl === "string" || typeof body.careerShopBio === "string") { const shopCatalogSnapForProfile = await db.doc("league_meta/careerShop").get(); const shopCatalogForProfile = shopCatalogSnapForProfile.exists ? (shopCatalogSnapForProfile.data()?.items || []) : []; const shopLedgerForProfile = await ensureCareerShopCurrentSeason(userId); const ownsAnyShopForProfile = IFWL_PARTS_SHOP_CATEGORIES.some((cat) => shopCatalogForProfile.some((it) => it.category === cat && shopLedgerForProfile.ownedItemIds.includes(it.id))); if (!ownsAnyShopForProfile) return json(res, 403, { error: "Customizing My Shop requires owning at least one Shop/Factory" }); if (typeof body.careerShopName === "string") updates.careerShopName = body.careerShopName.slice(0, 60); if (typeof body.careerShopBackgroundUrl === "string") updates.careerShopBackgroundUrl = body.careerShopBackgroundUrl.slice(0, 1000); if (typeof body.careerShopBackgroundRef === "string") updates.careerShopBackgroundRef = body.careerShopBackgroundRef.slice(0, 20); if (typeof body.careerShopBio === "string") updates.careerShopBio = body.careerShopBio.slice(0, 300); } // ✅ ADDED: careerWearCarId - which car (by id from the BOP TRACKer's // car catalog, league_meta/bopCarCatalog) the Parts Wear Diagram on // the driver's own Career page should picture. No ownership gate - // unlike My Shop above, every driver sees the wear diagram regardless // of what they own, so anyone can point it at their own car. Empty // string is a valid value (resets to the default car image). if (typeof body.careerWearCarId === "string") { updates.careerWearCarId = body.careerWearCarId.slice(0, 60); } updates.updatedAt = admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(); updates.updatedAtMs = Date.now(); await ref.set(updates, { merge: true }); const fresh = await ref.get(); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, profile: { discordId: userId, ...fresh.data() } }); } // --------------------------- // LICENCE TICKETS: GET (tiered response) // Gold/staff get full incident detail; everyone else gets only a count. // Matching is a substring check against accused/reporter strings using // the driver's linked ACC name(s) - not an exact match, since car // numbers embedded in those strings change between seasons. // --------------------------- if (req.query.tickets === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const profileSnap = await licenceRef(userId).get(); const profile = profileSnap.exists ? profileSnap.data() : null; const aliases = (profile?.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) { return json(res, 200, { ok: true, ticketCount: 0, tickets: [] }); } // accused/reporter have been seen stored as either a plain string or // an array elsewhere in this codebase - handle both so a ticket // never silently drops out of someone's own record just because of // that difference. const toNameArray = (raw) => Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.filter(Boolean).map(String) : (typeof raw === "string" && raw ? [raw] : []); const snap = await db.collection("tickets").limit(2000).get(); const matches = []; for (const doc of snap.docs) { const t = doc.data() || {}; const accused = toNameArray(t.accused); const reporter = toNameArray(t.reporter); const haystack = [...accused, ...reporter].join(" | ").toLowerCase(); if (aliases.some((alias) => haystack.includes(alias))) { // ✅ ADDED: reporter/date/penalty detail and a computed per-ticket // points value, for the tiered "Your Ticket History" display. // Points are derived independently from this ticket's own // finalisation fields (same rule the auto-points trigger uses) // rather than cross-referencing the profile's pointsHistory log, // so this stays correct even in the rare case that trigger's // own name-matching missed this ticket. const finalPenaltyText = String(t.finalPenaltyCode || t.postRaceCode || t.ruleBreach || "").trim(); const isFinalisedTicket = String(t.status || "").trim().toLowerCase() === "finalised"; // ✅ CHANGED: severity-weighted (mild/standard/severe -> 1/2/3), // same rule the licence points system now uses everywhere else - // was previously a flat 1 regardless of severity. const points = (isFinalisedTicket && finalPenaltyText && !IFWL_NO_ACTION_PATTERNS.test(finalPenaltyText)) ? ifwlSeverityPointsFromText(finalPenaltyText) : 0; const accusedHaystack = accused.join(" | ").toLowerCase(); const wasAccused = aliases.some((alias) => accusedHaystack.includes(alias)); matches.push({ id: doc.id, season: t.season || "", track: t.track || "", reporter: t.reporter || "", wasAccused, timestampMs: t.timestamp && t.timestamp.toMillis ? t.timestamp.toMillis() : null, ruleBreach: t.ruleBreach || "", postRaceCode: t.postRaceCode || "", fullIncident: t.fullIncident || "", proofUrl: t.proofUrl || "", videoProofUrl: t.videoProofUrl || "", stewardDecision: t.stewardDecision || "", finalPenalty: t.finalPenalty || "", finalPenaltyTime: t.finalPenaltyTime || "", points, status: t.status || "" }); } } // ✅ CHANGED: full detail (every ticket, every status, regardless of // outcome) is now shown to any driver viewing THEIR OWN record - this // was previously Gold-only, but a driver's own steward history is a // fairness/accountability matter, not a premium feature. Performance // stats (Overall Score/Consistency/Race Risk) remain Gold-only // separately - this change only affects ticket detail. return json(res, 200, { ok: true, ticketCount: matches.length, tickets: matches }); } // --------------------------- // ✅ ADDED: LICENCE POINTS NOTICE - MARK SEEN // Driver's own popup-notice dismissal (band-crossing notices pushed to // licencePointsNotices by ifwlApplyLicencePointsChange). Self-service // only - a driver can only ever touch their own notices, matched by // session identity, never a driverId from the request body. // --------------------------- if (req.query.licencePointsNoticeSeen === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const noticeId = String(body.noticeId || "").trim(); if (!noticeId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing noticeId" }); const ref = licenceRef(userId); await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(ref); if (!snap.exists) return; const notices = Array.isArray(snap.data()?.licencePointsNotices) ? snap.data().licencePointsNotices : []; let changed = false; const updated = notices.map((n) => { if (n && n.id === noticeId && !n.seen) { changed = true; return { ...n, seen: true }; } return n; }); if (changed) tx.set(ref, { licencePointsNotices: updated }, { merge: true }); }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // --------------------------- // TICKET AUDIT (Gold Patreon only): a driver's own ticket's steward // comment trail. Two checks before anything is returned - Gold/staff // access, AND that this ticket actually belongs to the requester // (same accDriverNames match ?tickets=1 uses) - a Gold driver can see // the audit trail on their OWN tickets, not anyone else's by guessing // an ID. Steward identity is masked to the email local-part only, // matching the same masking stewards already see of each other. // --------------------------- if (req.query.ticketAudit === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!isGoldAccess(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Steward comment audit requires Gold Patreon" }); } const ticketId = String(req.query.ticketId || "").trim(); if (!ticketId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing ticketId" }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const profileSnap = await licenceRef(userId).get(); const profile = profileSnap.exists ? profileSnap.data() : null; const aliases = (profile?.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) return json(res, 200, { ok: true, comments: [] }); const ticketSnap = await db.collection("tickets").doc(ticketId).get(); if (!ticketSnap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Ticket not found" }); const t = ticketSnap.data() || {}; const toNameArray2 = (raw) => Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.filter(Boolean).map(String) : (typeof raw === "string" && raw ? [raw] : []); const haystack = [...toNameArray2(t.accused), ...toNameArray2(t.reporter)].join(" | ").toLowerCase(); if (!aliases.some((alias) => haystack.includes(alias))) { return json(res, 403, { error: "This ticket is not on your record" }); } const maskAuthor = (raw) => { const s = String(raw || "").trim(); const at = s.indexOf("@"); return at > 0 ? s.slice(0, at) : (s || "Unknown"); }; const commentsSnap = await db.collection("tickets").doc(ticketId).collection("steward_comments").orderBy("createdAt", "asc").limit(250).get(); const comments = commentsSnap.docs.map((d) => { const c = d.data() || {}; const ts = c.createdAt && c.createdAt.toDate ? c.createdAt.toDate() : null; return { text: String(c.text || "").trim(), authorMasked: maskAuthor(c.authorKey || c.stewardEmail || ""), kind: String(c.kind || "").trim(), whenMs: ts ? ts.getTime() : null }; }).filter((c) => c.text); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, comments }); } // --------------------------- // LEGACY DRIVER ROSTER: fully public, mirrors the old ticketportal's // drivers_v2/drivers collection lookup - covers drivers who show up in // race results/incidents but have never made an IFWL licence profile // (guests, one-off Monday Funday entrants, etc). This is deliberately // separate from ?licenceRoster=1, which only covers linked, licensed // drivers - the ticket accused-driver picker searches both, licensed // drivers first. // --------------------------- if (req.query.legacyDriverRoster === "1") { let rows = []; for (const colName of ["drivers_v2", "drivers"]) { const snap = await db.collection(colName).limit(3000).get(); if (!snap.empty) { rows = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() })); break; } } const drivers = rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, fullName: String(r.fullName || r.discordNameRaw || r.name || r.discordUser || r.id || "").trim(), name: String(r.name || "").trim(), number: String(r.number || "").trim() })).filter((d) => d.fullName); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, drivers }); } // --------------------------- // TICKET TRACK LIST: fully public - the same "tracks" collection the // old ticket portal read/wrote directly from the browser. Distinct, // sorted track names for the ticket form's Track dropdown. // --------------------------- if (req.query.ticketTrackList === "1") { const snap = await db.collection("tracks").limit(500).get(); const seen = new Set(); const tracks = []; snap.docs.forEach((d) => { const name = String(d.data()?.name || "").trim(); if (!name) return; const key = name.toLowerCase(); if (seen.has(key)) return; seen.add(key); tracks.push(name); }); tracks.sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b)); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, tracks }); } // --------------------------- // SUBMIT TICKET: replaces ticketportal.html's direct-from-browser // Firestore writes. The Discord "new ticket" webhook now posts from HERE, server // side - the old portal had the live webhook URL sitting in // plain client-side JS, postable by literally anyone who viewed // page source. It's now only ever in this function's source. // 2. "Reporter" is derived from the driver's own already-linked // licence profile (accDriverNames), not a manually-typed Discord // nickname - they're already authenticated as themselves to load // this page at all, so there's no separate identity check to do, // and using their real ACC name means this ticket correctly // shows up in their own Ticket History later (which matches by // accDriverNames substring, not by Discord nickname). // --------------------------- if (req.query.submitTicket === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const profileSnap = await licenceRef(userId).get(); const profile = profileSnap.exists ? profileSnap.data() : null; const reporter = String((profile?.accDriverNames || [])[0] || v.user?.nick || v.user?.username || "Unknown driver").trim(); const body = req.body || {}; const competition = String(body.competition || "").trim(); const fullIncident = String(body.fullIncident || "").trim().slice(0, 6000); const event = String(body.event || "Practice").trim().slice(0, 60); const proofUrl = String(body.proofUrl || "").trim().slice(0, 500); const season = String(body.season || "Season 3").trim().slice(0, 40); const ruleBreach = String(body.ruleBreach || "ticket submission without assumed breach").trim().slice(0, 200); // ✅ ADDED: Gold Patreon video evidence. `[${accusedNumberManual}] - ${accusedNameManual}` : accusedNameManual; accused = fullNameBuilt; try { const rawId = `${accusedNumberManual}|${accusedNameManual}`.toLowerCase(); const docId = (rawId.replace(/\s+/g, "_").replace(/[^\w\-]/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "").slice(0, 150)) || `manual_${Date.now()}`; await db.collection("drivers_v2").doc(docId).set({ discordUser: "", discordNameRaw: fullNameBuilt, fullName: fullNameBuilt, key: rawId, name: accusedNameManual, number: accusedNumberManual, source: "manual", updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }, { merge: true }); } catch (e) { console.warn("submitTicket: could not save manually-added driver", e.message); } } let track = String(body.track || "").trim().slice(0, 120); const newTrackName = String(body.newTrackName || "").trim().slice(0, 120); if (track === "__ADD_NEW__") { track = newTrackName; if (newTrackName) { try { const key = newTrackName.toLowerCase(); const existing = await db.collection("tracks").where("key", "==", key).limit(1).get(); if (existing.empty) { await db.collection("tracks").add({ name: newTrackName, key }); } } catch (e) { console.warn("submitTicket: could not save new track", e.message); } } } // ✅ ADDED: licence numbers for both parties, where known. Reporter's // is free (already have their profile). Accused's requires a // reverse lookup - only resolves if `accused` is an EXACT // accDriverNames entry, which holds true whenever the driver was // actually picked from the "Licensed IFWL Drivers" results (that // list is built from this same field) - a manually-typed or // legacy/unlicensed accused simply won't match, and is left blank // rather than guessed at. const reporterLicenceNumber = String(profile?.licenceNumber || "").trim(); let accusedLicenceNumber = ""; if (accused && accused !== "__ADD_NEW__") { try { const accusedSnap = await db.collection("licences").where("accDriverNames", "array-contains", accused).limit(1).get(); if (!accusedSnap.empty) accusedLicenceNumber = String(accusedSnap.docs[0].data()?.licenceNumber || "").trim(); } catch (e) { console.warn("submitTicket: accused licence lookup failed", e.message); } } const newTicket = { accused, accusedLicenceNumber, reporter, reporterLicenceNumber, reporterDiscordId: userId, reporterDiscordUsername: String(v.user?.username || ""), reporterDiscordGlobal: String(v.user?.globalName || ""), competition, fullIncident, event, proofUrl, videoProofUrl, season, track, ruleBreach, status: "New Submission", stewardDecision: "", stewardTag: "", timestamp: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp() }; const docRef = await db.collection("tickets").add(newTicket); try { await publishNewTicketToDiscord(docRef.id, newTicket); } catch (e) { console.warn("submitTicket: ticket saved, but Discord publish failed:", e.message); } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, id: docRef.id }); } // --------------------------- // MY PENDING TICKET NOTICE: powers the "you've been reported" pop-up // on login. Only counts tickets that aren't finalised yet, // since a finalised ticket has already been decided. // --------------------------- if (req.query.myPendingTicketNotice === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const profileSnap = await licenceRef(userId).get(); const profile = profileSnap.exists ? profileSnap.data() : null; const aliases = (profile?.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) { return json(res, 200, { ok: true, count: 0, tickets: [] }); } const snap = await db.collection("tickets").limit(2000).get(); const matches = []; for (const doc of snap.docs) { const t = doc.data() || {}; const status = String(t.status || "").toLowerCase(); if (status === "finalised") continue; const rawAccused = t.accused; const accused = Array.isArray(rawAccused) ? rawAccused : (typeof rawAccused === "string" && rawAccused ? x.showcaseTrophies : [], // ✅ ADDED: entrylist ACC name(s) - already public via licenceRoster // and Live Timings itself, just also surfaced on the Directory card // now so drivers can recognise/thank each other by their in-game name. accDriverNames: Array.isArray(x.accDriverNames) ? x.accDriverNames : [], // ✅ ADDED: so Driver Directory cards can show the banned // overlay - a computed boolean only (never the raw ban object, // which can carry a staff-written reason), same "endsAtMs vs // now" formula used everywhere else ban status is checked, so // it never drifts out of sync with a ban that's naturally // lapsed since the driver last visited. banActive: !!(x.ban && Number(x.ban.endsAtMs || 0) > Date.now()) }; }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, drivers }); } // --------------------------- // LICENCE ROSTER: fully public, no token required. // Powers ticketportal.html's "Accused/Reporter" driver picker, which // has no persisted session token to send (its Discord login flow never // stores one). x.careerShopTransactions : []; let careerSeasonEarned = 0, careerSeasonSpent = 0; transactions.forEach((t) => { const amt = Number(t?.amount || 0); if (amt > 0) careerSeasonEarned += amt; else careerSeasonSpent += Math.abs(amt); }); return { discordId: d.id, name: x.name || "", licenceNumber: x.licenceNumber || "", tier: x.tier || "No Licence", tierHistory: Array.isArray(x.tierHistory) ? (GET, Owner-only) - the raw // season_plan/current doc, so the import picker can show every event // (locked or still draft) and which locked ones are already imported. if (req.query.ownerOverviewPlannerDraft === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); try { const [planSnap, importedSnap] = await Promise.all([ db.collection("season_plan").doc("current").get(), ownerEventsCol().get() ]); const plan = planSnap.exists ?
