(GET, Owner-only) - every imported // event (the League Owners Overview's own copy, fully editable here). if (req.query.ownerOverviewEvents === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); try { const snap = await ownerEventsCol().get(); const events = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() })).filter((e) => e.active !== false); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, events }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewEvents failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Could not load imported events: ${e.message}` }); } } // ?ownerOverviewImportPlannerEvents=1&token=... (POST, Owner-only) // body: { eventIds: [planner event id, ...] } // Imports every LOCKED planner event named, skipping anything not // locked and anything already imported (checked again here, not just // trusted from the client, so double-clicking Import can't create // duplicates). visibleTiers starts empty - the Owner sets it in the // amend step, since the planner has no concept of licence tiers. if (req.query.ownerOverviewImportPlannerEvents === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); const requestedIds = Array.isArray(req.body?.eventIds) ? req.body.eventIds.map(String) : []; if (!requestedIds.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "No events selected" }); try { const [planSnap, importedSnap] = await Promise.all([ db.collection("season_plan").doc("current").get(), ownerEventsCol().get() ]); const plan = planSnap.exists ? (planSnap.data() || {}) : {}; const plannerEvents = new Map((plan.events || []).map((e) => [e.id, e])); const alreadyImported = new Set(importedSnap.docs.filter((d) => d.data()?.active !== false).map((d) => d.data()?.importedFromPlannerId).filter(Boolean)); const imported = []; const skipped = []; const nowMs = Date.now(); const batch = db.batch(); for (const id of requestedIds) { const src = plannerEvents.get(id); if (!src) { skipped.push({ id, reason: "Not found in season planner" }); continue; } if (!src.locked) { skipped.push({ id, name: src.name, reason: "Not locked in yet" }); continue; } if (alreadyImported.has(id)) { skipped.push({ id, name: src.name, reason: "Already imported" }); continue; } const ref = ownerEventsCol().doc(); const doc = { name: src.name || "", startDate: src.startDate || "", schedule: (Array.isArray(src.schedule) ? src.schedule : []).map((r) => Object.assign({}, r, { password: "", pushedSimgridEventId: "", pushedAtMs: 0 })), rules: Array.isArray(src.rules) ? src.rules : [], points: Array.isArray(src.points) ? src.points : [], platforms: Array.isArray(src.platforms) ? src.platforms : [], mods: Array.isArray(src.mods) ? src.mods : [], visibleTiers: [], notes: "", active: true, importedFromPlannerId: id, importedAtMs: nowMs, importedBy: v.user?.sub || "", updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: v.user?.sub || "" }; batch.set(ref, doc); imported.push({ id: ref.id, name: doc.name }); } if (imported.length) await batch.commit(); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, imported, skipped }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewImportPlannerEvents failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Import failed: ${e.message}` }); } } // ?ownerOverviewEventResync=1&token=... (POST, Owner-only) // body: { id: owner_events doc id } // Re-pulls name/startDate/schedule/rules/points/platforms/mods from // the planner's CURRENT version of the source event, overwriting only // those fields. visibleTiers/notes/active are never touched here - // an explicit, separate action from ownerOverviewEventSave so a // re-sync can never silently wipe an Owner's licence-visibility tags. if (req.query.ownerOverviewEventResync === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); const id = String(req.body?.id || ""); if (!id) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing event id" }); try { const ref = ownerEventsCol().doc(id); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Imported event not found" }); const existing = snap.data() || {}; const plannerId = existing.importedFromPlannerId; if (!plannerId) return json(res, 400, { error: "This event has no linked planner source to re-sync from" }); const planSnap = await db.collection("season_plan").doc("current").get(); const plan = planSnap.exists ? (planSnap.data() || {}) : {}; const src = (plan.events || []).find((e) => e.id === plannerId); if (!src) return json(res, 404, { error: "The source event no longer exists in the season planner (it may have been removed)" }); const nowMs = Date.now(); // Re-syncing pulls fresh name/date/track/rules/points/etc from the // planner, but a round that's already been pushed to the licence // system carries state the planner knows nothing about (its // password, and the id of the simgrid_events doc it's linked to) // - merge by round id so that state survives, instead of the // planner's plain copy silently overwriting it. Any round that // existed here but is no longer in the planner's schedule (staff // removed it) gets its live licence-system listing unpublished // too, so a removed round can't keep showing to drivers forever. const oldRoundsById = new Map((Array.isArray(existing.schedule) ? existing.schedule : []).map((r) => [r.id, r])); const newSchedule = (Array.isArray(src.schedule) ? src.schedule : []).map((r) => { const old = oldRoundsById.get(r.id); return Object.assign({}, r, { password: old?.password || "", pushedSimgridEventId: old?.pushedSimgridEventId || "", pushedAtMs: old?.pushedAtMs || 0 }); }); const newRoundIds = new Set(newSchedule.map((r) => r.id)); const removedPushedIds = [...oldRoundsById.values()] .filter((r) => r.pushedSimgridEventId && !newRoundIds.has(r.id)) .map((r) => r.pushedSimgridEventId); if (removedPushedIds.length) { const unpubBatch = db.batch(); removedPushedIds.forEach((sgId) => unpubBatch.set(db.collection("simgrid_events").doc(sgId), { active: false, updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true })); await unpubBatch.commit(); } const updates = { name: src.name || "", startDate: src.startDate || "", schedule: newSchedule, rules: Array.isArray(src.rules) ? src.rules : [], points: Array.isArray(src.points) ? src.points : [], platforms: Array.isArray(src.platforms) ? src.platforms : [], mods: Array.isArray(src.mods) ? src.mods : [], updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: v.user?.sub || "" }; await ref.set(updates, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, event: { id, ...existing, ...updates }, unpublishedRoundCount: removedPushedIds.length }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewEventResync failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Re-sync failed: ${e.message}` }); } } // ?ownerOverviewEventSave=1&token=... (POST, Owner-only) // body: { id, name, startDate, schedule, visibleTiers, notes } // Full amend of an imported event's Owner-Overview-managed fields. // rules/points/platforms/mods are deliberately NOT accepted here - // those stay sourced from the planner (view-only in this panel, // refreshed via ownerOverviewEventResync) so there's one canonical // place rules/points/mods get edited, not two that can drift apart. if (req.query.ownerOverviewEventSave === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); const id = String(req.body?.id || ""); if (!id) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing event id" }); try { const ref = ownerEventsCol().doc(id); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Imported event not found" }); const body = req.body || {}; const updates = { updatedAtMs: Date.now(), updatedBy: v.user?.sub || "" }; if (typeof body.name === "string") updates.name = body.name.slice(0, 200); if (typeof body.startDate === "string") updates.startDate = body.startDate; if (typeof body.notes === "string") updates.notes = body.notes.slice(0, 2000); if (Array.isArray(body.visibleTiers)) updates.visibleTiers = body.visibleTiers.map(String).slice(0, 20); if (Array.isArray(body.schedule)) { updates.schedule = body.schedule.slice(0, 60).map((r) => ({ id: String(r?.id || ""), week: String(r?.week || "").slice(0, 60), date: String(r?.date || ""), name: String(r?.name || "").slice(0, 120), dlcName: String(r?.dlcName || "").slice(0, 120), track: String(r?.track || "").slice(0, 120), practiceTime: String(r?.practiceTime || "").slice(0, 40), quallyTime: String(r?.quallyTime || "").slice(0, 40), race1: String(r?.race1 || "").slice(0, 40), race2: String(r?.race2 || "").slice(0, 40), conditions: String(r?.conditions || "").slice(0, 200), misc: String(r?.misc || "").slice(0, 300), password: String(r?.password || "").slice(0, 100), // Preserve push-tracking fields across a normal edit save (this // endpoint never sets these itself - only ownerOverviewPushRounds/ // ownerOverviewUnpushRound do) so editing a name/date/tier here // can never silently disconnect an already-published round from // its live simgrid_events doc. pushedSimgridEventId: r?.pushedSimgridEventId ? String(r.pushedSimgridEventId) : "", pushedAtMs: Number.isFinite(Number(r?.pushedAtMs)) ? Number(r.pushedAtMs) : 0 })); } await ref.set(updates, { merge: true }); const after = await ref.get(); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, event: { id, ...after.data() } }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewEventSave failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Save failed: ${e.message}` }); } } // ?ownerOverviewEventDelete=1&token=... (POST, Owner-only) // body: { id } - soft delete (active:false), same convention as // staffSimgridEventDelete, so it never shows up again but nothing is // destructively removed from Firestore. if (req.query.ownerOverviewEventDelete === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); const id = String(req.body?.id || ""); if (!id) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing event id" }); try { const nowMs = Date.now(); const ref = ownerEventsCol().doc(id); const snap = await ref.get(); // Deleting the imported record shouldn't leave any round it already // pushed to the licence system still live and visible to drivers - // unpublish every pushed round's simgrid_events doc first. const pushedIds = (Array.isArray(snap.data()?.schedule) ? snap.data().schedule : []) .map((r) => r.pushedSimgridEventId).filter(Boolean); if (pushedIds.length) { const unpubBatch = db.batch(); pushedIds.forEach((sgId) => unpubBatch.set(db.collection("simgrid_events").doc(sgId), { active: false, updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true })); await unpubBatch.commit(); } await ref.set({ active: false, updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: v.user?.sub || "" }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, unpublishedRoundCount: pushedIds.length }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewEventDelete failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Delete failed: ${e.message}` }); } } // ?ownerOverviewPushRounds=1&token=... (POST, Owner-only) // body: { id: owner_events doc id, roundIds: [round.id, ...] } // The other half of "import -> amend -> push": publishes selected // rounds of an imported event into simgrid_events, the SAME collection // + fields staffSimgridEventSave writes and myAvailableRaces reads - // so a pushed round shows up for drivers exactly like a manually-added // or SimGrid-sourced one, no separate driver-facing code needed. // Deliberately does NOT reuse staffSimgridEventSave itself: that // action requires a non-empty simgridLink, which a planner-only event // legitimately doesn't have. if (req.query.ownerOverviewPushRounds === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); const id = String(req.body?.id || ""); const roundIds = Array.isArray(req.body?.roundIds) ? req.body.roundIds.map(String) : []; if (!id) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing event id" }); if (!roundIds.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "No rounds selected" }); try { const ref = ownerEventsCol().doc(id); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Imported event not found" }); const ev = snap.data() || {}; const tiers = Array.isArray(ev.visibleTiers) ? ev.visibleTiers : []; if (!tiers.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "Assign at least one licence tier to this event before pushing it to the licence system" }); // simgrid_events.platform is a single "console"|"pc"|"both" enum; // the planner's platforms[] is a free-text list (defaults PC/ // CONSOLE, but staff can rename or add to it) - map by substring // match rather than assuming exact casing/spelling. const platformNames = (Array.isArray(ev.platforms) ? ev.platforms : []).map((p) => String(p.platform || "").toLowerCase()); const hasPc = platformNames.some((p) => p.includes("pc")); const hasConsole = platformNames.some((p) => p.includes("console") || p.includes("xbox") || p.includes("ps4") || p.includes("ps5") || p.includes("playstation")); const platform = hasPc && !hasConsole ? "pc" : hasConsole && !hasPc ? "console" : "both"; const schedule = Array.isArray(ev.schedule) ? ev.schedule.slice() : []; const nowMs = Date.now(); const editor = { userId: v.user?.sub || "", username: v.user?.username || "user", nick: v.user?.nick || "" }; const pushed = []; const skipped = []; const batch = db.batch(); for (const roundId of roundIds) { const idx = schedule.findIndex((r) => r.id === roundId); if (idx === -1) { skipped.push({ roundId, reason: "Round not found" }); continue; } const r = schedule[idx]; const timePart = r.race1 || r.quallyTime || r.practiceTime || ""; let eventDateMs = 0; if (r.date) { const iso = timePart && /^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(timePart) ? `${r.date}T${timePart}:00` : `${r.date}T00:00:00`; const parsed = Date.parse(iso); if (Number.isFinite(parsed)) eventDateMs = parsed; } const titleParts = [ev.name || "(untitled event)", r.week, r.track].filter(Boolean); const title = titleParts.join(" - ").slice(0, 120); const noteParts = [ev.notes, r.conditions, r.misc].filter(Boolean); const notes = noteParts.join(" | ").slice(0, 500); const sgDoc = { title, simgridLink: "", simgridImageUrl: "", platform, tiers, notes, eventDateMs, eventDateLabel: eventDateMs ? "" : (r.week || title), password: String(r.password || ""), active: true, ownerEventId: id, ownerEventRoundId: roundId, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: editor }; let sgRef; if (r.pushedSimgridEventId) { sgRef = db.collection("simgrid_events").doc(r.pushedSimgridEventId); batch.set(sgRef, sgDoc, { merge: true }); } else { sgRef = db.collection("simgrid_events").doc(); batch.set(sgRef, Object.assign({ createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), createdAtMs: nowMs, createdBy: editor }, sgDoc)); } schedule[idx] = Object.assign({}, r, { pushedSimgridEventId: sgRef.id, pushedAtMs: nowMs }); pushed.push({ roundId, simgridEventId: sgRef.id, title, action: r.pushedSimgridEventId ? "updated" : "created" }); } if (pushed.length) { batch.update(ref, { schedule, updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: v.user?.sub || "" }); await batch.commit(); } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, pushed, skipped, event: { id, ...ev, schedule } }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewPushRounds failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Push failed: ${e.message}` }); } } // ?ownerOverviewUnpushRound=1&token=... (POST, Owner-only) // body: { id: owner_events doc id, roundId } // Unpublishes one already-pushed round (soft-deletes its // simgrid_events doc) and clears the round's push-tracking fields, so // a later Push treats it as fresh rather than re-using a now-retired // simgrid_events doc id. if (req.query.ownerOverviewUnpushRound === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "League Owners Overview requires the Owner role" }); const id = String(req.body?.id || ""); const roundId = String(req.body?.roundId || ""); if (!id || !roundId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing event id or round id" }); try { const ref = ownerEventsCol().doc(id); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Imported event not found" }); const ev = snap.data() || {}; const schedule = Array.isArray(ev.schedule) ? ev.schedule.slice() : []; const idx = schedule.findIndex((r) => r.id === roundId); if (idx === -1) return json(res, 404, { error: "Round not found" }); const sgId = schedule[idx].pushedSimgridEventId; const nowMs = Date.now(); if (sgId) await db.collection("simgrid_events").doc(sgId).set({ active: false, updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); schedule[idx] = Object.assign({}, schedule[idx], { pushedSimgridEventId: "", pushedAtMs: 0 }); await ref.set({ schedule, updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: v.user?.sub || "" }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, event: { id, ...ev, schedule } }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ownerOverviewUnpushRound failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: `Unpublish failed: ${e.message}` }); } } // --------------------------- // STAFF LICENCE MANAGEMENT: SAVE (edit any driver's profile / tier override) // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffLicenceSave === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Editing driver licences requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const driverId = String(body.driverId || "").trim(); if (!driverId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing driverId" }); const ref = licenceRef(driverId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Driver not found" }); const p = snap.data(); const updates = {}; if (typeof body.name === "string") updates.name = body.name.slice(0, 80); if (Array.isArray(body.accDriverNames)) { updates.accDriverNames = body.accDriverNames.map((n) => String(n).slice(0, 80)).slice(0, 10); } if (typeof body.platform === "string" && ["", "pc", "console", "both"].includes(body.platform)) { updates.platform = body.platform; } let upgradeInfo = null; if (typeof body.tier === "string" && body.tier !== p.tier) { const tierHistory = Array.isArray(p.tierHistory) ? p.tierHistory : []; tierHistory.push({ tier: body.tier, date: new Date().toISOString(), dateMs: Date.now(), reason: String(body.reason || "No reason given").slice(0, 300), setBy: "staff" }); updates.tier = body.tier; updates.tierHistory = tierHistory; if (await isTierUpgrade(p.tier, body.tier)) { upgradeInfo = { name: updates.name || p.name || "Unnamed driver", fromTier: p.tier || "No Licence", toTier: body.tier }; } } updates.updatedAt = admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(); updates.updatedAtMs = Date.now(); await ref.set(updates, { merge: true }); if (upgradeInfo) { publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord( licenceBotWebhook, `⬆️ **IFWL Licence Upgrade**\n**Driver:** ${discordSafe(upgradeInfo.name)}\n**${discordSafe(upgradeInfo.fromTier)}** → **${discordSafe(upgradeInfo.toTier)}**` ).catch((e) => logger.warn("Licence upgrade Discord publish failed", e)); } return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // --------------------------- // STAFF LICENCE MANAGEMENT: ADJUST LICENCE POINTS // Points live on the same licences/{discordId} doc as everything else - // that doc IS the driver's unique licence record (licenceNumber lives // right there too), so points are inherently tied to their licence // number without needing a second lookup collection. delta can be // positive (points added, e.g. for an incident) or negative (points // removed/expired). Uses a transaction so two staff acting on the same // driver at once can't silently overwrite each other's change. // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffLicencePoints === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Adjusting licence points requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const driverId = String(body.driverId || "").trim(); const delta = Math.round(Number(body.delta || 0)); const reason = String(body.reason || "").slice(0, 300); if (!driverId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing driverId" }); if (!Number.isFinite(delta) || delta === 0) return json(res, 400, { error: "Points delta must be a non-zero number" }); if (!reason) return json(res, 400, { error: "A reason is required for any points change" }); const ref = licenceRef(driverId); const staffName = v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff"; // ✅ CHANGED: routed through the shared ifwlApplyLicencePointsChange // core (season-boundary reset + band-crossing notify) so a manual // staff adjustment behaves consistently with the automatic // ticket-finalisation points below - no Beginner leeway here though, // that only ever applies to the automatic path. let result; try { result = await ifwlApplyLicencePointsChange(ref, { rawDelta: delta, reason: `Staff: ${reason}`, setBy: staffName, allowBeginnerLeeway: false }); } catch (e) { return json(res, 404, { error: e.message || "Driver not found" }); } await ifwlNotifyLicencePointsBandCrossing(result, licenceBotWebhook); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, licencePoints: result.after }); } // --------------------------- // ✅ ADDED: STAFF ALERTS FEED // Band-crossing events (licence points AMBER / FINAL WARNING / // ESCALATE) land here so staff have one feed instead of relying on // catching each Discord post individually - same Ops+ gate as every // other licence-points action. // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffAlerts === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Viewing staff alerts requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); } const snap = await db.collection("staffAlerts").orderBy("createdAtMs", "desc").limit(200).get(); const alerts = snap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() })); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, alerts }); } if (req.query.staffAlertAck === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Managing staff alerts requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const alertId = String(body.alertId || "").trim(); if (!alertId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing alertId" }); await db.collection("staffAlerts").doc(alertId).set({ acknowledged: true, acknowledgedBy: v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff", acknowledgedAtMs: Date.now() }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // --------------------------- // ✅ ADDED: LICENCE POINTS - ONE-TIME BACKFILL // Staff Alerts (and the driver popup/Discord posts) are otherwise // forward-only - they only ever fire on a NEW points change made // under this system, so drivers who already carried points before // this went live never had a "crossing" event logged, even though // their current licencePoints total is real. This treats every // driver's CURRENT total as if it just landed in that band today and // fires the exact same driver-notice/staff-alert/Discord pipeline a // live crossing would. Idempotent per driver via // licencePointsBackfilledAtMs - safe to run more than once, already- // processed drivers are skipped rather than re-notified. ESCALATE // (7+) still only ever raises a staff alert here too, same as a live // crossing - never Discord or a driver-facing notice, so this can't // jump ahead of a manual Head Steward/Owner decision either. Owner // only, since this fires real Discord posts and driver notices. // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffLicencePointsBackfill === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Backfilling licence points alerts requires Owner" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; if (String(body.confirm || "") !== "BACKFILL") { return json(res, 400, { error: 'Pass confirm: "BACKFILL" to run this - it notifies every driver currently on 3+ licence points and posts to Discord for AMBER/FINAL WARNING as if it just happened right now. Safe to run more than once - already-backfilled drivers are skipped.' }); } const snap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); const nowMs = Date.now(); let processed = 0; for (const doc of snap.docs) { const p = doc.data() || {}; if (p.licencePointsBackfilledAtMs) continue; // already run for this driver const points = Number(p.licencePoints || 0); const band = ifwlLicencePointsBand(points); if (band === "none") continue; // nothing to backfill, no write needed const noticeText = ifwlLicencePointsNoticeText(band, points); const notices = Array.isArray(p.licencePointsNotices) ? p.licencePointsNotices.slice(-19) : []; notices.push({ id: `lpn_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, text: noticeText, band, points, dateMs: nowMs, seen: false }); await doc.ref.set({ licencePointsNotices: notices, licencePointsBackfilledAtMs: nowMs, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); await ifwlNotifyLicencePointsBandCrossing({ bandCrossed: true, newBand: band, driverId: doc.id, driverName: p.name || "Unnamed driver", after: points }, licenceBotWebhook); processed++; } logger.info(`staffLicencePointsBackfill: processed ${processed} driver(s) by ${v.user?.username || v.user?.sub}`); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, processed }); } // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: AC RALLY KNOCKOUT EVENTS - staff endpoints // See the big module-level comment above ifwlAcRallyIsPowerOfTwoAtLeast4 // for the overall design. Owner only, since this can't be // undone from here. if (req.query.staffRlLockIn === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const v = verifySession(String(req.query.token || "") || (String(req.get("Authorization") || "").startsWith("Bearer ") ? req.get("Authorization").slice(7) : ""), secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "Locking in an RL 3v3 event requires Owner" }); const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const eventId = String(body.eventId || "").trim(); if (!eventId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing eventId" }); const ref = db.collection("rl_events").doc(eventId); const result = await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(ref); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Event not found"); const ev = snap.data(); if (ev.status !== "signup") throw new Error("Event is not open for signup - it may already be locked"); const entrants = Array.isArray(ev.entrants) ? ev.entrants : []; if (!ifwlRlIsValidEntrantCount(entrants.length)) { throw new Error(`Entrant count must divide evenly into squads of 3, with a power-of-two squad count (6, 12, 24, 48, ...) - currently ${entrants.length}. req.body : {}; const eventId = String(body.eventId || "").trim(); const driverId = String(body.driverId || "").trim(); const toSquadId = String(body.toSquadId || "").trim(); if (!eventId || !driverId || !toSquadId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing eventId, driverId or toSquadId" }); const ref = db.collection("rl_events").doc(eventId); await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(ref); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Event not found"); const ev = snap.data(); if (ev.status !== "in_progress") throw new Error("Squads can only be adjusted once the event is locked in"); const round0 = (ev.rounds || [])[0]; if (!round0 || (round0.matches || []).some((m) => m.status === "completed")) { throw new Error("Squads can no longer be moved - a match has already been decided"); } const squads = (ev.squads || []).map((s) => ({ ...s, drivers: [...(s.drivers || [])] })); const fromSquad = squads.find((s) => (s.drivers || []).some((d) => d.driverId === driverId)); const toSquad = squads.find((s) => s.id === toSquadId); if (!fromSquad) throw new Error("Driver not found in any squad"); if (!toSquad) throw new Error("Target squad not found"); if (fromSquad.id === toSquad.id) throw new Error("Driver is already on that squad"); if ((toSquad.drivers || []).length >= IFWL_RL_SQUAD_SIZE) throw new Error("Target squad is already full"); const driverEntry = fromSquad.drivers.find((d) => d.driverId === driverId); fromSquad.drivers = fromSquad.drivers.filter((d) => d.driverId !== driverId); toSquad.drivers = [...toSquad.drivers, driverEntry]; tx.set(ref, { squads, updatedAtMs: Date.now() }, { merge: true }); }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // Edits one round's best-of length and/or scheduled date/time - // allowed any time (including ahead of the round opening, so staff // can plan ahead), same as AC Rally's round config. Changing bestOf // also updates every not-yet-completed match already in that round. if (req.query.staffRlRoundConfig === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const v = verifySession(String(req.query.token || "") || (String(req.get("Authorization") || "").startsWith("Bearer ") ? req.get("Authorization").slice(7) : ""), secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "Configuring rounds requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const eventId = String(body.eventId || "").trim(); const roundIndex = Number(body.roundIndex); if (!eventId || !Number.isInteger(roundIndex)) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing eventId or roundIndex" }); const hasScheduleField = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, "scheduledAtMs"); const scheduledAtMs = body.scheduledAtMs === null || body.scheduledAtMs === "" ? null : Number(body.scheduledAtMs); const bestOf = Number.isInteger(Number(body.bestOf)) && Number(body.bestOf) > 0 ? "verify" : body.decision === "refuse" ? "refuse" : null; if (!eventId || !Number.isInteger(roundIndex) || !Number.isInteger(matchIndex) || !submissionId || !decision) { return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing eventId, roundIndex, matchIndex, submissionId or a valid decision" }); } const refusalReason = String(body.refusalReason || "").trim().slice(0, 300); if (decision === "refuse" && !refusalReason) return json(res, 400, { error: "A reason is required to refuse a submission" }); const ref = db.collection("rl_events").doc(eventId); const staffName = v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff"; let imagePathToDelete = null; await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(ref); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Event not found"); const ev = snap.data(); const rounds = [...(ev.rounds || [])]; const round = rounds[roundIndex]; if (!round) throw new Error("Round not found"); const matches = [...(round.matches || [])]; const match = matches[matchIndex]; if (!match) throw new Error("Match not found"); if (match.status === "completed") throw new Error("This match has already been decided"); const submissions = [...(match.submissions || [])]; const subIdx = submissions.findIndex((s) => s.id === submissionId); if (subIdx === -1) throw new Error("Submission not found"); const sub = submissions[subIdx]; if (sub.status !== "pending") throw new Error("This submission has already been reviewed"); imagePathToDelete = sub.imagePath || null; const nowMs = Date.now(); if (decision === "verify") { submissions[subIdx] = { ...sub, status: "verified", reviewedAtMs: nowMs, reviewedBy: staffName }; matches[matchIndex] = { ...match, submissions, finalScoreA: sub.scoreA, finalScoreB: sub.scoreB, winnerSquadId: sub.scoreA > sub.scoreB ? No screenshot involved. Owner only. if (req.query.staffRlManualMatchOverride === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const v = verifySession(String(req.query.token || "") || (String(req.get("Authorization") || "").startsWith("Bearer ") ? req.get("Authorization").slice(7) : ""), secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "Manually overriding a match score requires Owner" }); const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const eventId = String(body.eventId || "").trim(); const roundIndex = Number(body.roundIndex); const matchIndex = Number(body.matchIndex); const scoreA = Number(body.scoreA); const scoreB = Number(body.scoreB); if (!eventId || !Number.isInteger(roundIndex) || !Number.isInteger(matchIndex) || !Number.isInteger(scoreA) || !Number.isInteger(scoreB) || scoreA < 0 || scoreB < 0 || scoreA === scoreB) { return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing or invalid eventId, roundIndex, matchIndex, scoreA or scoreB (a series can't end in a tie)" }); } const ref = db.collection("rl_events").doc(eventId); await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(ref); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Event not found"); const ev = snap.data(); const rounds = [...(ev.rounds || [])]; const round = rounds[roundIndex]; if (!round) throw new Error("Round not found"); const matches = [...(round.matches || [])]; const match = matches[matchIndex]; if (!match) throw new Error("Match not found"); matches[matchIndex] = { ...match, finalScoreA: scoreA, finalScoreB: scoreB, winnerSquadId: scoreA > scoreB ? match.squadAId : match.squadBId, status: "completed", completedAtMs: Date.now() }; round.matches = matches; rounds[roundIndex] = round; tx.set(ref, { rounds, updatedAtMs: Date.now() }, { merge: true }); }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // Requires every match in the round to be decided first. Pairs the // winners (in bracket order) into the next round's matches, or - if // this round only had one match (the Final) - crowns the champion // squad and completes the event. Manual, same as AC Rally: staff // decides when a round is "done". if (req.query.staffRlAdvanceRound === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const v = verifySession(String(req.query.token || "") || (String(req.get("Authorization") || "").startsWith("Bearer ") ? req.get("Authorization").slice(7) : ""), secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) return json(res, 403, { error: "Advancing a round requires Owner" }); const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const eventId = String(body.eventId || "").trim(); const roundIndex = Number(body.roundIndex); if (!eventId || !Number.isInteger(roundIndex)) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing eventId or roundIndex" }); const ref = db.collection("rl_events").doc(eventId); const result = await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(ref); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Event not found"); const ev = snap.data(); const rounds = [...(ev.rounds || [])]; const round = rounds[roundIndex]; if (!round) throw new Error("Round not found"); if (round.status !== "open") throw new Error("This round is not currently open"); const matches = round.matches || []; if (!matches.length || matches.some((m) => m.status !== "completed")) { throw new Error("Every match in this round must be decided before advancing"); } const winners = matches.map((m) => m.winnerSquadId); const isFinal = matches.length === 1; const nowMs = Date.now(); rounds[roundIndex] = { ...round, status: "completed", advancedAtMs: nowMs }; const squadsById = Object.fromEntries((ev.squads || []).map((s) => [s.id, s])); let championSquadId = ev.championSquadId || null; let championSquadName = ev.championSquadName || null; let championAtMs = ev.championAtMs || null; let newCurrentRoundIndex = ev.currentRoundIndex; let eventStatus = ev.status; if (isFinal) { championSquadId = winners[0] || null; championSquadName = championSquadId ? (squadsById[championSquadId]?.name || "") : null; championAtMs = nowMs; eventStatus = "completed"; } else { const nextRound = { ...rounds[roundIndex + 1] }; nextRound.matches = ifwlRlPairSquadsIntoMatches(winners, nextRound.bestOf); nextRound.status = "open"; rounds[roundIndex + 1] = nextRound; newCurrentRoundIndex = roundIndex + 1; } tx.set(ref, { rounds, currentRoundIndex: newCurrentRoundIndex, status: eventStatus, championSquadId, championSquadName, championAtMs, updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); return { advancedSquadNames: winners.map((id) => squadsById[id]?.name || ""), champion: isFinal ? championSquadName : null }; }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, ...result }); } // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: RL 3v3 KNOCKOUT EVENTS - driver-facing endpoints // ======================================================= // Public/driver board - every non-cancelled event, with per-driver // detail limited to what's safe to share: full round/match config // and every DECIDED match's score (that's the whole point of a // public bracket), but never another squad's pending/refused // submission or proof image - only the requester's own squad's show // up, under mySubmissions on each match. if (req.query.rlBoard === "1") { const v = verifySession(String(req.query.token || "") || (String(req.get("Authorization") || "").startsWith("Bearer ") ? "Banned" is always computed from ban.endsAtMs vs now on read - // never a separate status flag that could drift out of sync if a ban // naturally lapses without anyone visiting the page to update it. // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffLicenceBan === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Banning drivers requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const driverId = String(body.driverId || "").trim(); if (!driverId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing driverId" }); const ref = licenceRef(driverId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Driver not found" }); const p = snap.data(); const nowMs = Date.now(); const banHistory = Array.isArray(p.banHistory) ? p.banHistory : []; const staffName = v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff"; if (body.lift === true) { banHistory.push({ action: "lifted", date: new Date().toISOString(), dateMs: nowMs, by: staffName }); await ref.set({ ban: null, banHistory, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, lifted: true }); } const permanent = body.permanent === true; const days = permanent ? null : Math.round(Number(body.days || 0)); if (!permanent && (!Number.isFinite(days) || days < 1 || days > 100)) { return json(res, 400, { error: "Ban length must be between 1 and 100 days, or set permanent" }); } const reason = String(body.reason || "").slice(0, 500); // A permanent ban just uses an endsAtMs 100 years out - the ban is // still stored/lifted through the exact same mechanism as a timed // one, so there's no separate "permanent" code path anywhere else // that could fall out of sync with it. const endsAtMs = permanent ? (nowMs + 100 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : (nowMs + days * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const ban = { days: permanent ? null : days, permanent, reason, startedAtMs: nowMs, endsAtMs, setBy: staffName }; banHistory.push({ action: permanent ? "banned (permanent)" : "banned", days: permanent ? null : days, permanent, reason, date: new Date().toISOString(), dateMs: nowMs, by: staffName }); await ref.set({ ban, banHistory, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, ban }); } // --------------------------- // STAFF LICENCE MANAGEMENT: REMOVE PROFILE/HEADER IMAGE // For content that shouldn't be up at all (illegal imagery, slurs, // anything else that's a ToS breach) - clears the image immediately, // logs a permanent staffNotes entry recording who removed it and why // (never silently vanishes with no record), and can optionally ban // the driver in the exact same action rather than needing a second // trip to the ban tool. // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffRemoveProfileImage === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Removing profile images requires Owner or Ops Manager" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const driverId = String(body.driverId || "").trim(); const which = body.which === "header" ? "header" : "photo"; const reason = String(body.reason || "").trim().slice(0, 500); if (!driverId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing driverId" }); if (!reason) return json(res, 400, { error: "A reason is required to remove a driver's image" }); const ref = licenceRef(driverId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Driver not found" }); const nowMs = Date.now(); const staffName = v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff"; const updates = which === "header" ? { headerUploadUrl: "", headerUploadRef: "" } : { photoUploadUrl: "", photoUploadRef: "" }; if (which === "photo" && (snap.data() || {}).avatarMode === "upload") updates.avatarMode = "discord"; updates.updatedAt = admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(); updates.updatedAtMs = nowMs; await ref.set(updates, { merge: true }); await ref.collection("staffNotes").add({ text: `Removed ${which === "header" ? "header background" : "profile photo"} image. Reason: ${reason}`, by: staffName, byUserId: v.user?.sub || "", createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), createdAtMs: nowMs }); let banResult = null; if (body.banAlso === true) { const banPermanent = body.banPermanent === true; const banDays = banPermanent ? null : Math.round(Number(body.banDays || 0)); if (!banPermanent && (!Number.isFinite(banDays) || banDays < 1 || banDays > 100)) { return json(res, 400, { error: "Image removed and noted, but ban length must be between 1 and 100 days, or set permanent - ban was not applied" }); } const p = snap.data(); const banHistory = Array.isArray(p.banHistory) ? p.banHistory : []; const endsAtMs = banPermanent ? (nowMs + 100 * 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) : (nowMs + banDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const banReason = `ToS breach (${which === "header" ? "header" : "profile"} image): ${reason}`; const ban = { days: banPermanent ? null : banDays, permanent: banPermanent, reason: banReason, startedAtMs: nowMs, endsAtMs, setBy: staffName }; banHistory.push({ action: banPermanent ? "banned (permanent)" : "banned", days: banPermanent ? null : banDays, permanent: banPermanent, reason: banReason, date: new Date().toISOString(), dateMs: nowMs, by: staffName }); await ref.set({ ban, banHistory }, { merge: true }); banResult = ban; } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, driverId, which, banResult }); } // --------------------------- // SIMGRID EVENTS: STAFF SAVE / CREATE // Pass eventId to update an existing event; omit it to create a new one. // Reuses fetchSimgridOgImage (already defined above for Race Team Events) // so these cards get the same preview image treatment. // --------------------------- // --------------------------- // ✅ ADDED: SIMGRID ROUNDS PREVIEW // Best-effort - see fetchSimgridRounds above for exactly what this // does and doesn't guarantee. Preview only, never creates or changes // any event on its own - staff still decide what to do with what // comes back. // --------------------------- if (req.query.staffSimgridFetchRounds === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? Takes a list of eligible discordIds // (the frontend already knows who raced this season and who doesn't // already have a deal, via the same data Season Progression's staff // view uses) - the actual dice roll and write happen here, server-side, // so the outcome can't be influenced client-side. if (req.query.staffRunSponsorshipLottery === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Running the sponsorship lottery requires Owner only" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const eligibleIds = Array.isArray(body.eligibleDriverIds) ? Each run is fully // independent - a driver can win on more than one run, and each // run re-reads the driver's live balance/owned-items/existing-deals // fresh, so run 2 correctly sees whatever run 1 just wrote. const runs = Math.max(1, Math.min(50, Math.round(Number(body.runs) || 1))); const poolSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/sponsorPool").get(); const sponsors = poolSnap.exists ? (poolSnap.data()?.sponsors || []) : []; if (!sponsors.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "Add at least one sponsor to the pool first" }); // ✅ FIXED: Support Staff Shop items claimed "+X% sponsorship revenue" // but nothing here ever read them - and separately, a sponsorship win // was ONLY ever written to the display-only "sponsorships" array, // never actually credited to the spendable IFWL Shop balance at all. // Both are fixed together here: Support Staff bonus now stacks with // the Performance Sponsorship Network perk, and the final amount is // credited straight into the driver's Shop ledger as a normal // transaction, the same way a Season Start Payment is. const shopCatalogSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/careerShop").get(); const shopCatalog = shopCatalogSnap.exists ? (shopCatalogSnap.data()?.items || []) : []; const currentSeasonKey = await careerShopSeasonKeyNow(); // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing / Sponsorship Contracts - mirrors the // equivalent block in ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery (kept in sync // manually, same as the rest of this handler already is with that // one). Fetched once for the whole call (every run below shares it - // contracts don't change mid-call), backer writes deferred to one // pass after every run finishes so a backer with several winning // drivers across multiple runs only gets read/written once. const backingContractsSnapStaff = await db.collection("driverBackingContracts").limit(3000).get(); const activeContractsByDriverStaff = new Map(); backingContractsSnapStaff.docs.forEach((d) => { const c = { id: d.id, ...d.data() }; if (c.status !== "active") return; const list = activeContractsByDriverStaff.get(c.driverId) || []; list.push(c); activeContractsByDriverStaff.set(c.driverId, list); }); const backingBackerDeltasStaff = new Map(); // backerId -> { delta, txEntries: [] } const runResults = []; const allWinners = []; for (let runNumber = 1; runNumber <= runs; runNumber++) { const winners = []; const nowMs = Date.now(); for (const driverId of eligibleIds) { if (Math.random() >= 0.05) continue; // 5% chance per eligible driver const ref = licenceRef(driverId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) continue; const p = snap.data() || {}; // ✅ CHANGED: drivers can now stack multiple simultaneous // sponsorships - no longer skipped just for already holding one. // The eligible pool used to shrink every roll as more drivers // landed a deal and got excluded from every future roll; each // deal now gets its own id and is appended rather than // overwriting the old single-deal field, capped at 50 per driver // purely as a technical safety limit against unbounded growth. const existingSponsorships = Array.isArray(p.sponsorships) ? p.sponsorships : (p.activeSponsorship ? [p.activeSponsorship] : []); if (existingSponsorships.length >= 50) continue; const sponsor = sponsors[Math.floor(Math.random() * sponsors.length)]; let amount = Math.round(sponsor.minAmount + Math.random() * (sponsor.maxAmount - sponsor.minAmount)); // ✅ ADDED: Performance Sponsorship Network perk - +8%, only if the // driver holds this perk AT THE MOMENT of this specific roll. // Picking the perk later never retroactively boosts a deal // already won before they had it - this is baked in once, here, // not recalculated on every page load afterward. const perks = Array.isArray(p.careerPerks) ? p.careerPerks : []; const hadSponsorshipPerk = perks.includes("sponsorshipNetwork"); if (hadSponsorshipPerk) amount = Math.round(amount * 1.08); // ✅ ADDED: Support Staff Shop items, same "baked in at roll time" // rule as the perk above - only items owned THIS season count, and // only as of this exact roll. const isCurrentSeason = p.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKey; const ownedItemIds = isCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(p.careerShopOwnedItemIds) ? p.careerShopOwnedItemIds : []; const supportStaffBonusPct = shopCatalog .filter((it) => it.category === "supportStaff" && ownedItemIds.includes(it.id)) .reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.bonusPct) || 0), 0); if (supportStaffBonusPct > 0) amount = Math.round(amount * (1 + supportStaffBonusPct / 100)); const deal = { id: `sponsor_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, companyName: sponsor.companyName, dealDescription: sponsor.dealDescription, amount, boostedBySponsorshipPerk: hadSponsorshipPerk, boostedBySupportStaffPct: supportStaffBonusPct, awardedAtMs: nowMs, acknowledged: false }; const priorBalance = isCurrentSeason ? (Number(p.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; const priorTransactions = isCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(p.careerShopTransactions) ? p.careerShopTransactions : []; const ledgerTx = { id: careerShopTxId(), type: "sponsorship", amount, description: `Sponsorship deal - ${sponsor.companyName}`, reason: `Sponsorship deal - ${sponsor.companyName}`, isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }; // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing - see the matching block in // ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery for the full rationale. Always a // straight cut (never a "cover a loss" case) since a lottery win // is never negative. const driverActiveContractsStaff = activeContractsByDriverStaff.get(driverId) || []; const backingTxsStaff = []; let netAmountAfterBackingStaff = amount; for (const contract of driverActiveContractsStaff) { const cutAmount = Math.round(amount * ((Number(contract.pctBack) || 0) / 100)); if (!cutAmount) continue; netAmountAfterBackingStaff -= cutAmount; backingTxsStaff.push({ id: careerShopTxId(), type: "backingSponsorshipCut", amount: -cutAmount, contractId: contract.id, counterpartId: contract.backerId, counterpartName: contract.backerName || "a sponsor", description: `Driver Backing: paid ${contract.backerName || "a sponsor"} ${cutAmount} (${contract.pctBack}% of your ${sponsor.companyName} deal)`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - sponsorship deal cut", isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }); const backerEntryStaff = backingBackerDeltasStaff.get(contract.backerId) || { delta: 0, txEntries: [] }; backerEntryStaff.delta += cutAmount; backerEntryStaff.txEntries.push({ id: careerShopTxId(), type: "backingSponsorshipCut", amount: cutAmount, contractId: contract.id, counterpartId: driverId, counterpartName: p.name || "a driver", description: `Driver Backing: received ${cutAmount} from ${p.name || "a driver"}'s ${sponsor.companyName} deal (${contract.pctBack}%)`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - sponsorship deal cut", isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }); backingBackerDeltasStaff.set(contract.backerId, backerEntryStaff); } await ref.set({ sponsorships: admin.firestore.FieldValue.arrayUnion(deal), activeSponsorship: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(), // retire the old single-deal field once a driver has moved onto the array careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKey, careerShopBalance: priorBalance + netAmountAfterBackingStaff, careerShopOwnedItemIds: ownedItemIds, careerShopTransactions: [ledgerTx, ...backingTxsStaff, ...priorTransactions].slice(0, 200), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); winners.push({ driverId, driverName: p.name || "Unnamed driver", ...deal }); } runResults.push({ runNumber, rolledCount: eligibleIds.length, winnerCount: winners.length, winners }); allWinners.push(...winners); } // end runs loop // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing - second pass, once per backer touched // across every run in this whole call. for (const [backerIdStaff, entryStaff] of backingBackerDeltasStaff.entries()) { if (!entryStaff.delta && !entryStaff.txEntries.length) continue; } catch (e) { logger.warn(`staffRunSponsorshipLottery: could not settle Driver Backing backer ${backerIdStaff}`, e); } } // ✅ ADDED: match the scheduled auto-roll's Discord announcement - // staff running the lottery by hand should get the same "who won" // post the automatic daily roll does, not silence. if (allWinners.length) { try { const shown = allWinners.slice(0, 25); const remainder = allWinners.length - shown.length; const lines = shown.map((w) => `**${w.driverName}** - ${w.companyName} (£${w.amount.toLocaleString()})`).join("\n"); const more = remainder > 0 ? `\n...and ${remainder} more` : ""; await publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord(licenceBotWebhook, `🤝 **Sponsorship Lottery Results** - ${allWinners.length} driver(s) landed a deal today:\n${lines}${more}`); } catch (e) { logger.warn("staffRunSponsorshipLottery: Discord notification failed", e); } } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, runs, rolledCount: eligibleIds.length, winnerCount: allWinners.length, winners: allWinners, runResults }); } // "Who's got the lucky number" - every driver currently holding an // active sponsorship deal. if (req.query.staffCurrentSponsorships === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); [p.activeSponsorship] : []); list.forEach((s) => sponsorships.push({ driverId: d.id, driverName: p.name || "Unnamed driver", ...s })); }); sponsorships.sort((a, b) => (b.awardedAtMs || 0) - (a.awardedAtMs || 0)); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, sponsorships }); } if (req.query.staffClearSponsorship === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? per-tyre. partsMaintenance: { engine: 8000, suspension: 6000, damper: 4000, aero: 5000 }, // ✅ ADDED: Race Fuel - a new Fuel Depot Shop/Factory, priced and // consumed like Tyres (flat per-litre RRP, body.tracks.map((t) => { const slug = String(t.slug || "").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, "_").slice(0, 60); const name = String(t.name || "").trim().slice(0, 80); const kmPerLap = Math.max(0, Math.round((Number(t.kmPerLap) || 0) * 1000) / 1000); return { slug, name, kmPerLap }; }).filter((t) => { if (!t.slug || !t.name || !t.kmPerLap) return false; if (seenSlugs.has(t.slug)) return false; // drop duplicate slugs rather than let one silently overwrite another driver's mileage lookups seenSlugs.add(t.slug); return true; }).slice(0, 200) : []; if (!tracks.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "At least one track with a name and km/lap is required" }); // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace maintenance costs, staff-editable // exactly like entryFeePerRace/tyre.pricePerTyre above. const partsMaintenance = (body.partsMaintenance && typeof body.partsMaintenance === "object") ? v.user.roles : []; if (canManageLicencesOpsOnly(roles)) return true; if (String(v.user?.sub || "") !== team.ownerId) return false; if (team.donationVerified) return true; return roles.includes(ROLE_PATREON_GOLD_ID); } async function applyTeamCacheToMember(driverId, team) { await licenceRef(driverId).set({ teamId: team.id, teamName: team.teamName || "", teamLogoUrl: team.logoUrl || "" }, { merge: true }); } async function clearTeamCacheFromMember(driverId) { await licenceRef(driverId).set({ teamId: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(), teamName: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(), teamLogoUrl: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete() }, { merge: true }); } if (req.query.applyRaceTeam === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? The // first ledger read/write for a driver after that key changes wipes // their balance/owned-items back to zero before doing anything else - // no cron job needed, and old transactions are superseded, not // deleted, in case they're ever needed for a dispute. // // Deliberately a PURE transaction ledger - it does NOT automatically // credit race or sponsorship earnings. Sponsorship deals already live // server-side (per driver, in `sponsorships`) and could be wired in // later; race earnings are currently computed entirely client-side in // Season Progression and have no server-side equivalent to draw from // without duplicating that whole calculation here. For now, every // credit into a driver's balance is a manual staff transaction. // ======================================================= async function careerShopSeasonKeyNow() { const snap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonWindow").get(); const data = snap.exists ? snap.data() : {}; return String(data?.startDate || "no-season-set"); } // Returns { balance, ownedItemIds, transactions } for a driver, // resetting them first if their stored season key doesn't match the // current one. Always safe to call before any read or mutation. async function ensureCareerShopCurrentSeason(driverId) { const ref = licenceRef(driverId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Driver not found"); const p = snap.data() || {}; const currentKey = await careerShopSeasonKeyNow(); // ✅ ADDED: ownedLiveryIds - a PERMANENT record of which League // Liveries a driver has bought, deliberately read here but never // written by the season-reset block below. A livery is a one-off // paid digital download, not a season-scoped Shop/Factory // ownership - it must survive every season reset or a driver would // need to re-buy the same zip file every single season. const ownedLiveryIds = Array.isArray(p.ownedLiveryIds) ? p.ownedLiveryIds : []; if (p.careerShopSeasonKey === currentKey) { return { ref, balance: Number(p.careerShopBalance) || 0, ownedItemIds: Array.isArray(p.careerShopOwnedItemIds) ? p.careerShopOwnedItemIds : [], transactions: Array.isArray(p.careerShopTransactions) ? p.careerShopTransactions : [], // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace inventory - same ledger, same lazy // season-key reset as balance/owned items above. tyreInventoryCount: Number(p.tyreInventoryCount) || 0, partsInventory: Array.isArray(p.partsInventory) ? p.partsInventory : [], // ✅ ADDED: Race Fuel inventory - litres, same lazy reset as // tyreInventoryCount right above it. fuelInventoryLitres: Number(p.fuelInventoryLitres) || 0, ownedLiveryIds }; } // Stale (or first-ever) season - reset now. Parts/tyre/fuel // inventory resets fully too - "full reset on season ending" // applies to the whole Shop ledger, not just balance/owned items. // ownedLiveryIds is NOT part of this reset object - merge:true // below leaves it completely untouched, exactly as intended. const reset = { careerShopSeasonKey: currentKey, careerShopBalance: 0, careerShopOwnedItemIds: [], careerShopTransactions: [], tyreInventoryCount: 0, partsInventory: [], fuelInventoryLitres: 0 }; await ref.set(reset, { merge: true }); return { ref, balance: 0, ownedItemIds: [], transactions: [], tyreInventoryCount: 0, partsInventory: [], fuelInventoryLitres: 0, ownedLiveryIds }; } function careerShopTxId() { return `cstx_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`; } // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: SEASON START PAYMENTS // A one-off Day 1 balance, sized by Patreon tier, paid into the same // IFWL Shop ledger every driver already has (careerShopBalance). // Tracked per-driver via seasonStartPaymentSeasonKey on their own // licence doc, using the SAME season key careerShopSeasonKeyNow() // already computes from league_meta/seasonWindow - so this naturally // resets and becomes payable again whenever a new season starts, // with no separate season concept to keep in sync. Re-running the // payment is always safe: anyone who already has this season's key // is skipped, never paid twice. // ======================================================= const DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS = { gold: 120000, silver: 85000, bronze: 60000, none: 30000, enabled: false }; if (req.query.seasonStartPayments === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const snap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonStartPayments").get(); const data = snap.exists ? snap.data() : {}; return json(res, 200, { ok: true, gold: Number.isFinite(Number(data.gold)) ? Number(data.gold) : DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.gold, silver: Number.isFinite(Number(data.silver)) ? Number(data.silver) : DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.silver, bronze: Number.isFinite(Number(data.bronze)) ? Number(data.bronze) : DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.bronze, none: Number.isFinite(Number(data.none)) ? Number(data.none) : DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.none, enabled: data.enabled !== false }); } if (req.query.staffSeasonStartPaymentsSave === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Managing season start payments requires Owner only" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const clean = (n, fallback) => (Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 0) ? Math.round(Number(n)) : fallback; const config = { gold: clean(body.gold, DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.gold), silver: clean(body.silver, DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.silver), bronze: clean(body.bronze, DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.bronze), none: clean(body.none, DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.none), enabled: body.enabled === true, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: Date.now(), updatedBy: v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff" }; await db.doc("league_meta/seasonStartPayments").set(config, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, ...config }); } if (req.query.staffRunSeasonStartPayments === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Running season start payments requires Owner only" }); } const cfgSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonStartPayments").get(); const cfg = cfgSnap.exists ? cfgSnap.data() : {}; const amounts = { gold: Number.isFinite(Number(cfg.gold)) ? Number(cfg.none) : DEFAULT_SEASON_START_PAYMENTS.none }; if (cfg.enabled === false) return json(res, 400, { error: "Season start payments are currently disabled - enable and save first." }); const currentSeasonKey = await careerShopSeasonKeyNow(); const staffName = v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff"; const nowMs = Date.now(); const licencesSnap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); const totalLicences = licencesSnap.size; let paidCount = 0; let totalPaidAmount = 0; let skippedAlreadyPaid = 0; // Batched writes instead of one sequential read+write round-trip per // driver - with hundreds of licence holders, the old per-driver // await loop could run past the function's timeout, which Chrome // then misreports as a CORS failure rather than what it actually // was. "Gold Patreon" : tier === "silver" ? "Silver Patreon" : tier === "bronze" ? Buying is open to every driver, any tier - // only ownership of a production shop is Gold-gated, not marketplace // access. A 7% league cut is taken from every sale and credited to // league_meta/treasury (never paid to a person) - buyer pays the // full listed price, seller receives 93% of it. // // Same convention as `licences`/`tickets` elsewhere in this file: // small collections read in full with a `.limit()` and filtered/ // sorted in memory, rather than composite Firestore queries that // would need new indexes provisioned before this can go live. // ======================================================= function marketplaceId(prefix) { return `${prefix}_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`; } // ✅ ADDED: My Shop special offers pricing - given a listing's active // offers and how many units a buyer is taking, works out the CHEAPEST // price for the buyer across every offer that applies (a listing can // carry both a 'bogo' and a 'bulk' offer at once - whichever actually // saves the buyer more wins, never both stacked together). Real price // math, not a display-only badge - purchaseMarketplaceListing below // charges exactly what this returns. function computeBestOfferDiscount(offers, quantity, pricePerUnit) { const fullPrice = Math.round(pricePerUnit * quantity); const active = (Array.isArray(offers) ? offers : []).filter((o) => o && o.active !== false); let best = null; for (const o of active) { let price; if (o.type === "bogo") { if (quantity < 2) continue; const freeUnits = Math.floor(quantity / 2); price = pricePerUnit * (quantity - freeUnits); } else if (o.type === "bulk") { const thresholdQty = Number(o.thresholdQty) || 0; const discountPct = Number(o.discountPct) || 0; if (quantity < thresholdQty || discountPct <= 0) continue; price = pricePerUnit * quantity * (1 - discountPct / 100); } else continue; if (best === null || price < best.price) best = { price, offer: o }; } if (!best) return { totalPaid: fullPrice, fullPrice, savings: 0, appliedOffer: null }; const totalPaid = Math.max(0, Math.round(best.price)); return { totalPaid, fullPrice, savings: Math.max(0, fullPrice - totalPaid), appliedOffer: best.offer }; } if (req.query.myPartsInventory === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); if (!userId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing user id" }); const ledger = await ensureCareerShopCurrentSeason(userId); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, tyreInventoryCount: ledger.tyreInventoryCount, partsInventory: ledger.partsInventory, fuelInventoryLitres: ledger.fuelInventoryLitres }); } // ✅ ADDED: generic per-part-type artwork (Tyres/Engine/Suspension/ // Damper/Aero) shown next to every Marketplace listing, Craft Parts // card, and inventory row - a single shared image per part type, not // one per shop item (that's careerShopItem's imageUrl, unchanged). // Any signed-in driver can read it; only Owner can set it (see // staffSavePartsMarketplaceImage below and rtLicenceImageUpload's // "partsMarketplaceImage" kind for the actual file upload). if (req.query.partsMarketplaceImages === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? (appliedOffer.type === "bogo" ? " - Buy 1 Get 1 Free applied" : ` - ${appliedOffer.discountPct}% bulk discount applied`) : ""; // Buyer side: pay + receive stock. const buyerTx = { id: careerShopTxId(), type: "marketplacePurchase", amount: -totalPaid, description: `Marketplace: bought ${quantity} x ${listing.partType}${offerNote}`, reason: `Marketplace: bought ${quantity} x ${listing.partType}${offerNote}`, isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }; } else { const purchasedParts = (partsAcquired || []).map((p) => ({ ...p, source: "purchased", acquiredAtMs: nowMs })); buyerUpdate.partsInventory = [...buyerLedger.partsInventory, ...purchasedParts]; // League cut -> treasury (never paid to a person). await db.doc("league_meta/treasury").set({ balance: admin.firestore.FieldValue.increment(leagueCut), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); // Update / close out the listing. if (listing.partType === "tyre" || listing.partType === "fuel") { const newRemaining = (Number(listing.quantityRemaining) || 0) - quantity; A driver can // have multiple active backers at once, but their combined % can // never exceed 100 - enforced when an offer is ACCEPTED, not when // it's sent, since a driver may be sitting on several competing // pending offers and only accept some of them. // // Deliberately named "Driver Backing" (not "sponsorship") in every // field/endpoint/collection here - this codebase already has an // unrelated random lottery cash-bonus feature called "sponsorship" // (ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery / the `sponsorships` field), and // reusing that name would collide with it both in code and in a // driver's own head reading their transaction history. // // Firestore shape: // licences/{userId}.openMarketEnabled / .openMarketEnabledAtMs // driverBackingOffers/{offerId} - a backer's proposal, pending // until the driver accepts/declines it (or the backer withdraws it) // driverBackingContracts/{contractId} - created on accept; the live // agreement race-earnings settlement applies cuts/cover against, // including its own raceLog for a driver/backer to see exactly // what happened each race. // =========================================================== const IFWL_BACKING_MAX_RACES = 4; if (req.query.myBackingOverview === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); if (!userId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing user id" }); const [meSnap, offersSnap, contractsSnap] = await Promise.all([ licenceRef(userId).get(), db.collection("driverBackingOffers").limit(3000).get(), db.collection("driverBackingContracts").limit(3000).get() ]); const me = meSnap.exists ?
