Only the party who did NOT propose // the current terms may accept/decline/counter it. proposedBy: "backer", history: [], createdAtMs: nowMs }; const offerId = marketplaceId("bko"); await db.collection("driverBackingOffers").doc(offerId).set(offer); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, offer: { id: offerId, ...offer } }); } // ✅ ADDED: a driver (or the backer) can propose different terms back // on a pending offer instead of just accepting/declining outright - // updates the SAME offer doc in place (amount/races/pctBack overwritten // with the new proposal, prior terms pushed onto history) and flips // proposedBy to whoever just countered, so the ball is now in the // OTHER party's court. Deliberately light validation here (just the // same range checks sendBackingOffer uses) - balance and the driver's // 100% cap are only hard-enforced at actual accept time, same as the // original offer. if (req.query.counterBackingOffer === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); if (!(await requireCareerSystemOpen(res))) return; const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const body = req.body || {}; const offerId = String(body.offerId || "").trim(); const amount = Math.round(Number(body.amount) || 0); const races = Math.round(Number(body.races) || 0); const pctBack = Math.round(Number(body.pctBack) || 0); if (!offerId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing offerId" }); if (amount <= 0) return json(res, 400, { error: "Enter an amount greater than 0" }); if (races < 1 || races > IFWL_BACKING_MAX_RACES) return json(res, 400, { error: `Races must be between 1 and ${IFWL_BACKING_MAX_RACES}` }); if (pctBack < 1 || pctBack > 100) return json(res, 400, { error: "% back must be between 1 and 100" }); const offerRef = db.collection("driverBackingOffers").doc(offerId); const offerSnap = await offerRef.get(); if (!offerSnap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Offer not found" }); const offer = offerSnap.data() || {}; if (userId !== offer.driverId && userId !== offer.backerId) return json(res, 403, { error: "This isn't your offer" }); if (offer.status !== "pending") return json(res, 400, { error: "This offer is no longer pending" }); const respondentId = offer.proposedBy === "backer" ? offer.driverId : offer.backerId; if (userId !== respondentId) return json(res, 403, { error: "It's not your turn to respond to this offer" }); const nowMs = Date.now(); const priorRound = { amount: offer.amount, races: offer.races, pctBack: offer.pctBack, proposedBy: offer.proposedBy || "backer", atMs: offer.updatedAtMs || offer.createdAtMs || nowMs }; const history = [priorRound, ...(Array.isArray(offer.history) ? offer.history : [])].slice(0, 20); const newProposedBy = userId === offer.driverId ? "driver" : "backer"; await offerRef.set({ amount, races, pctBack, proposedBy: newProposedBy, history, updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, offer: { id: offerId, ...offer, amount, races, pctBack, proposedBy: newProposedBy, history, updatedAtMs: nowMs } }); } // ✅ ADDED: either side can cancel a still-pending negotiation at any // point (no money has moved yet, so this is low-risk) - not just the // original backer, since a driver who countered may also want to back // out before the backer responds. if (req.query.withdrawBackingOffer === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const body = req.body || {}; const offerId = String(body.offerId || "").trim(); if (!offerId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing offerId" }); const ref = db.collection("driverBackingOffers").doc(offerId); const snap = await ref.get(); if (!snap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Offer not found" }); const offer = snap.data() || {}; if (offer.backerId !== userId && offer.driverId !== userId) return json(res, 403, { error: "This isn't your offer" }); if (offer.status !== "pending") return json(res, 400, { error: "This offer is no longer pending" }); await ref.set({ status: "withdrawn", respondedAtMs: Date.now() }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } if (req.query.respondBackingOffer === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const body = req.body || {}; const offerId = String(body.offerId || "").trim(); const accept = !!body.accept; if (!offerId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing offerId" }); // Only ACCEPTING is blocked while the Career system is closed (it's // a brand new financial commitment) - declining stays open, same // reasoning as withdrawBackingOffer just below: getting out of a // pending negotiation is never the risky direction. if (accept && !(await requireCareerSystemOpen(res))) return; const offerRef = db.collection("driverBackingOffers").doc(offerId); const offerSnapPreCheck = await offerRef.get(); if (!offerSnapPreCheck.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Offer not found" }); const offerPreCheck = offerSnapPreCheck.data() || {}; // ✅ CHANGED: with counter-offers, either side can be the one // waiting on a response - only the party who did NOT propose the // current terms may accept/decline (see counterBackingOffer above). const respondentIdPreCheck = offerPreCheck.proposedBy === "backer" ? offerPreCheck.driverId : offerPreCheck.backerId; if (userId !== respondentIdPreCheck) return json(res, 403, { error: "It's not your turn to respond to this offer" }); if (offerPreCheck.status !== "pending") return json(res, 400, { error: "This offer is no longer pending" }); const nowMs = Date.now(); if (!accept) { await offerRef.set({ status: "declined", respondedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // Re-validate the driver's total committed % at ACCEPT time, not // just offer-send/counter time - other contracts may have been // accepted since. driverId is whichever side of the offer is the // DRIVER (not necessarily userId - the backer can be the one // accepting a driver's counter). const driverIdForAccept = offerPreCheck.driverId; const activeContractsSnap = await db.collection("driverBackingContracts").limit(3000).get(); const currentCommittedPct = activeContractsSnap.docs .map((d) => d.data() || {}) .filter((c) => c.driverId === driverIdForAccept && c.status === "active") .reduce((sum, c) => sum + (Number(c.pctBack) || 0), 0); if (currentCommittedPct + (Number(offerPreCheck.pctBack) || 0) > 100) { return json(res, 400, { error: `Accepting this would push the driver's total backing commitment to ${currentCommittedPct + offerPreCheck.pctBack}% - they already have ${currentCommittedPct}% committed and can't go over 100%.` }); } // ✅ ADDED: the actual accept - claiming the offer, moving money // between the two drivers, and creating the contract - now runs // inside a real Firestore transaction, so only ONE concurrent // request can ever win a given offer. Without this, two // near-simultaneous accepts (two tabs open, a flaky network retry) // could both pass the "still pending" checks above before either // write landed, creating two contracts and double-charging the // backer. Everything the transaction touches is read fresh INSIDE // it via tx.get - including the season-key-reset check // ensureCareerShopCurrentSeason normally does, inlined here since // that helper's own internal .get()/.set() aren't coordinated with // this transaction's tx object. const currentSeasonKey = await careerShopSeasonKeyNow(); const backerRef = licenceRef(offerPreCheck.backerId); const driverRef = licenceRef(driverIdForAccept); const contractId = marketplaceId("bkc"); const contractRef = db.collection("driverBackingContracts").doc(contractId); try { const result = await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const [offerSnap, backerSnap, driverSnap] = await Promise.all([tx.get(offerRef), tx.get(backerRef), tx.get(driverRef)]); if (!offerSnap.exists) throw new Error("Offer not found"); const offer = offerSnap.data() || {}; const currentRespondentId = offer.proposedBy === "backer" ? offer.driverId : offer.backerId; if (userId !== currentRespondentId) throw new Error("It's not your turn to respond to this offer"); if (offer.status !== "pending") throw new Error("This offer is no longer pending - it may have just been accepted, declined, or countered elsewhere"); if (!backerSnap.exists) throw new Error("The sponsor's account could not be found"); const bp = backerSnap.data() || {}; const backerIsCurrentSeason = bp.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKey; const backerBalance = backerIsCurrentSeason ? (Number(bp.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; const backerTransactions = backerIsCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(bp.careerShopTransactions) ? bp.careerShopTransactions : []; if (backerBalance < offer.amount) throw new Error("The sponsor no longer has enough balance to cover this offer - ask them to send a fresh offer once they've topped up."); if (!driverSnap.exists) throw new Error("Driver not found"); const dp = driverSnap.data() || {}; const driverIsCurrentSeason = dp.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKey; const driverBalance = driverIsCurrentSeason ? (Number(dp.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; const driverTransactions = driverIsCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(dp.careerShopTransactions) ? dp.careerShopTransactions : []; // ✅ ADDED: contractId (not just offerId) on the upfront-payment // transactions too, matching every race/sponsorship cut // transaction this contract will ever generate - lets a driver // or backer's own ledger be filtered by ONE contractId to get a // complete, accurate "money made or lost on this contract so // far" total (stake + every cut/cover since), see // renderDriverBackingSection's netSoFar in career.html. const backerTx = { id: careerShopTxId(), type: "backingContractStart", amount: -offer.amount, offerId, contractId, counterpartId: driverIdForAccept, counterpartName: offer.driverName || "a driver", description: `Driver Backing: backed ${offer.driverName || "a driver"} with ${offer.amount} over ${offer.races} race${offer.races === 1 ? "" : "s"} for ${offer.pctBack}%`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - upfront payment sent", isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }; const driverTx = { id: careerShopTxId(), type: "backingContractStart", amount: offer.amount, offerId, contractId, counterpartId: offer.backerId, counterpartName: offer.backerName || "a sponsor", description: `Driver Backing: accepted backing from ${offer.backerName || "a sponsor"} - ${offer.amount} over ${offer.races} race${offer.races === 1 ? "" : "s"} for ${offer.pctBack}%`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - upfront payment received", isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }; const contract = { offerId, backerId: offer.backerId, backerName: offer.backerName || "Unnamed driver", driverId: driverIdForAccept, driverName: offer.driverName || "Unnamed driver", amount: offer.amount, races: offer.races, pctBack: offer.pctBack, racesCompleted: 0, status: "active", raceLog: [], createdAtMs: nowMs }; tx.set(contractRef, contract); tx.set(offerRef, { status: "accepted", respondedAtMs: nowMs, contractId }, { merge: true }); tx.set(backerRef, { careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKey, careerShopBalance: backerBalance - offer.amount, careerShopTransactions: [backerTx, ...backerTransactions].slice(0, 200) }, { merge: true }); tx.set(driverRef, { careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKey, careerShopBalance: driverBalance + offer.amount, careerShopTransactions: [driverTx, ...driverTransactions].slice(0, 200) }, { merge: true }); return contract; }); // ✅ ADDED: announce a new Driver Backing contract to Discord the // moment an offer is accepted (money already moved by this // point, inside the transaction above) - fire-and-forget, same // pattern as every other Discord post in this file. publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord( licenceBotWebhook, `🤝 **New Driver Backing Contract!**\n**${discordSafe(result.backerName)}** is backing **${discordSafe(result.driverName)}** with £${(Number(result.amount) || 0).toLocaleString()} over ${result.races} race${result.races === 1 ? "" : "s"}, for ${result.pctBack}% back.\n[View Driver Backing](${CAREER_BACKING_URL})` ).catch((e) => logger.warn("Driver Backing contract Discord publish failed", e)); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, contract: { id: contractId, ...result } }); } catch (e) { return json(res, 400, { error: e?.message || "Could not accept this offer" }); } } // ✅ ADDED: Owner-only read-only overview, mirrors staffMarketplaceOverview's // shape - lets staff see the whole Driver Backing market at a glance // without any dedicated admin UI needing to exist for it yet. if (req.query.staffBackingOverview === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const v = verifySession(tokenFromQuery || bearer, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Driver Backing oversight requires Owner only" }); } const [offersSnap, contractsSnap] = await Promise.all([ db.collection("driverBackingOffers").limit(3000).get(), db.collection("driverBackingContracts").limit(3000).get() ]); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, offers: offersSnap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() })), contracts: contractsSnap.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, ...d.data() })) }); } // --------------------------- // RACEDAY INFO: driver-facing notification check // Only surfaces a briefing whose linked event is TODAY (UTC calendar // day) and which this driver is actually eligible for (same // tier/platform matching as Available Races) and hasn't already // acknowledged today. Client enforces the 60s minimum read timer; // this just decides whether to show anything at all. // --------------------------- if (req.query.myRacedayNotifications === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const profileSnap = await licenceRef(userId).get(); const profileData = profileSnap.exists ? profileSnap.data() : {}; const myTier = profileData.tier || "No Licence"; const myPlatform = profileData.platform || ""; const acknowledgedToday = profileData.racedayAcknowledged || {}; // ✅ ADDED: first-shown stamp per briefing per day, so the 60s forced // read timer counts real elapsed time since a driver first saw the // briefing today, not since whichever page load happens to be // asking - see the client-side note next to this timer for why. const firstShownToday = profileData.racedayFirstShown || {}; const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); const todayStart = Date.parse(todayStr + "T00:00:00Z"); const todayEnd = Date.parse(todayStr + "T23:59:59Z"); // ✅ FIXED (properly this time): the previous fix changed todayEnd, // which only controls whether an event's SCHEDULED race time counts // as "today" - it never checked what time the DRIVER is actually // loading the page. A race starting at 19:50 still passed that // check regardless, so nothing stopped the notification showing at // 23:16 or any other time before midnight. This checks the real // clock instead: once it's past 21:30 GMT today, stop showing // raceday briefings entirely until tomorrow, independent of what // time any individual race was scheduled for. const racedayCutoffMs = Date.parse(todayStr + "T21:30:00Z"); if (Date.now() > racedayCutoffMs) return json(res, 200, { ok: true, notifications: [] }); const eventsSnap = await db.collection("simgrid_events") .where("tiers", "array-contains", myTier) .limit(200) .get(); const todaysEventIds = eventsSnap.docs.filter((d) => { const x = d.data() || {}; if (x.active === false) return false; if (!x.eventDateMs || x.eventDateMs < todayStart || x.eventDateMs > todayEnd) return false; const eventPlatform = x.platform || "both"; if (myPlatform && myPlatform !== "both" && eventPlatform !== "both" && eventPlatform !== myPlatform) return false; return true; }).map((d) => d.id); if (!todaysEventIds.length) return json(res, 200, { ok: true, notifications: [] }); const infoSnap = await db.collection("raceday_info").get(); const nowMs = Date.now(); const stampsNeeded = {}; const notifications = infoSnap.docs.map((d) => { const x = d.data() || {}; if (x.active === false) return null; const linkedIds = Array.isArray(x.linkedEventIds) ? x.linkedEventIds : []; const matchesToday = linkedIds.some((id) => todaysEventIds.includes(id)); if (!matchesToday) return null; if (acknowledgedToday[d.id] === todayStr) return null; // already read today // First time this specific briefing has been fetched today gets // stamped now and stays fixed for the rest of the day; every // later fetch (a refresh, a trip to another page and back) reuses // that same stamp instead of restarting the clock. const existingStamp = firstShownToday[d.id]; let firstShownAtMs = existingStamp && existingStamp.date === todayStr ? existingStamp.atMs : null; if (!firstShownAtMs) { firstShownAtMs = nowMs; stampsNeeded[d.id] = { date: todayStr, atMs: nowMs }; } return { id: d.id, title: x.title || "", content: x.content || "", quickFacts: x.quickFacts || {}, videoLinks: Array.isArray(x.videoLinks) ? x.videoLinks : [], firstShownAtMs }; }).filter(Boolean); if (Object.keys(stampsNeeded).length) { const stampUpdate = {}; for (const [id, stamp] of Object.entries(stampsNeeded)) stampUpdate[`racedayFirstShown.${id}`] = stamp; await licenceRef(userId).set(stampUpdate, { merge: true }); } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, notifications }); } if (req.query.myRacedayAcknowledge === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const infoId = String(body.infoId || "").trim(); if (!infoId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing infoId" }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const todayStr = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10); await licenceRef(userId).set({ [`racedayAcknowledged.${infoId}`]: todayStr }, { merge: true }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true }); } // --------------------------- // SIMGRID EVENTS: MY AVAILABLE RACES (driver, filtered to their CURRENT tier) // If a driver is promoted, this list changes for them automatically on // their next visit - it's a live query against their licence doc's tier, // not a snapshot taken at promotion time. // --------------------------- if (req.query.myAvailableRaces === "1") { const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const userId = String(v.user?.sub || ""); const profileSnap = await licenceRef(userId).get(); const myTier = profileSnap.exists ? "both" // events always show; a driver with no platform set yet sees // everything (permissive default, so nobody's events silently // vanish just because they haven't filled that field in). const myPlatform = profileSnap.exists ? (profileSnap.data()?.platform || "") : ""; // ✅ ADDED: staff (and only staff - checked server-side against their // real Discord roles, never trusted from the query string alone) can // ask for every active race across every tier, not just the ones // gated to their own licence, so they can audit at a glance that // events are actually showing and which tier(s) each is gated to - // without switching tiers or digging through the separate Staff: // SimGrid Events management panel. const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; const staffAllRaces = req.query.staffAll === "1" && canManageLicences(roles); // ✅ ADDED: reconcile this driver's per-event Discord role grants // against their CURRENT tier every time they load this list (see // syncEventDiscordRoleGrants above) - self-healing, no need to hook // every place a tier can change. Skipped in staff-all mode: that's // an audit view of every race, not a real driver session to // reconcile grants against. const unlockedEventIds = staffAllRaces ? new Set() : await syncEventDiscordRoleGrants(db, userId, myTier, botToken).catch((e) => { logger.warn("myAvailableRaces: Discord role grant sync failed", e); return new Set(); }); const cutoffMs = Date.now() - (4 * 60 * 60 * 1000); const snap = staffAllRaces ? await db.collection("simgrid_events").limit(200).get() : await db.collection("simgrid_events").where("tiers", "array-contains", myTier).limit(100).get(); // ✅ ADDED (driver view only): this never actually hid a past event - // if you added all 9 rounds of a season as separate events, all 9 // would sit on this page forever, last week's races included, with // nothing to make "what's coming up next" naturally rotate. A 4-hour // grace period after the scheduled time (rather than hiding the // instant it starts) means a race still shows if someone checks // mid-event. Events with no date set at all always stay visible, // since there's nothing to judge "past" against. The staff audit // view deliberately skips this filter - staff want to see events // that HAVE dropped off drivers' lists too, so they can confirm // that's actually why a given race isn't showing. const events = snap.docs.map((d) => { const x = d.data() || {}; this // is purely for the staff audit view, showing every tier a race // is gated to and whether it's currently past the same 4h grace // period that would otherwise be hiding it from drivers. ...(staffAllRaces ? { tiers: Array.isArray(x.tiers) ? x.tiers : [], ended: !!(x.eventDateMs && x.eventDateMs < cutoffMs) } : {}) }; }).filter(Boolean); events.sort((a, b) => (a.eventDateMs || Infinity) - (b.eventDateMs || Infinity)); // Staff auditing every race wants to see every round of every // series, not just the next-due one - skip the next-round-only // collapse entirely in staff-all mode. if (staffAllRaces) { return json(res, 200, { ok: true, myTier, staffAllRaces: true, events }); } // ✅ ADDED: rounds are added one-per-round (e.g. "Round 4 - Hungaroring", // "Round 5 - Silverstone", all sharing the same championship // simgridLink) so drivers only ever needed to see the NEXT round due, // not the whole rest of the season sitting there at once. Groups // dated events by simgridLink and keeps only the soonest one per // group - once Round 4's date passes (the existing 4h grace period // above already handles that), Round 5 becomes the soonest in that // same group and takes its place automatically. Events with no date // set can't be sequenced this way, so they're left untouched, same // as before. A driver eligible for a genuinely separate championship // (different simgridLink) still sees that one's own next round too - // this only collapses rounds of the SAME series, never hides an // unrelated one. const nextRoundOnly = []; const seenLinkGroup = new Set(); for (const e of events) { if (!e.eventDateMs || !e.simgridLink) { nextRoundOnly.push(e); continue; } if (seenLinkGroup.has(e.simgridLink)) continue; // events are already sorted soonest-first, so the first one seen per link is the next round seenLinkGroup.add(e.simgridLink); nextRoundOnly.push(e); } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, myTier, events: nextRoundOnly }); } // --------------------------- // SIMGRID EVENTS: UNLOCK DISCORD ROLE (driver) // ?unlockEventDiscordRole=1&token=... (POST) body: { eventId } // The driver-facing half of the per-event discordRoleId field staff // set on Staff: SimGrid Events above. Re-checks eligibility // server-side against the driver's CURRENT tier/platform - never // trusts that the button was only shown to an eligible driver // client-side, since that state came from a fetch a moment earlier // and could be stale (a demotion since page load) or simply spoofed // (a raw POST with someone else's eventId) - then asks the IFWL // Discord bot to grant the role directly. Number(e.restrictor) : 0; entries.push({ track, carModel, ballastKg, restrictor }); } if (!entries.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "None of the uploaded entries had a valid track, carModel and ballastKg" }); const secPerKg = Number.isFinite(Number(body.secPerKg)) ? Math.max(0, Number(body.secPerRestrictorPct)) : 0.05; const nowMs = Date.now(); await db.doc("league_meta/bopData").set({ name, entries, secPerKg, secPerRestrictorPct, uploadedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), uploadedAtMs: nowMs, uploadedBy: v.user?.nick || v.user?.globalName || v.user?.username || "Staff" }); return json(res, 200, { ok: true, name, entryCount: entries.length, secPerKg, secPerRestrictorPct }); } if (req.query.staffBopCarCatalogSave === "1") { if (req.method !== "POST") return json(res, 405, { error: "POST only" }); const tokenFromQuery = String(req.query.token || ""); const authHeader = String(req.get("Authorization") || ""); const bearer = authHeader.startsWith("Bearer ") ? authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? v.user.roles : []; if (!canManageLicencesOwnerOnly(roles)) { return json(res, 403, { error: "Managing the BOP car catalog requires Owner only" }); } const body = typeof req.body === "object" ? req.body : {}; const cars = Array.isArray(body.cars) ? body.cars.map((c, i) => { const pcCarModelId = Number(c.pcCarModelId); // Console ID defaults to the same as PC - most cars aren't // swapped, only a known few (e.g. BMW/Ferrari) are, so staff // only needs to override those specific cars. const consoleCarModelId = Number.isFinite(Number(c.consoleCarModelId)) ? Now accepts discordIds (array, real bulk creation - one // record per driver) while entryId still supports editing a single // existing award, and a lone discordId is accepted too for // backward compatibility with any other caller still using it. const discordIds = Array.isArray(body.discordIds) ? body.discordIds.map((id) => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean) : (body.discordId ? [String(body.discordId).trim()] : []); if (!entryId && !discordIds.length) { return json(res, 400, { error: "Select at least one driver" }); } const tierListSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/licenceTiers").get(); const tierList = tierListSnap.exists ? (designSnap.data()?.designs || []) : []; const design = designList.find((x) => x.id === designId); trophyImageUrl = design ? design.url : ""; } const nowMs = Date.now(); const editor = { userId: v.user?.sub || "", username: v.user?.username || "user", nick: v.user?.nick || "" }; // Editing a single existing award (entryId present) - unchanged // single-record behaviour, uses the first/only discordId given. if (entryId) { const discordId = discordIds[0]; if (!discordId) return json(res, 400, { error: "Missing discordId" }); const driverSnap = await licenceRef(discordId).get(); if (!driverSnap.exists) return json(res, 404, { error: "Driver not found" }); const dp = driverSnap.data() || {}; const tierDef = tierList.find((t) => t.name === (dp.tier || "No Licence")); const tierColor = tierDef ? tierDef.color : "#93a1a9"; const record = { discordId, driverName: String(dp.name || "Unnamed driver").slice(0, 80), licenceNumber: String(dp.licenceNumber || "-"), tier: String(dp.tier || "No Licence"), tierColor, title, designId, trophyImageUrl, active: true, createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), createdAtMs: nowMs, createdBy: editor, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: nowMs, updatedBy: editor }; const ref = await db.collection("achievement_trophies").add(record); results.push({ id: ref.id, ...record }); } return json(res, 200, { ok: true, awardedCount: results.length, notFoundCount, results }); } // --------------------------- // ACHIEVEMENT TROPHIES: BULK AWARD (same trophy, many drivers at once) // Built for team-wide awards where a squad of up to ~15 drivers all // earn the same recognition - one title/design, a list of driver ids, // one call. authHeader.slice(7) : ""; const sessionToken = tokenFromQuery || bearer; const v = verifySession(sessionToken, secret); if (!v.ok) return json(res, 401, { error: v.error }); const roles = Array.isArray(v.user?.roles) ? if (badType) return json(res, 400, { error: "Only JPEG images are allowed" }); if (tooLarge) return json(res, 400, { error: "File too large (max 5MB)" }); const buffer = Buffer.concat(chunks); if (!buffer.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "Empty file" }); const userId = String(v.user.sub || "user"); const ts = Date.now(); const path = `gteam3/${userId}/team_${ts}.jpg`; const bucket = admin.storage().bucket("ifwl-591d8.firebasestorage.app"); const f = bucket.file(path); const downloadToken = require("crypto").randomUUID(); await f.save(buffer, { resumable: false, contentType: "image/jpeg", metadata: { cacheControl: "public, max-age=31536000", metadata: { firebaseStorageDownloadTokens: downloadToken } } }); // Firebase's own download-token URL scheme - the same one the client // SDK's getDownloadURL() produces. Deliberately NOT using getSignedUrl() // here: that requires the "Service Account Token Creator" IAM role, // which is a recurring source of exactly the kind of permission // headaches this sidesteps entirely - this only needs the file to exist. const url = `https://firebasestorage.googleapis.com/v0/b/${bucket.name}/o/${encodeURIComponent(path)}?alt=media&token=${downloadToken}`; return json(res, 200, { ok: true, url, path }); }); // Firebase's runtime already buffers the full request body into // req.rawBody before this handler runs, which drains req's readable // stream - piping an already-drained stream into Busboy means it never // receives any bytes and never fires its events. Everything else about this function is shared. const isVideoUpload = kindFromQuery === "ticketVideo"; // ✅ ADDED: livery zip - Owner-only, up to 60MB (a full ACC livery // pack with liveries.json + textures can run a fair bit bigger than a // plain image, but doesn't need video-sized headroom). const isZipUpload = kindFromQuery === "liveryFile"; const maxBytes = isVideoUpload ? 100 * 1024 * 1024 : isZipUpload ? 60 * 1024 * 1024 : 5 * 1024 * 1024; const ALLOWED_VIDEO_TYPES = { "video/mp4": "mp4", "video/quicktime": "mov", "video/webm": "webm" }; const ALLOWED_IMAGE_TYPES = { "image/jpeg": "jpg", "image/png": "png" }; // Browsers/OSes disagree on the MIME type for a .zip - Chrome on // Windows commonly sends application/x-zip-compressed, some send the // generic application/octet-stream. "Only MP4, MOV or WebM video files are allowed" : isZipUpload ? "Only .zip files are allowed" : "Only JPEG or PNG images are allowed" }); if (tooLarge) return json(res, 400, { error: `File too large (max ${isVideoUpload ? "100MB" : isZipUpload ? "60MB" : "5MB"})` }); const buffer = Buffer.concat(chunks); if (!buffer.length) return json(res, 400, { error: "Empty file" }); const userId = String(v.user.sub || "user"); const ts = Date.now(); const path = kind === "callingcard" ? `calling_cards/${ts}.jpg` : kind === "trophy" ? `trophy_designs/${ts}.jpg` : kind === "seasonalOverlay" ? `seasonal_overlays/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "affiliateAd" ? `affiliate_ads/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "seasonProgressionBg" ? `season_progression_bg/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "raceTeamLogo" || kind === "raceTeamBackground" ? `race_teams/${ts}_${kind}.${fileExt}` : kind === "bopCarImage" ? `bop_car_images/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "partsMarketplaceImage" ? `parts_marketplace_images/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "liveryPreview" ? `career_livery_previews/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "liveryFile" ? `career_liveries/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "acRallyProof" ? `ac_rally_proof/${userId}/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "acRallyEventImage" ? `ac_rally_event_images/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "rlMatchProof" ? `rl_match_proof/${userId}/${ts}.${fileExt}` : kind === "rlEventImage" ? `rl_event_images/${ts}.${fileExt}` : isVideoUpload ? `ticket_evidence/${userId}/${ts}.${fileExt}` : `licences/${userId}/${kind}_${ts}.jpg`; const bucket = admin.storage().bucket("ifwl-591d8.firebasestorage.app"); const f = bucket.file(path); const downloadToken = require("crypto").randomUUID(); await f.save(buffer, { resumable: false, // ✅ FIXED: this was hardcoded to "image/jpeg" for every non-video // upload, even when the actual bytes were a PNG - harmless while // every upload kind so far happened to be JPEG in practice, but // seasonal overlay art specifically needs real PNG transparency // to sit correctly on top of the licence card, so it has to be // stored with its real, detected content type. contentType: fileMimeType, metadata: { cacheControl: "public, max-age=31536000", metadata: { firebaseStorageDownloadTokens: downloadToken } } }); // Same fix as rtTeamImageUpload - Firebase's own download-token // scheme instead of getSignedUrl(), so this doesn't depend on the // Service Account Token Creator IAM role at all. const url = `https://firebasestorage.googleapis.com/v0/b/${bucket.name}/o/${encodeURIComponent(path)}?alt=media&token=${downloadToken}`; return json(res, 200, { ok: true, url, path, kind }); } catch (e) { // This inner catch is the actual fix - without it, any failure here // (most commonly getSignedUrl needing the "Service Account Token // Creator" IAM role) previously hung the request forever with no // response ever sent, instead of surfacing an error. logger.error("rtLicenceImageUpload finish handler failed", e); return json(res, 500, { error: String(e?.message || e) }); } }); // Same fix as rtTeamImageUpload above - Firebase's runtime has already // buffered the full body into req.rawBody before this code runs, so // req's readable stream is drained. Piping it into Busboy delivered // zero bytes, meaning 'finish' never fired - for a 5MB file or a 5KB // one, it made no difference, which is exactly what you were seeing. bb.end(req.rawBody); } catch (e) { logger.error(e); return json(res, 500, { error: String(e?.message || e) }); } }) ); // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED (Firestore trigger support) — existing code untouched // Auto ticket status + statement invites on ticket creation // - Changes "New Submission" -> "Under Review" // - Creates accused + reporter + witness invites // - Witness invite has higher maxUses because link is shared // - Uses idempotency flag invitesGenerated to prevent duplicates // ======================================================= // Keep aligned with your GitHub statement page (WPF uses this too) const IFWL_STATEMENT_BASE_URL = "https://ifwlowner.github.io/ACC-driver-classifier/playstatements.html"; // Defaults (change if you want) const IFWL_INVITE_EXPIRY_HOURS = 24; const IFWL_WITNESS_MAX_USES = 25; // Helpers (namespaced to avoid collisions) function ifwl_genInviteId(role) { const crypto = require("crypto"); const suffix = crypto.randomBytes(7).toString("hex"); // 14 chars return `${role}_${suffix}`.slice(0, 20); } function ifwl_genToken(bytes = 24) { const crypto = require("crypto"); return crypto .randomBytes(bytes) .toString("base64") .replace(/\+/g, "-") .replace(/\//g, "_") .replace(/=+$/g, ""); } function ifwl_buildLink(ticketId, inviteId) { const sep = IFWL_STATEMENT_BASE_URL.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"; return `${IFWL_STATEMENT_BASE_URL}${sep}ticketId=${encodeURIComponent(ticketId)}&inviteId=${encodeURIComponent(inviteId)}`; } // Firestore trigger: when a ticket is created exports.ifwlAutoReviewAndInvitesOnTicketCreate = onDocumentCreated( { document: "tickets/{ticketId}", region: "europe-west2" }, async (event) => { try { const ticketId = event.params.ticketId; const snap = event.data; if (!snap) return; const ticketRef = snap.ref; await db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const fresh = await tx.get(ticketRef); const ticket = fresh.data() || {}; // Prevent duplicates / replays if (ticket.invitesGenerated === true) return; // Only flip brand-new portal submissions // (ticketportal.html sets status: "New Submission") const updates = {}; if ((ticket.status || "") === "New Submission") { updates.status = "Under Review"; } const now = admin.firestore.Timestamp.now(); const expiresAt = admin.firestore.Timestamp.fromMillis( now.toMillis() + (IFWL_INVITE_EXPIRY_HOURS * 60 * 60 * 1000) ); const invitesCol = ticketRef.collection("invites"); const roles = [ { role: "accused", maxUses: 1 }, { role: "reporter", maxUses: 1 }, { role: "witness", maxUses: IFWL_WITNESS_MAX_USES } ]; for (const r of roles) { const inviteId = ifwl_genInviteId(r.role); const token = ifwl_genToken(24); tx.set( invitesCol.doc(inviteId), { role: r.role, token, createdAt: now, expiresAt, createdBy: "system", uses: 0, maxUses: r.maxUses, active: true, used: false // If you ever want the link stored server-side, uncomment: // , link: ifwl_buildLink(ticketId, inviteId) }, { merge: true } ); } updates.invitesGenerated = true; updates.invitesGeneratedAt = now; tx.set(ticketRef, updates, { merge: true }); }); } catch (e) { logger.error("ifwlAutoReviewAndInvitesOnTicketCreate failed", e); // Firestore triggers don't have res/json; just log } } ); // ======================================================= // ✅ CHANGED: LICENCE POINTS SYSTEM - supersedes the old standalone TLS / // Driver Traffic Light System tool (tls.html), which is being retired now // that the Licence Centre has a full licence system of its own. Points are // now SEVERITY-WEIGHTED (mild/standard/severe -> 1/2/3, parsed exactly the // way TLS itself always parsed a penalty's severity) rather than a flat 1 // per finalised ticket, a Beginner driver's first at-fault incident each // Season is logged but excluded from their total ("Beginner leeway"), // totals reset each Season the same lazy way the Career Shop ledger does, // and crossing a band (AMBER / FINAL WARNING) now automatically notifies // the driver, raises a staff alert, and posts to Discord - only the top // band (7+, ESCALATE) is left as a manual Head Steward/Owner call (via the // existing staffLicenceBan tool), so nothing here jumps ahead of that // decision by publicly flagging a driver before staff have actually acted. // // Bands: 0-2 no action / 3-4 AMBER / 5-6 FINAL WARNING / 7+ ESCALATE. // ======================================================= const IFWL_SEVERITY_POINTS = { mild: 1, standard: 2, severe: 3 }; const IFWL_NO_ACTION_PATTERNS = /no action|no further action|\bnfa\b|dismiss/i; // Ported from tls.html's parseSeverityFromText() so a penalty classifies // exactly the same way it always has for staff - an RPxxxMI/ST/SE/SV code // suffix, or an explicit MILD/STANDARD/SEVERE word in the penalty text, // defaulting to STANDARD when neither is found. function ifwlParseSeverityKey(text) { const src = String(text || "").trim().toUpperCase(); if (!src) return "standard"; const mCode = src.match(/\bRP[0-9A-Z]+(MI|ST|SE|SV)\b/); if (mCode && mCode[1]) { const s = mCode[1]; if (s === "MI") return "mild"; if (s === "ST") return "standard"; if (s === "SE" || s === "SV") return "severe"; } if (src.includes("MILD")) return "mild"; if (src.includes("SEVERE")) return "severe"; if (src.includes("STANDARD")) return "standard"; return "standard"; } function ifwlSeverityPointsFromText(text) { return IFWL_SEVERITY_POINTS[ifwlParseSeverityKey(text)] || IFWL_SEVERITY_POINTS.standard; } // Beginner leeway is keyed off the DRIVER'S OWN current licence tier (not // the race/event class TLS itself used to define "beginner") - matched as // a loose substring so it keeps working however staff have customised the // tier's display name on league_meta/licenceTiers (live site currently // shows "IFWL-GT-Beginner" rather than DEFAULT_LICENCE_TIERS' default // "GT3 Beginner"). function ifwlIsBeginnerTierName(tierName) { return String(tierName || "").toUpperCase().includes("BEGINNER"); } function ifwlLicencePointsBand(points) { const n = Number(points) || 0; if (n >= 7) return "escalate"; if (n >= 5) return "warning"; if (n >= 3) return "amber"; return "none"; } // Shared so the live band-crossing path (ifwlApplyLicencePointsChange) and // the one-time backfill below (staffLicencePointsBackfill) always word // notices identically. function ifwlLicencePointsNoticeText(band, points) { if (band === "amber") return `Your licence points have reached ${points} (AMBER). Keep your record clean - a further incident may bring a final warning.`; if (band === "warning") return `FINAL WARNING: your licence points have reached ${points}. Any further incident may result in a ban.`; return `Your licence points have reached ${points}. This has been referred to Head Steward/Owner for review.`; } // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: AC RALLY KNOCKOUT EVENTS // A staff-run dynamic knockout event type, separate from the SimGrid- // registration-driven "Available Races" system (simgrid_events) - drivers // sign THEMSELVES up here, staff "lock in" the field once it's a clean // power-of-two (4/8/16/32/...), and the system builds the round structure // automatically: every round races the same set of stages, ranks drivers // by combined stage time, and cuts the bottom half - down to a 4-driver // Final that crowns a single champion directly (never a 4->2->1 knockout). // One doc per event in ac_rally_events, holding every round/stage/result/ // submission - small enough (max 32 drivers, a handful of rounds) to stay // well within Firestore's per-doc size limit and lets every state change // (signup, lock-in, submission, review, advance) go through one // transaction. // // Stage times come from drivers themselves (self-reported), each required // to attach a screenshot as proof - staff Approve or Refuse each // submission; either decision deletes the screenshot from Storage right // away (see ifwlDeleteStorageFile) so proof images never pile up once // they've served their purpose. // ======================================================= function ifwlAcRallyIsPowerOfTwoAtLeast4(n) { return Number.isInteger(n) && n >= 4 && (n & (n - 1)) === 0; } function ifwlAcRallyRoundLabel(startCount) { if (startCount <= 4) return "Final"; if (startCount === 8) return "Semi Final"; if (startCount === 16) return "Quarter Final"; return "Qualifier"; // 32 or more } function ifwlAcRallyDefaultStageCount(startCount) { return startCount <= 4 ? 4 : 3; // Final defaults to 4 stages, every earlier round to 3 } // Builds the full rounds[] skeleton for a freshly locked-in event - stage // configs are left as staff-editable defaults (track/weather/assists // blank), and only round 0 gets its driver roster filled in here (later // rounds are populated as each prior round advances). function ifwlAcRallyBuildRounds(bracketSize) { const rounds = []; let count = bracketSize; let roundIndex = 0; while (count >= 4) { const stageCount = ifwlAcRallyDefaultStageCount(count); rounds.push({ roundIndex, name: ifwlAcRallyRoundLabel(count), stages: Array.from({ length: stageCount }, (_, i) => ({ stageIndex: i, name: `Stage ${i + 1}`, track: "", weather: "", assists: "" })), drivers: [], results: {}, submissions: [], status: roundIndex === 0 ? "open" : "pending", advancedAtMs: null }); if (count === 4) break; // the Final cuts 4 -> 1 directly, never 4 -> 2 -> 1 count = count / 2; roundIndex++; } return rounds; } // Ranks a round's drivers by combined verified stage time (a driver // missing even one verified stage time - refused, never submitted, or // still pending review - counts as incomplete and always ranks below // every driver with a full set of verified times, in whatever order they // came in). Returns the same array sorted with rank/combinedSeconds/ // complete set on each entry - does not mutate Firestore itself, callers // decide what to do with the ranking. function ifwlAcRallyRankRound(round) { const driverIds = Array.isArray(round.drivers) ? round.drivers : []; const stageCount = Array.isArray(round.stages) ? round.stages.length : 0; const ranked = driverIds.map((id) => { const r = (round.results || {})[id] || {}; const stageTimes = r.stageTimes || {}; const values = Object.values(stageTimes).filter((v) => Number.isFinite(v)); const complete = stageCount > 0 && values.length >= stageCount; const combinedSeconds = complete ? values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) : null; return { driverId: id, name: r.name || "", licenceNumber: r.licenceNumber || "", combinedSeconds, complete }; }); ranked.sort((a, b) => { if (a.complete && b.complete) return a.combinedSeconds - b.combinedSeconds; if (a.complete) return -1; if (b.complete) return 1; return 0; }); ranked.forEach((r, i) => { r.rank = i + 1; }); return ranked; } // Deletes one file from the Firebase Storage bucket - used to remove a // stage-time proof screenshot the moment staff verify or refuse it. // ignoreNotFound so a double-review race (or a file that somehow never // saved) never throws back into the caller's transaction-adjacent flow. async function ifwlDeleteStorageFile(path) { if (!path) return; try { await admin.storage().bucket("ifwl-591d8.firebasestorage.app").file(String(path)).delete({ ignoreNotFound: true }); } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlDeleteStorageFile: could not delete ${path}`, e); } } // ======================================================= // RL 3v3 KNOCKOUT (Rocket League) - same event/bracket family as AC // Rally, but a standard head-to-head single-elimination bracket (pairwise // matches) instead of a ranked field cut each round, since Rocket League // results are decided match-by-match, not by a combined time. Squads are // auto-formed from the solo signup pool at lock-in (random shuffle, fixed // size of 3) and stay fixed for the whole event - only which squads are // still alive changes round to round. Deliberately its own separate // Firestore collection (rl_events) and its own "RL 3v3 Results" page area // from AC Rally, per the explicit ask that the two formats not compete // for the same space. // ======================================================= const IFWL_RL_SQUAD_SIZE = 3; // Entrant count must divide evenly into squads of 3, with a power-of-two // squad count (>= 2) - same "no bye support, trim/pad first" convention // AC Rally uses for drivers, just extended one level up to squads. function ifwlRlIsValidEntrantCount(entrantCount) { if (!Number.isInteger(entrantCount) || entrantCount <= 0 || entrantCount % IFWL_RL_SQUAD_SIZE !== 0) return false; const squadCount = entrantCount / IFWL_RL_SQUAD_SIZE; return squadCount >= 2 && (squadCount & (squadCount - 1)) === 0; } function ifwlRlRoundLabel(squadCount) { if (squadCount <= 2) return "Final"; if (squadCount === 4) return "Semi Final"; if (squadCount === 8) return "Quarter Final"; return `Round of ${squadCount}`; // 16, 32, ... } function ifwlRlDefaultBestOf(squadCount) { return squadCount <= 2 ? 5 : 3; // Final is best of 5, every earlier round best of 3 } // Random shuffle + chunk the signup pool into squads of 3. Squad ids are // short stable strings (squad_0, squad_1, ...) - they only ever live // inside the event doc, never referenced outside it, so there's no need // for a Firestore-generated id here. function ifwlRlBuildSquads(entrants) { const shuffled = [...entrants]; for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) { const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1)); [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]]; } const squads = []; for (let i = 0; i < shuffled.length; i += IFWL_RL_SQUAD_SIZE) { const members = shuffled.slice(i, i + IFWL_RL_SQUAD_SIZE); squads.push({ id: `squad_${squads.length}`, name: `Squad ${squads.length + 1}`, drivers: members.map((e) => ({ driverId: e.driverId, name: e.name, licenceNumber: e.licenceNumber })) }); } return squads; } // Pairs an ordered list of squad ids into matches (squadIds[0] v // squadIds[1], squadIds[2] v squadIds[3], ...) - used both for round 0 // (from the freshly-built squad list) and for every later round (from the // previous round's winners, still in bracket order). function ifwlRlPairSquadsIntoMatches(squadIds, bestOf) { const matches = []; for (let i = 0; i < squadIds.length; i += 2) { matches.push({ matchIndex: matches.length, squadAId: squadIds[i] || null, squadBId: squadIds[i + 1] || null, bestOf, status: "open", submissions: [], finalScoreA: null, finalScoreB: null, winnerSquadId: null, completedAtMs: null }); } return matches; } // Builds the full rounds[] skeleton for a freshly locked-in event - only // round 0's matches are populated (from the squad list); every later // round's matches start empty and get filled in by staffRlAdvanceRound as // each prior round's winners become known - same "advance is what fills // the next round" shape AC Rally uses. function ifwlRlBuildRounds(squadCount, squadIds) { const rounds = []; let count = squadCount; let roundIndex = 0; while (count >= 2) { const bestOf = ifwlRlDefaultBestOf(count); rounds.push({ roundIndex, name: ifwlRlRoundLabel(count), bestOf, status: roundIndex === 0 ? "open" : "pending", matches: roundIndex === 0 ? ifwlRlPairSquadsIntoMatches(squadIds, bestOf) : [], scheduledAtMs: null, advancedAtMs: null }); if (count === 2) break; // the Final IS the 2-squad round count = count / 2; roundIndex++; } return rounds; } // Same lazy season-key reset pattern as the Career Shop ledger // (careerShopSeasonKeyNow/ensureCareerShopCurrentSeason, further up this // file) - kept as its own tiny module-level copy because this is called // from ifwlAutoLicencePointsOnTicketFinalise, a top-level Firestore // trigger with no access to careerShopSeasonKeyNow() (defined INSIDE the // rtGate HTTP handler's own closure). async function ifwlLicencePointsSeasonKeyNow() { const snap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonWindow").get(); const data = snap.exists ? snap.data() : {}; return String(data?.startDate || "no-season-set"); } // The one shared core every point-adding path (the auto ticket trigger // below AND the staffLicencePoints manual-adjustment endpoint) runs // through, so both always agree on season reset, Beginner leeway and // band-crossing detection. Runs inside its own transaction - callers // should NOT wrap this in another transaction. pointsHistory is a // PERMANENT audit log and is never touched by the season reset, only the // active licencePoints total (and beginnerLeewayUsed) resets. async function ifwlApplyLicencePointsChange(driverRef, { rawDelta, reason, setBy, allowBeginnerLeeway = false }) { const currentSeasonKey = await ifwlLicencePointsSeasonKeyNow(); const nowMs = Date.now(); return db.runTransaction(async (tx) => { const snap = await tx.get(driverRef); if (!snap.exists) throw new Error("Driver not found"); const p = snap.data() || {}; // ✅ IMPORTANT: distinguish "never had a season key yet" (this driver's // very first touch by the new system) from "stale key from a past // season". The former must NOT reset to 0 - per the confirmed decision, // every driver's new-system standing starts from whatever licencePoints // they already have (no TLS history migrates in, but the existing flat- // rate auto-points total that was already accruing here is kept as the // clean-start baseline). Only an actual season-boundary change resets. const hasSeasonKey = typeof p.licencePointsSeasonKey === "string" && p.licencePointsSeasonKey.length > 0; const seasonStale = hasSeasonKey && p.licencePointsSeasonKey !== currentSeasonKey; const before = seasonStale ? 0 : (Number(p.licencePoints || 0)); const beginnerLeewayUsed = seasonStale ? false : (p.beginnerLeewayUsed === true); const pointsHistory = Array.isArray(p.pointsHistory) ? p.pointsHistory : []; const priorBand = ifwlLicencePointsBand(before); let delta = Math.round(Number(rawDelta) || 0); let leewayApplied = false; if (allowBeginnerLeeway && delta > 0 && !beginnerLeewayUsed && ifwlIsBeginnerTierName(p.tier)) { leewayApplied = true; delta = 0; // recorded in history below, but excluded from the total } const after = Math.max(0, before + delta); const newBand = ifwlLicencePointsBand(after); pointsHistory.push({ delta, before, after, reason: leewayApplied ? `${reason} (Beginner leeway - excluded from total)` : reason, date: new Date().toISOString(), dateMs: nowMs, setBy }); // Driver-facing unseen-notice queue (mirrors the career.html pattern // used for unseen sponsorship deals) - only written when points are // CLIMBING into a worse band (bandRank increasing), never on a // downward move - a staff correction that happens to cross a // threshold on the way DOWN (e.g. an escalate-band driver being // corrected back to warning) must never fire an "escalating" notice. // Capped at 20 entries. const bandRank = { none: 0, amber: 1, warning: 2, escalate: 3 }; const notices = Array.isArray(p.licencePointsNotices) ? p.licencePointsNotices.slice(-19) : []; if (bandRank[newBand] > bandRank[priorBand] && (newBand === "amber" || newBand === "warning" || newBand === "escalate")) { const noticeText = ifwlLicencePointsNoticeText(newBand, after); notices.push({ id: `lpn_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, text: noticeText, band: newBand, points: after, dateMs: nowMs, seen: false }); } const update = { licencePoints: after, licencePointsSeasonKey: currentSeasonKey, beginnerLeewayUsed: beginnerLeewayUsed || leewayApplied, pointsHistory, licencePointsNotices: notices, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: nowMs }; tx.set(driverRef, update, { merge: true }); return { before, after, delta, leewayApplied, priorBand, newBand, // Only true on an upward move into a worse band - never on a // downward correction that happens to cross a threshold going down. bandCrossed: bandRank[newBand] > bandRank[priorBand], driverName: p.name || "Unnamed driver", driverId: driverRef.id }; }); } // Fires the staff-alert + Discord side-effects for a band crossing. // Deliberately OUTSIDE the transaction above (a Firestore transaction // shouldn't hold external network calls) - call this right after that // transaction commits. ESCALATE (7+) intentionally only creates a staff // alert, never a Discord post or driver-facing announcement - see the // big comment above this section for why that step stays manual. async function ifwlNotifyLicencePointsBandCrossing(result, webhookUrl) { if (!result?.bandCrossed) return; const { newBand, driverId, driverName, after } = result; if (newBand !== "amber" && newBand !== "warning" && newBand !== "escalate") return; const nowMs = Date.now(); try { await db.collection("staffAlerts").add({ type: "licencePointsBand", band: newBand, driverId, driverName, points: after, createdAtMs: nowMs, createdAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), acknowledged: false }); } catch (e) { logger.warn("ifwlNotifyLicencePointsBandCrossing: staffAlerts write failed", e); } if (newBand === "escalate") return; // staff alert only - manual Head Steward/Owner action from here try { const label = newBand === "warning" ? "⚠️ **FINAL WARNING**" : "🟠 **Licence Points - Amber**"; const content = `${label}\n${discordSafe(driverName)} is now on **${after} licence points**.` + (newBand === "warning" ? " Any further incident may result in a ban." : " A further incident may bring a final warning."); await publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord(webhookUrl, content); } catch (e) { logger.warn("ifwlNotifyLicencePointsBandCrossing: Discord post failed", e); } } function ifwl_accusedNamesFromTicket(t) { const raw = t?.accused; if (Array.isArray(raw)) return raw.filter(Boolean).map(String); if (typeof raw === "string" && raw.trim()) return [raw.trim()]; return []; } // Fires only on the actual transition to "Finalised" (re-saving an // already-finalised ticket does not re-award points), and only when a // genuine penalty was applied - a dismissed ticket / "no further action" // outcome awards nothing. Matches the accused name(s) against every licence // profile's accDriverNames the same substring-based way every other feature // on the Licence Centre does, since ticket accused strings often carry a // bracketed car number a driver's self-reported name usually won't include // verbatim. If no licence is linked yet, this just logs and does nothing - // Head Steward can link one manually from the ticket review page, and the // points can then be added by hand for that specific ticket if still needed. exports.ifwlAutoLicencePointsOnTicketFinalise = onDocumentUpdated( { document: "tickets/{ticketId}", region: "europe-west2", secrets: [LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK] }, async (event) => { try { const before = event.data?.before?.data() || {}; const after = event.data?.after?.data() || {}; const beforeStatus = String(before.status || "").toLowerCase(); const afterStatus = String(after.status || "").toLowerCase(); if (afterStatus !== "finalised" || beforeStatus === "finalised") return; const penaltyText = String(after.finalPenaltyCode || after.postRaceCode || after.ruleBreach || "").trim(); if (!penaltyText || IFWL_NO_ACTION_PATTERNS.test(penaltyText)) return; const accusedNames = ifwl_accusedNamesFromTicket(after).map((n) => n.trim().toLowerCase()); if (!accusedNames.length) return; const snap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); let matchedRef = null; for (const doc of snap.docs) { const arr = doc.data()?.accDriverNames; if (!Array.isArray(arr)) continue; const hit = arr.some((n) => { const key = String(n).trim().toLowerCase(); return accusedNames.some((a) => a.includes(key) || key.includes(a)); }); if (hit) { matchedRef = doc.ref; break; } } if (!matchedRef) { logger.warn(`ifwlAutoLicencePointsOnTicketFinalise: no linked licence for accused [${accusedNames.join(", ")}] on ticket ${event.params.ticketId}`); return; } const severityPoints = ifwlSeverityPointsFromText(penaltyText); const result = await ifwlApplyLicencePointsChange(matchedRef, { rawDelta: severityPoints, reason: `Auto: ticket ${event.params.ticketId} finalised - ${penaltyText}`, setBy: "system (ticket finalisation)", allowBeginnerLeeway: true }); await ifwlNotifyLicencePointsBandCrossing(result, LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK.value()); } catch (e) { logger.error("ifwlAutoLicencePointsOnTicketFinalise failed", e); } } ); // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: SCHEDULED QUALIFICATION-COMPLETION CHECK // Runs periodically, independent of anyone actually visiting the site - // re-derives lap totals per driver from the same public manifest + session // files the licence centre's own stats engine reads client-side (there's no // separate server-side copy of this data, so this replicates that same // logic here), filtered to the CURRENT season window, and posts a Discord // notification the first time a driver crosses the qualification threshold // on either server. A driver is only ever notified once per server - a // `qualNotified.` flag on their own licence profile is what // prevents this from re-firing on every run once they're already qualified. // ======================================================= const IFWL_MANIFEST_URL = "https://ifwlowner.github.io/ACC-driver-classifier/data/manifest.json"; const IFWL_QUAL_MIN_LAPS = 15; const IFWL_QUAL_SERVERS = ["pc-licence", "console-licence"]; function ifwlServerIdFromPath(path) { const match = String(path || "").match(/([a-z0-9-]+)\/[^/]+$/i); const folder = match ? match[1].toLowerCase() : ""; return IFWL_QUAL_SERVERS.includes(folder) ? folder : ""; } function ifwlFileTimestampMs(fileName) { const name = String(fileName || "").split("/").pop(); const m = name.match(/^(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})_(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})_/); if (!m) return 0; const [, yy, mm, dd, hh, mi, ss] = m; return Date.UTC(2000 + Number(yy), Number(mm) - 1, Number(dd), Number(hh), Number(mi), Number(ss)); } async function ifwlFetchJsonPublic(url) { const res = await fetch(url); if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`Fetch failed (${res.status}) for ${url}`); const buf = Buffer.from(await res.arrayBuffer()); return JSON.parse(ifwlDecodeJsonBuffer(buf)); } // ACC's dedicated server (and the classifier pipeline republishing its // result files) writes some/all race-result JSON as UTF-16LE, often // without a BOM. Node's fetch()/res.json() decodes response bodies as // UTF-8 by default - for UTF-16LE content that turns every ASCII // character into "", which breaks JSON.parse immediately // (typically right after the opening "{", since that's the first // character whose following NUL byte isn't a valid property name or "}") - // identically for every affected file, regardless of its actual content. // This is exactly the "Expected property name or '}' in JSON at position 1" // error seen across every _R.json file on both PC and Console servers once // this bug was diagnosed. Detect the encoding from the raw bytes and decode // accordingly instead of trusting res.json()'s UTF-8 assumption. function ifwlDecodeJsonBuffer(buf) { if (buf.length >= 2 && buf[0] === 0xFF && buf[1] === 0xFE) { return buf.slice(2).toString("utf16le"); // UTF-16LE with BOM } if (buf.length >= 2 && buf[0] === 0xFE && buf[1] === 0xFF) { return buf.slice(2).swap16().toString("utf16le"); // UTF-16BE with BOM } if (buf.length >= 4) { // Heuristic for BOM-less UTF-16LE: ASCII-range JSON encoded this way // has a NUL byte after nearly every character. let nulAtOdd = 0; const sampleLen = Math.min(buf.length, 64); for (let i = 1; i < sampleLen; i += 2) if (buf[i] === 0x00) nulAtOdd++; if (nulAtOdd / Math.floor(sampleLen / 2) > 0.8) return buf.toString("utf16le"); } let text = buf.toString("utf8"); if (text.charCodeAt(0) === 0xFEFF) text = text.slice(1); // strip stray UTF-8 BOM return text; } // ✅ ADDED: bounded-concurrency fetch helper, ported over from the exact // same fix already proven on the frontend (ifwl-stats-engine.js's // mapWithConcurrency) for the exact same underlying problem - fetching a // season's worth of session files ONE AT A TIME, sequentially awaited, is // fine early in a qualification window but gets slower every single week // as more files pile up (pc-licence/console-licence never prune anything, // by design - see "Prepare result folders" in the sync workflow), and // ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions only had a 300s Cloud Function // timeout. Once the sequential fetch loop started taking longer than // that, the function would get killed after it had already posted a // Discord update but before (or during) the Firestore write that marks // each driver's qualNotified flag - so the write never landed, and the // very next run (30 minutes later) found the exact same drivers "not yet // notified" again and re-posted the identical update. Bounding the fetch // loop to a handful of concurrent requests instead of one at a time // brings total runtime back down comfortably inside the timeout, so the // notify-write actually gets a chance to run and complete. async function ifwlMapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) { let nextIndex = 0; async function runNext() { while (nextIndex < items.length) { const idx = nextIndex++; await worker(items[idx], idx); } } await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(limit, items.length) }, runNext)); } // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: RACE EARNINGS -> REAL SHOP BALANCE // Previously, everything on My Season Progression (points, Race Earnings, // Net Event Earnings) was purely a client-side display, recalculated fresh // on every page load and never actually paid into the spendable IFWL Shop // balance - the "1 pot" the Shop/perks/sponsorships all share. This closes // that gap: race results now genuinely pay into the same real balance, // per individual race, within the current Season Window. // // Money-crediting can NEVER trust client-reported results (a browser could // fabricate any result it likes), so this independently re-fetches and // re-computes each race server-side from the same public result files // Live Timings and My Season Progression already read - it does not trust // anything the frontend sends it. // // Tyre spend uses a flat, deterministic assumption per race weekend - 16 // tyres (8 for the race itself, including one assumed pit stop; +4 for // Practice; +4 for Qualifying) - rather than trying to detect real pit // stops. There is no genuine pit-stop count anywhere in this data // pipeline; the field that used to stand in for one (isValidForBest) is // actually ACC's own track-limit-violation flag on individual laps, not // pit data, so it was never a real signal to begin with. // // Idempotency: each driver's licence doc keeps a // careerShopCreditedRaceFiles array of race file paths already paid out - // re-running this (scheduled or manual) only ever pays a given driver for // a given race exactly once, regardless of how many times it runs. // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace constants - shared between the request // handler above (careerShopPurchase, staffCareerShopCatalogSave, the new // marketplace endpoints) and ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore below, so declared // truly top-level rather than nested inside either. Production/wear rates // and the league cut % aren't staff-editable yet (only the four // maintenance costs are, via Seasonal Costs) - these are mine to set // (delegated by staff) and can be made configurable later if needed. // ✅ ADDED: "fuelDepot" - Race Fuel's Shop/Factory category, same flat // bonusPct-discount family as tyreFactory (NOT one of the 3-tier shops // below) since fuel is a consumable priced per litre, not a durable part // with a life%. const IFWL_PARTS_SHOP_CATEGORIES = ["tyreFactory", "engineShop", "tuningShop", "aeroShop", "fuelDepot"]; const IFWL_PARTS_WEAR_PCT = { engine: 25, suspension: 25, damper: 50, aero: 50 }; const IFWL_TYRE_FACTORY_PRODUCTION_PER_RACE = 32; // covers the owner's own 16-tyre need plus a 16-tyre surplus to sell const IFWL_PART_SHOP_PRODUCTION_PER_RACE = 1; // one spare part per race weekend raced, per part type that shop makes const IFWL_MARKETPLACE_LEAGUE_CUT_PCT = 7; const DEFAULT_PARTS_MAINTENANCE_COSTS = { engine: 8000, suspension: 6000, damper: 4000, aero: 5000 }; // ✅ ADDED: 3-tier Shop/Factory system - Engine Shop, Tuning Shop and Aero // Tuning Company each now come in Level 1/2/3 variants (Tyre Factory is // deliberately excluded - it stays on its own bonusPct/costReduction system, // per request). A catalog item's `tier` field (1-3, default 1 for any // existing/un-tiered item so nothing breaks for shops staff already set up) // drives two things everywhere in the economy: how cheap it is to CRAFT // that shop's part (tier's % of RRP - a steep discount at Level 3), and how // much life a produced/crafted part starts with (a Level 2/3 shop's output // is worth more precisely because it lasts longer, not because it's // cheaper per-weekend). A driver can own more than one tier of the same // shop (e.g. bought Level 1 early, later bought Level 2) - the highest tier // owned is what counts, so buying up never loses anything. const IFWL_TIERED_SHOP_CATEGORIES = ["engineShop", "tuningShop", "aeroShop"]; const IFWL_SHOP_TIER_CRAFT_COST_PCT = { 1: 80, 2: 50, 3: 15 }; // craft cost = this % of RRP const IFWL_SHOP_TIER_LIFE_PCT = { 1: 100, 2: 125, 3: 150 }; // starting conditionPct of a produced/crafted part function ifwlShopItemTier(it) { const t = Math.round(Number(it && it.tier) || 1); return t >= 1 && t <= 3 ? t : 1; } // Highest tier of `category` the driver owns, from their ownedItemIds - 0 if // they don't own any item in that category at all (i.e. don't own the // shop/factory itself). function ifwlEffectiveShopTier(shopCatalog, ownedItemIds, category) { const owned = shopCatalog.filter((it) => it.category === category && ownedItemIds.includes(it.id)); if (!owned.length) return 0; return Math.max(...owned.map(ifwlShopItemTier)); } const IFWL_RACE_EARNINGS_TYRES_PER_RACE = 16; const IFWL_RACE_EARNINGS_DEFAULT_TABLE = { 1:30000,2:22000,3:16000,4:12000,5:9500,6:8000,7:7000,8:6000,9:5200,10:4500, 11:4000,12:3600,13:3300,14:3000,15:2800,16:2600,17:2400,18:2200,19:2000,20:1800, 21:1650,22:1500,23:1400,24:1300,25:1200,26:1100,27:1000,28:950,29:900,30:850, 31:800,32:750,33:700,34:650,35:600,36:550,37:500,38:475,39:450,40:425, 41:400,42:375,43:350,44:325,45:300,46:275,47:250,48:225,49:200,50:175 }; const IFWL_SP_PERK_TRACK_ANALYSIS_BONUS = 0.022; const IFWL_SP_PERK_STRATEGY_BONUS = 0.03; const IFWL_SP_PERK_TYRE_DISCOUNT = 0.05; const IFWL_SP_PERK_ENTRY_FEE_DISCOUNT = 0.06; // ✅ ADDED: Main Sponsor Program - picks whichever of a driver's two // per-season sponsor deals (seasonStart / midSeason) was locked most // recently as of a given race's own timestamp, so a mid-season // renegotiation only affects races from that point forward, even when a // backlog spanning both halves gets settled in one run. Returns 0 (no // effect) for a driver who never negotiated a deal, or whose stored deals // belong to a past season (seasonKey mismatch). function ifwlTitleSponsorPctForRace(titleSponsorDeals, currentSeasonKey, raceTs) { if (!titleSponsorDeals || titleSponsorDeals.seasonKey !== currentSeasonKey) return 0; const candidates = ["seasonStart", "midSeason"] .map((k) => titleSponsorDeals[k]) .filter((d) => d && Number.isFinite(Number(d.lockedAtMs)) && Number(d.lockedAtMs) <= raceTs); if (!candidates.length) return 0; candidates.sort((a, b) => Number(b.lockedAtMs) - Number(a.lockedAtMs)); return Number(candidates[0].multiplierPct) || 0; } async function ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore() { const seasonSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonWindow").get(); const season = seasonSnap.exists ? seasonSnap.data() : {}; const startMs = season.startDate ? Date.parse(season.startDate + "T00:00:00Z") : 0; const endMs = season.endDate ? Date.parse(season.endDate + "T23:59:59Z") : Date.now(); if (!startMs) return { creditedCount: 0, totalNetCredited: 0, skipped: "no_season_window" }; const currentSeasonKey = String(season.startDate || "no-season-set"); const costsSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonalCosts").get(); const costsData = costsSnap.exists ? costsSnap.data() : {}; const entryFeePerRace = Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.entryFeePerRace)) ? Number(costsData.entryFeePerRace) : 10000; const pricePerTyre = Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.tyre?.pricePerTyre)) ? Number(costsData.tyre.pricePerTyre) : 1023; const raceEarningsTable = (costsData.raceEarnings && Object.keys(costsData.raceEarnings).length) ? costsData.raceEarnings : IFWL_RACE_EARNINGS_DEFAULT_TABLE; // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace maintenance costs for this settlement pass. const partsMaintenanceCosts = { engine: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.partsMaintenance?.engine)) ? Number(costsData.partsMaintenance.engine) : DEFAULT_PARTS_MAINTENANCE_COSTS.engine, suspension: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.partsMaintenance?.suspension)) ? Number(costsData.partsMaintenance.suspension) : DEFAULT_PARTS_MAINTENANCE_COSTS.suspension, damper: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.partsMaintenance?.damper)) ? Number(costsData.partsMaintenance.damper) : DEFAULT_PARTS_MAINTENANCE_COSTS.damper, aero: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.partsMaintenance?.aero)) ? Number(costsData.partsMaintenance.aero) : DEFAULT_PARTS_MAINTENANCE_COSTS.aero }; // ✅ ADDED: Race Fuel costs/consumption for this settlement pass - see // DEFAULT_SEASONAL_COSTS.fuel for what each field means. const fuelCosts = { pricePerLitre: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.fuel?.pricePerLitre)) ? Number(costsData.fuel.pricePerLitre) : 2.3, practiceQualifyingLitres: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.fuel?.practiceQualifyingLitres)) ? Number(costsData.fuel.practiceQualifyingLitres) : 120, raceLitres: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.fuel?.raceLitres)) ? Number(costsData.fuel.raceLitres) : 120, standardRaceLaps: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.fuel?.standardRaceLaps)) ? Number(costsData.fuel.standardRaceLaps) : 20, extraLitresPerLap: Number.isFinite(Number(costsData.fuel?.extraLitresPerLap)) ? Number(costsData.fuel.extraLitresPerLap) : 2.75 }; const fuelBaselineLitresPerWeekend = fuelCosts.practiceQualifyingLitres + fuelCosts.raceLitres; const shopCatalogSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/careerShop").get(); const shopCatalog = shopCatalogSnap.exists ? (shopCatalogSnap.data()?.items || []) : []; const manifest = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(IFWL_MANIFEST_URL); const rawItems = manifest.results || manifest.files || []; const raceFiles = rawItems .map((x) => (typeof x === "string" ? { file: x } : { file: x.file || x.path || x.name })) .filter((x) => x.file && /_R\.json$/i.test(x.file)) .map((x) => ({ file: x.file, tsMs: ifwlFileTimestampMs(x.file.split("/").pop()) })) .filter((x) => x.tsMs && x.tsMs >= startMs && x.tsMs <= endMs); if (!raceFiles.length) return { creditedCount: 0, totalNetCredited: 0, skipped: "no_race_files_in_window" }; // Fetch each race file exactly once, not per driver. const raceRowsByFile = new Map(); for (const rf of raceFiles) { try { const data = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(`https://ifwlowner.github.io/ACC-driver-classifier/${rf.file}`); const lines = (data.sessionResult && data.sessionResult.leaderBoardLines) || []; // ✅ ADDED: lapCount per row, straight from the same ACC session JSON // field (l.timing.lapCount) ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions already // reads for a different purpose - used below for Race Fuel's "extra // litres beyond a standard race distance" charge. const rows = lines.filter((l) => !l.bIsSpectator).map((l) => { const d = l.currentDriver || (l.car && l.car.drivers && l.car.drivers[0]) || {}; const name = `${d.firstName || ""} ${d.lastName || ""}`.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() || d.shortName || ""; const lapCount = Number(l.timing?.lapCount || 0); return { name, lapCount }; }); raceRowsByFile.set(rf.file, { rows, tsMs: rf.tsMs, track: String(data.trackName || "").trim() }); } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore: skipped unreadable file ${rf.file}`, e); } } // Tickets are fetched once and matched in memory per driver, same // substring-alias convention used everywhere else in this file. const ticketsSnap = await db.collection("tickets").limit(2000).get(); const toNameArray = (raw) => Array.isArray(raw) ? raw.filter(Boolean).map(String) : (typeof raw === "string" && raw ? [raw] : []); const allTickets = ticketsSnap.docs.map((d) => { const t = d.data() || {}; return { accused: toNameArray(t.accused).map((s) => s.toLowerCase()), track: String(t.track || "").toLowerCase(), timestampMs: t.timestamp && t.timestamp.toMillis ? t.timestamp.toMillis() : null, finalPenalty: String(t.finalPenalty || "").trim() }; }); const licencesSnap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); let creditedCount = 0; let totalNetCredited = 0; const nowMs = Date.now(); // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing / Sponsorship Contracts - fetched ONCE up // front (not per-driver inside the loop below) and grouped by driverId, // so applying a contract's per-race cut/cover during settlement costs no // extra Firestore reads per race. Backer-side balance changes and // contract racesCompleted/status updates are accumulated in these two // maps across the ENTIRE settlement pass (every driver, every newly // credited race) and only written once each, after the main loop below - // see the second pass right after this function's main loop closes. const backingContractsSnap = await db.collection("driverBackingContracts").limit(3000).get(); const activeContractsByDriver = new Map(); backingContractsSnap.docs.forEach((d) => { const c = { id: d.id, ref: d.ref, ...d.data() }; if (c.status !== "active") return; const list = activeContractsByDriver.get(c.driverId) || []; list.push(c); activeContractsByDriver.set(c.driverId, list); }); const backingBackerDeltas = new Map(); // backerId -> { delta, txEntries: [] } const backingContractUpdates = new Map(); // contractId -> { contract, racesCompletedDelta, raceLogEntries: [] } for (const doc of licencesSnap.docs) { const p = doc.data() || {}; const aliases = (p.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) continue; const alreadyCredited = new Set(Array.isArray(p.careerShopCreditedRaceFiles) ? p.careerShopCreditedRaceFiles : []); const perks = Array.isArray(p.careerPerks) ? p.careerPerks : []; const hasPerk = (id) => perks.includes(id); const isCurrentSeason = p.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKey; let runningBalance = isCurrentSeason ? (Number(p.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; let runningTransactions = isCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(p.careerShopTransactions) ? p.careerShopTransactions : []; let runningOwnedItemIds = isCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(p.careerShopOwnedItemIds) ? p.careerShopOwnedItemIds : []; // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace inventory, carried through this driver's // race-file loop the same way balance/transactions/owned items already are. let runningTyreInventory = isCurrentSeason ? (Number(p.tyreInventoryCount) || 0) : 0; let runningPartsInventory = isCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(p.partsInventory) ? p.partsInventory.slice() : []; // ✅ ADDED: Race Fuel inventory, carried through this driver's race-file // loop exactly like runningTyreInventory above. let runningFuelInventory = isCurrentSeason ?
