(Number(p.fuelInventoryLitres) || 0) : 0; const newlyCredited = []; let driverNetTotal = 0; let touched = false; const shopRaceIncomeBonusPct = shopCatalog .filter((it) => it.category === "teamGear" && runningOwnedItemIds.includes(it.id)) .reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.bonusPct) || 0), 0); // ✅ CHANGED: category "tyreFactory" also qualifies for the tyre-cost // discount, not just legacy "costReduction" - lets the existing IFWL // Tyre Factory item keep its personal discount once staff recategorise // it to "tyreFactory" to also gain production (see IFWL_PARTS_SHOP_CATEGORIES). const shopTyreCostReductionPct = shopCatalog .filter((it) => (it.category === "costReduction" || it.category === "tyreFactory") && it.costTarget === "tyre" && runningOwnedItemIds.includes(it.id)) .reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.bonusPct) || 0), 0); // ✅ ADDED: same flat-discount mechanism, for Race Fuel - a Fuel Depot // (or a costReduction item targeting "fuel"). const shopFuelCostReductionPct = shopCatalog .filter((it) => (it.category === "costReduction" || it.category === "fuelDepot") && it.costTarget === "fuel" && runningOwnedItemIds.includes(it.id)) .reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.bonusPct) || 0), 0); const shopEntryFeeReductionPct = shopCatalog .filter((it) => it.category === "costReduction" && it.costTarget === "entryFee" && runningOwnedItemIds.includes(it.id)) .reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.bonusPct) || 0), 0); // ✅ CHANGED: Engine Shop/Tuning Shop/Aero Tuning Company are now // 3-tier - the discount on a CASH-charged maintenance cost (only // charged when a driver has no owned stock left to consume instead, // see consumeDurablePart below) comes purely from the highest tier // owned of that shop, same tier pricing used for crafting // (IFWL_SHOP_TIER_CRAFT_COST_PCT: Level 1 pays 80% of RRP i.e. a 20% // reduction, Level 2 pays 50% i.e. 50% off, Level 3 pays 15% i.e. 85% // off) rather than a staff-set bonusPct. Also tracks the tier's part // life% (IFWL_SHOP_TIER_LIFE_PCT), used below wherever this shop // produces a fresh part. const PART_SHOP_CATEGORY = { engine: "engineShop", suspension: "tuningShop", damper: "tuningShop", aero: "aeroShop" }; const partShopTier = Object.fromEntries( Object.keys(PART_SHOP_CATEGORY).map((pt) => [pt, ifwlEffectiveShopTier(shopCatalog, runningOwnedItemIds, PART_SHOP_CATEGORY[pt])]) ); const partShopCostReductionPct = Object.fromEntries( Object.keys(PART_SHOP_CATEGORY).map((pt) => [ pt, partShopTier[pt] > 0 ? Math.max(0, 100 - (IFWL_SHOP_TIER_CRAFT_COST_PCT[partShopTier[pt]] ?? 100)) : 0 ]) ); const partShopLifePct = Object.fromEntries( Object.keys(PART_SHOP_CATEGORY).map((pt) => [ pt, partShopTier[pt] > 0 ? (IFWL_SHOP_TIER_LIFE_PCT[partShopTier[pt]] ?? 100) : 100 ]) ); // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace shop ownership - determines production // eligibility each raced weekend, below. const ownsShopCategory = (category) => shopCatalog.some((it) => it.category === category && runningOwnedItemIds.includes(it.id)); const ownsTyreFactory = ownsShopCategory("tyreFactory"); const ownsEngineShop = ownsShopCategory("engineShop"); const ownsTuningShop = ownsShopCategory("tuningShop"); const ownsAeroShop = ownsShopCategory("aeroShop"); const ownsFuelDepot = ownsShopCategory("fuelDepot"); for (const [file, race] of raceRowsByFile.entries()) { if (alreadyCredited.has(file)) continue; const myIdx = race.rows.findIndex((r) => { const key = r.name.trim().toLowerCase(); return aliases.some((a) => key.includes(a) || a.includes(key)); }); if (myIdx < 0) continue; const position = myIdx + 1; const points = Math.max(0, 51 - position); const raceTs = race.tsMs; const cleanBonus = !allTickets.some((t) => t.accused.some((a) => aliases.some((al) => a.includes(al) || al.includes(a))) && t.timestampMs && t.timestampMs >= raceTs && t.timestampMs <= raceTs + (3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000)); const IFWL_NO_ACTION_RE = /no.?action|dismiss/i; const noPenaltyThisRace = !allTickets.some((t) => t.accused.some((a) => aliases.some((al) => a.includes(al) || al.includes(a))) && t.track === race.track.toLowerCase() && t.timestampMs && t.timestampMs >= raceTs && t.timestampMs <= raceTs + (3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) && t.finalPenalty && !IFWL_NO_ACTION_RE.test(t.finalPenalty) ); let raceEarnings = raceEarningsTable[position] || 0; if (hasPerk("trackAnalysis")) raceEarnings = Math.round(raceEarnings * (1 + IFWL_SP_PERK_TRACK_ANALYSIS_BONUS)); if (hasPerk("strategyCommand") && noPenaltyThisRace) raceEarnings = Math.round(raceEarnings * (1 + IFWL_SP_PERK_STRATEGY_BONUS)); if (shopRaceIncomeBonusPct > 0) raceEarnings = Math.round(raceEarnings * (1 + shopRaceIncomeBonusPct / 100)); // ✅ ADDED: Main Sponsor Program multiplier - see // ifwlTitleSponsorPctForRace above. const titleSponsorPct = ifwlTitleSponsorPctForRace(p.titleSponsorDeals, currentSeasonKey, raceTs); if (titleSponsorPct) raceEarnings = Math.round(raceEarnings * (1 + titleSponsorPct / 100)); // ✅ ADDED: Parts Marketplace production - shops only produce for a // race weekend the owner actually raced (this loop iteration IS // that - each iteration is one newly-credited race file), matching // "gated on racing" like everything else in this economy. if (ownsTyreFactory) runningTyreInventory += IFWL_TYRE_FACTORY_PRODUCTION_PER_RACE; // ✅ ADDED: Fuel Depot production - same "2x the owner's own weekend // need" shape as the Tyre Factory (IFWL_TYRE_FACTORY_PRODUCTION_PER_RACE // is 2x the fixed 16-tyre need), just computed dynamically since the // fuel baseline is staff-editable rather than a fixed constant. if (ownsFuelDepot) runningFuelInventory += fuelBaselineLitresPerWeekend * 2; const produceDurablePart = (partType) => { // ✅ CHANGED: a produced part now starts at its shop tier's life% // (100/125/150 for Level 1/2/3) instead of a flat 100 - a Level 2/3 // Engine Shop etc. produces parts that simply last longer. const lifePct = partShopLifePct[partType] || 100; const tier = partShopTier[partType] || 1; for (let i = 0; i < IFWL_PART_SHOP_PRODUCTION_PER_RACE; i++) { runningPartsInventory.push({ id: `part_${raceTs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, partType, conditionPct: lifePct, tier, source: "produced", acquiredAtMs: raceTs }); } }; if (ownsEngineShop) produceDurablePart("engine"); if (ownsTuningShop) { produceDurablePart("suspension"); produceDurablePart("damper"); } if (ownsAeroShop) produceDurablePart("aero"); const effectiveTyreCost = pricePerTyre * (hasPerk("tyreManagement") ? (1 - IFWL_SP_PERK_TYRE_DISCOUNT) : 1) * (shopTyreCostReductionPct > 0 ? Math.max(0, 1 - shopTyreCostReductionPct / 100) : 1); // ✅ CHANGED: draw from owned tyre stock (produced or bought on the // marketplace) before charging cash for the rest - "the system uses // the stack they have purchased before charging them for new". const tyresNeeded = IFWL_RACE_EARNINGS_TYRES_PER_RACE; const tyresFromStock = Math.min(runningTyreInventory, tyresNeeded); runningTyreInventory -= tyresFromStock; const tyresToBuy = tyresNeeded - tyresFromStock; const tyreSpend = Math.round(tyresToBuy * effectiveTyreCost); // ✅ ADDED: Race Fuel - flat P&Q + Race litres every raced weekend, // PLUS extra litres for laps actually run beyond fuelCosts. // standardRaceLaps (read straight from this race file's ACC lapCount // for this driver, see raceRowsByFile above) - covers longer/ // endurance-format weekends costing more fuel than a normal sprint // one. Same "draw from owned stock first, charge cash for the rest at // the driver's own discounted rate" pattern as tyres. const myLapCount = Number(race.rows[myIdx]?.lapCount) || 0; const extraLaps = Math.max(0, myLapCount - fuelCosts.standardRaceLaps); const litresNeeded = fuelBaselineLitresPerWeekend + (extraLaps * fuelCosts.extraLitresPerLap); const effectiveFuelPrice = fuelCosts.pricePerLitre * (shopFuelCostReductionPct > 0 ? Math.max(0, 1 - shopFuelCostReductionPct / 100) : 1); const litresFromStock = Math.min(runningFuelInventory, litresNeeded); runningFuelInventory -= litresFromStock; const litresToBuy = litresNeeded - litresFromStock; const fuelSpend = Math.round(litresToBuy * effectiveFuelPrice); // ✅ ADDED: durable parts (engine/suspension/damper/aero) - each // raced weekend, wear down the most-worn owned usable unit of that // type; if none is usable, charge the maintenance cost in cash // instead (no inventory added for a cash charge - same "charge them // and replace" behaviour as a cash tyre buy). const consumeDurablePart = (partType, wearPct) => { const candidates = runningPartsInventory .map((part, i) => ({ part, i })) .filter(({ part }) => part.partType === partType && Number(part.conditionPct) > 0) .sort((a, b) => Number(a.part.conditionPct) - Number(b.part.conditionPct)); if (candidates.length) { const { i } = candidates[0]; const newCondition = Number(runningPartsInventory[i].conditionPct) - wearPct; if (newCondition <= 0) { runningPartsInventory.splice(i, 1); } else { runningPartsInventory[i] = { ...runningPartsInventory[i], conditionPct: newCondition }; } return 0; } const reductionPct = partShopCostReductionPct[partType] || 0; const effectiveCost = (partsMaintenanceCosts[partType] || 0) * Math.max(0, 1 - reductionPct / 100); return Math.round(effectiveCost); }; const engineSpend = consumeDurablePart("engine", IFWL_PARTS_WEAR_PCT.engine); const suspensionSpend = consumeDurablePart("suspension", IFWL_PARTS_WEAR_PCT.suspension); const damperSpend = consumeDurablePart("damper", IFWL_PARTS_WEAR_PCT.damper); const aeroSpend = consumeDurablePart("aero", IFWL_PARTS_WEAR_PCT.aero); const effectiveEntryFee = entryFeePerRace * (hasPerk("pitCrewTraining") ? (1 - IFWL_SP_PERK_ENTRY_FEE_DISCOUNT) : 1) * (shopEntryFeeReductionPct > 0 ? Math.max(0, 1 - shopEntryFeeReductionPct / 100) : 1); const entryFee = Math.round(effectiveEntryFee); const netEventEarnings = raceEarnings - entryFee - tyreSpend - fuelSpend - engineSpend - suspensionSpend - damperSpend - aeroSpend; const tx = { id: `cstx_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, type: "race", amount: netEventEarnings, description: `Race result - P${position}${cleanBonus ? " (clean)" : ""} at ${race.track || "unknown track"}`, reason: `Race result - P${position}${cleanBonus ? " (clean)" : ""} at ${race.track || "unknown track"}`, isManual: false, dateMs: raceTs, dateLabel: new Date(raceTs).toISOString(), tyreSpend, fuelSpend, engineSpend, suspensionSpend, damperSpend, aeroSpend, // ✅ ADDED: structured fields (not just baked into description) so // the Owner dashboard's "Latest Race — Cost Breakdown Per Driver" // table can render position/track/gross/entry fee directly instead // of parsing text - description/reason above are left untouched // for anything already reading them. track: race.track || "", position, raceEarningsGross: raceEarnings, entryFee, cleanBonus }; runningBalance += netEventEarnings; // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing / Sponsorship Contracts - apply this // driver's active backing contracts' cut/cover for THIS race, right // where netEventEarnings for the race is known. Positive // netEventEarnings -> the backer takes their agreed % OUT of the // driver's balance; negative netEventEarnings (a losing race) -> the // backer pays their % IN to help cover the driver's loss. One // formula (runningBalance -= cutAmount, backer += cutAmount) handles // both directions correctly since cutAmount itself is negative on a // losing race. A contract stops applying once it hits its agreed // race count (races, capped at 4 when the contract was created) - // racesAppliedSoFar accounts for races already applied earlier in // THIS SAME settlement pass, not just what was persisted before it // started, since one pass can credit several newly-raced files at once. const backingTxs = []; const driverActiveContracts = activeContractsByDriver.get(doc.id) || []; for (const contract of driverActiveContracts) { const priorUpdate = backingContractUpdates.get(contract.id); const racesAppliedSoFar = (Number(contract.racesCompleted) || 0) + (priorUpdate ? priorUpdate.racesCompletedDelta : 0); if (racesAppliedSoFar >= (Number(contract.races) || 0)) continue; // contract already fulfilled const cutAmount = Math.round(netEventEarnings * ((Number(contract.pctBack) || 0) / 100)); runningBalance -= cutAmount; const backingTx = { id: `bktx_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, type: "backingRaceSettlement", amount: -cutAmount, contractId: contract.id, counterpartId: contract.backerId, counterpartName: contract.backerName || "a sponsor", description: cutAmount >= 0 ? `Driver Backing: paid ${contract.backerName || "a sponsor"} ${Math.abs(cutAmount)} (${contract.pctBack}% of this race)` : `Driver Backing: ${contract.backerName || "a sponsor"} covered ${Math.abs(cutAmount)} of this race's loss (${contract.pctBack}%)`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - per-race settlement", isManual: false, dateMs: raceTs, dateLabel: new Date(raceTs).toISOString() }; backingTxs.push(backingTx); const backerEntry = backingBackerDeltas.get(contract.backerId) || { delta: 0, txEntries: [] }; backerEntry.delta += cutAmount; backerEntry.txEntries.push({ id: `bktx_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, type: "backingRaceSettlement", amount: cutAmount, contractId: contract.id, counterpartId: doc.id, counterpartName: p.name || "a driver", description: cutAmount >= 0 ? `Driver Backing: received ${cutAmount} from ${p.name || "a driver"} (${contract.pctBack}% of their race)` : `Driver Backing: covered ${Math.abs(cutAmount)} of ${p.name || "a driver"}'s race loss (${contract.pctBack}%)`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - per-race settlement", isManual: false, dateMs: raceTs, dateLabel: new Date(raceTs).toISOString() }); backingBackerDeltas.set(contract.backerId, backerEntry); const contractUpdate = backingContractUpdates.get(contract.id) || { contract, racesCompletedDelta: 0, raceLogEntries: [] }; contractUpdate.racesCompletedDelta += 1; contractUpdate.raceLogEntries.push({ track: race.track || "", position, netEventEarnings, cutAmount, dateMs: raceTs }); backingContractUpdates.set(contract.id, contractUpdate); } runningTransactions = [tx, ...backingTxs, ...runningTransactions].slice(0, 200); newlyCredited.push(file); driverNetTotal += netEventEarnings; touched = true; } if (touched) { await doc.ref.set({ careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKey, careerShopBalance: runningBalance, careerShopOwnedItemIds: runningOwnedItemIds, careerShopTransactions: runningTransactions, careerShopCreditedRaceFiles: [...alreadyCredited, ...newlyCredited].slice(-2000), // ✅ ADDED: persist Parts Marketplace inventory changes from this pass. tyreInventoryCount: runningTyreInventory, partsInventory: runningPartsInventory.slice(0, 500), fuelInventoryLitres: runningFuelInventory, updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); creditedCount++; totalNetCredited += driverNetTotal; } } // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing / Sponsorship Contracts - second pass, once // per backer / once per contract touched in this whole settlement run // (not once per race), applying the accumulated deltas from the loop // above. Wrapped per-entry in try/catch so one bad/missing backer doc // (e.g. deleted account) can't abort the rest of race-earnings // settlement - it just gets logged and skipped. for (const [backerId, entry] of backingBackerDeltas.entries()) { if (!entry.delta && !entry.txEntries.length) continue; try { // ✅ NOTE: ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore is a top-level function (also // called from a scheduled trigger, not just the router), so the // router-local ensureCareerShopCurrentSeason() helper isn't in scope // here - this inlines the same season-key-reset read/write shape it // uses, reusing currentSeasonKey already computed above in this // function rather than recomputing it. const backerRef = licenceRef(backerId); const backerSnap = await backerRef.get(); if (!backerSnap.exists) { console.error(`Driver Backing: backer ${backerId} not found, skipping race-cut delta`); continue; } const bp = backerSnap.data() || {}; const backerIsCurrentSeason = bp.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKey; const backerBalance = backerIsCurrentSeason ? (Number(bp.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; const backerTransactions = backerIsCurrentSeason && Array.isArray(bp.careerShopTransactions) ? bp.careerShopTransactions : []; await backerRef.set({ careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKey, careerShopBalance: backerBalance + entry.delta, careerShopTransactions: [...entry.txEntries, ...backerTransactions].slice(0, 200), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(`Driver Backing: could not settle backer ${backerId}'s race-cut delta`, e); } } for (const [contractId, update] of backingContractUpdates.entries()) { try { const newRacesCompleted = (Number(update.contract.racesCompleted) || 0) + update.racesCompletedDelta; const isComplete = newRacesCompleted >= (Number(update.contract.races) || 0); const priorRaceLog = Array.isArray(update.contract.raceLog) ? update.contract.raceLog : []; const patch = { racesCompleted: newRacesCompleted, raceLog: [...update.raceLogEntries, ...priorRaceLog].slice(0, 50), updatedAtMs: nowMs }; if (isComplete) { patch.status = "completed"; patch.completedAtMs = nowMs; } await update.contract.ref.set(patch, { merge: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(`Driver Backing: could not update contract ${contractId} after settlement`, e); } } return { creditedCount, totalNetCredited, raceFilesChecked: raceFiles.length }; } // ✅ ADDED: companion diagnostic to ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore above - see // the staffDiagnoseRaceEarningsMatching endpoint for why this exists. Pure // read: fetches the same season-window race files and extracts driver // names the exact same way, but never writes anything. async function ifwlDiagnoseRaceEarningsMatching() { const seasonSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonWindow").get(); const season = seasonSnap.exists ? seasonSnap.data() : {}; const startMs = season.startDate ? Date.parse(season.startDate + "T00:00:00Z") : 0; const endMs = season.endDate ? Date.parse(season.endDate + "T23:59:59Z") : Date.now(); if (!startMs) return { skipped: "no_season_window", raceFilesChecked: 0, raceDriverNames: [], licences: [], unmatchedRaceNames: [] }; const manifest = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(IFWL_MANIFEST_URL); const rawItems = manifest.results || manifest.files || []; const raceFiles = rawItems .map((x) => (typeof x === "string" ? { file: x } : { file: x.file || x.path || x.name })) .filter((x) => x.file && /_R\.json$/i.test(x.file)) .map((x) => ({ file: x.file, tsMs: ifwlFileTimestampMs(x.file.split("/").pop()) })) .filter((x) => x.tsMs && x.tsMs >= startMs && x.tsMs <= endMs); if (!raceFiles.length) return { skipped: "no_race_files_in_window", raceFilesChecked: 0, raceDriverNames: [], licences: [], unmatchedRaceNames: [] }; // driver name (lowercased) -> { name, count, tracks: Set } const raceNameStats = new Map(); for (const rf of raceFiles) { try { const data = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(`https://ifwlowner.github.io/ACC-driver-classifier/${rf.file}`); const lines = (data.sessionResult && data.sessionResult.leaderBoardLines) || []; const track = String(data.trackName || "").trim(); for (const l of lines) { if (l.bIsSpectator) continue; const d = l.currentDriver || (l.car && l.car.drivers && l.car.drivers[0]) || {}; const name = `${d.firstName || ""} ${d.lastName || ""}`.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() || d.shortName || ""; if (!name) continue; const key = name.trim().toLowerCase(); if (!raceNameStats.has(key)) raceNameStats.set(key, { name, count: 0, tracks: new Set() }); const entry = raceNameStats.get(key); entry.count++; if (track) entry.tracks.add(track); } } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlDiagnoseRaceEarningsMatching: skipped unreadable file ${rf.file}`, e); } } const raceNameKeys = [...raceNameStats.keys()]; const licencesSnap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); let licencesWithAliases = 0; let licencesWithoutAliases = 0; const licences = []; for (const doc of licencesSnap.docs) { const p = doc.data() || {}; const aliases = (p.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) { licencesWithoutAliases++; continue; } licencesWithAliases++; const matchedKeys = raceNameKeys.filter((key) => aliases.some((a) => key.includes(a) || a.includes(key))); licences.push({ name: p.name || p.licenceNumber || doc.id, accDriverNames: p.accDriverNames || [], matched: matchedKeys.length > 0, matchedRaceNames: matchedKeys.map((k) => raceNameStats.get(k).name) }); } const matchedNameSet = new Set(licences.flatMap((l) => l.matchedRaceNames.map((n) => n.toLowerCase()))); const unmatchedRaceNames = [...raceNameStats.values()] .filter((e) => !matchedNameSet.has(e.name.toLowerCase())) .map((e) => ({ name: e.name, appearances: e.count, tracks: [...e.tracks] })) .sort((a, b) => b.appearances - a.appearances); return { raceFilesChecked: raceFiles.length, licencesWithAliases, licencesWithoutAliases, raceDriverNames: [...raceNameStats.values()] .map((e) => ({ name: e.name, appearances: e.count, tracks: [...e.tracks] })) .sort((a, b) => b.appearances - a.appearances), licences: licences.sort((a, b) => (a.matched === b.matched) ? 0 : (a.matched ? 1 : -1)), unmatchedRaceNames }; } exports.ifwlAutoCreditRaceEarnings = onSchedule( { schedule: "every 2 hours", region: "europe-west2", timeoutSeconds: 540, memory: "512MiB" }, async () => { try { const result = await ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore(); logger.info(`ifwlAutoCreditRaceEarnings: checked ${result.raceFilesChecked || 0} race file(s), credited ${result.creditedCount} driver(s), total £${result.totalNetCredited || 0}.`); } catch (e) { logger.error("ifwlAutoCreditRaceEarnings failed", e); } } ); exports.ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions = onSchedule( // ✅ FIXED: 300s -> 540s as extra headroom on top of the concurrency fix // below - matches the timeout already used by ifwlAutoCreditRaceEarnings // just above, which fetches session files from the same servers. { schedule: "every 30 minutes", region: "europe-west2", timeoutSeconds: 540, memory: "512MiB", secrets: [LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK] }, async () => { try { const licenceBotWebhook = LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK.value(); const qualSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/qualificationWindow").get(); const qualData = qualSnap.exists ? qualSnap.data() : {}; const startMs = (qualData.enabled !== false && qualData.startDate) ? Date.parse(qualData.startDate + "T00:00:00Z") : 0; const endMs = qualData.endDate ? Date.parse(qualData.endDate + "T23:59:59Z") : Date.now(); if (!startMs) { logger.info("ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions: no qualification window set (or it's disabled) - skipping this run."); return; } const manifest = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(IFWL_MANIFEST_URL); const rawItems = manifest.results || manifest.files || []; const items = rawItems .map((x) => (typeof x === "string" ? { file: x } : { file: x.file || x.path || x.name })) .filter((x) => x.file) .map((x) => ({ file: x.file, serverId: ifwlServerIdFromPath(x.file), tsMs: ifwlFileTimestampMs(x.file.split("/").pop()) })) .filter((x) => x.serverId && x.tsMs >= startMs && x.tsMs <= endMs); if (!items.length) { logger.info("ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions: no in-window session files found for either licence server this run."); return; } // lapTotals[serverId] = Map const lapTotals = { "pc-licence": new Map(), "console-licence": new Map() }; // ✅ FIXED: was one fetch at a time, sequentially awaited - see the // comment on ifwlMapWithConcurrency above for why that was the root // cause of this function repeatedly re-posting the same "newly // qualified" list. Files are independent of each other (each just // adds its own laps to the relevant driver's total, order doesn't // matter), so there's no correctness reason they need to run one at // a time - a modest concurrency cap gets nearly all of the speedup // without hammering GitHub Pages with hundreds of simultaneous // requests. await ifwlMapWithConcurrency(items, 8, async (item) => { try { const data = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(`https://ifwlowner.github.io/ACC-driver-classifier/${item.file}`); const lines = (data.sessionResult && data.sessionResult.leaderBoardLines) || []; const map = lapTotals[item.serverId]; for (const l of lines) { if (l.bIsSpectator) continue; const d = l.currentDriver || (l.car && l.car.drivers && l.car.drivers[0]) || {}; const name = `${d.firstName || ""} ${d.lastName || ""}`.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() || d.shortName || ""; if (!name) continue; const key = name.trim().toLowerCase(); const lapCount = Number(l.timing?.lapCount || 0); map.set(key, (map.get(key) || 0) + lapCount); } } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions: skipped unreadable file ${item.file}`, e); } }); // Match qualifying drivers against every licence profile's own // accDriverNames - same substring-based match convention used // everywhere else in this file, since a session's recorded name often // carries a bracketed car number a driver's self-reported name won't. const licencesSnap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); const newlyQualified = { "pc-licence": [], "console-licence": [] }; for (const doc of licencesSnap.docs) { const p = doc.data() || {}; const aliases = (p.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) continue; for (const serverId of IFWL_QUAL_SERVERS) { const alreadyNotified = !!(p.qualNotified && p.qualNotified[serverId]); if (alreadyNotified) continue; let totalLaps = 0; for (const [driverKey, laps] of lapTotals[serverId].entries()) { if (aliases.some((a) => driverKey.includes(a) || a.includes(driverKey))) totalLaps += laps; } if (totalLaps >= IFWL_QUAL_MIN_LAPS) { newlyQualified[serverId].push({ ref: doc.ref, name: p.name || "Unnamed driver", totalLaps }); } } } for (const serverId of IFWL_QUAL_SERVERS) { const list = newlyQualified[serverId]; if (!list.length) continue; const label = serverId === "pc-licence" ? "PC" : "Console"; const shown = list.slice(0, 25); const remainder = list.length - shown.length; await publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord( licenceBotWebhook, `🏁 **IFWL Qualification Complete (${label})**\n${list.length} driver(s) have now completed their ${IFWL_QUAL_MIN_LAPS} qualification laps this season:\n${shown.map((d) => `• ${discordSafe(d.name)} (${d.totalLaps} laps)`).join("\n")}${remainder > 0 ? `\n_...and ${remainder} more_` : ""}` ); const BATCH_SIZE = 450; for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) { const batch = db.batch(); for (const entry of list.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE)) { batch.set(entry.ref, { [`qualNotified.${serverId}`]: true, updatedAt: admin.firestore.FieldValue.serverTimestamp(), updatedAtMs: Date.now() }, { merge: true }); } await batch.commit(); } } } catch (e) { logger.error("ifwlCheckQualificationCompletions failed", e); } } ); // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: SPONSORSHIP AUTO-ROLL // Runs every 60 minutes, but only actually does anything if: (a) staff have // enabled the schedule and today's day-of-week is one they picked, and // (b) it hasn't already run today. Mirrors the exact same eligibility logic // the manual "Run Sponsorship Lottery" button uses client-side - race files // identified by filename (_R.json), not the sessionType field (proven more // reliable elsewhere in this project), matched against every licence // profile's own accDriverNames via substring matching. NOTE: unlike the // qualification checker, this deliberately does NOT restrict to specific // server folders - sponsorships should apply to races on any server, not // just the two dedicated licence-qualification ones. // ======================================================= exports.ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery = onSchedule( { schedule: "every 60 minutes", region: "europe-west2", timeoutSeconds: 300, memory: "512MiB", secrets: [LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK] }, async () => { try { const scheduleSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/sponsorshipSchedule").get(); const schedule = scheduleSnap.exists ? scheduleSnap.data() : {}; if (!schedule.enabled) { logger.info("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: schedule not enabled - skipping."); return; } const now = new Date(); const todayDow = now.getUTCDay(); // 0=Sunday..6=Saturday const todayStr = now.toISOString().slice(0, 10); if (!(schedule.daysOfWeek || []).includes(todayDow)) { logger.info(`ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: today (day ${todayDow}) isn't a scheduled roll day - skipping.`); return; } if (schedule.lastRunDateStr === todayStr) { logger.info("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: already rolled today - skipping."); return; } const seasonSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonWindow").get(); const seasonData = seasonSnap.exists ? seasonSnap.data() : {}; const startMs = seasonData.startDate ? Date.parse(seasonData.startDate + "T00:00:00Z") : 0; const endMs = seasonData.endDate ? Date.parse(seasonData.endDate + "T23:59:59Z") : Date.now(); if (!startMs) { logger.info("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: no Season Window set - skipping."); return; } const poolSnap = await db.doc("league_meta/sponsorPool").get(); const sponsors = poolSnap.exists ? (poolSnap.data()?.sponsors || []) : []; if (!sponsors.length) { logger.info("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: sponsor pool is empty - skipping."); return; } const manifest = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(IFWL_MANIFEST_URL); const rawItems = manifest.results || manifest.files || []; const raceFiles = rawItems .map((x) => (typeof x === "string" ? { file: x } : { file: x.file || x.path || x.name })) .filter((x) => x.file && /_R\.json$/i.test(x.file)) .map((x) => ({ file: x.file, tsMs: ifwlFileTimestampMs(x.file.split("/").pop()) })) .filter((x) => x.tsMs >= startMs && x.tsMs <= endMs); if (!raceFiles.length) { logger.info("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: no race files found in the current season window - skipping."); // Still mark today as "run" - an empty pool of races isn't worth // retrying every 60 minutes for the rest of the day. await db.doc("league_meta/sponsorshipSchedule").set({ lastRunDateStr: todayStr }, { merge: true }); return; } const seasonDriverKeys = new Set(); for (const item of raceFiles) { try { const data = await ifwlFetchJsonPublic(`https://ifwlowner.github.io/ACC-driver-classifier/${item.file}`); const lines = (data.sessionResult && data.sessionResult.leaderBoardLines) || []; for (const l of lines) { if (l.bIsSpectator) continue; const d = l.currentDriver || (l.car && l.car.drivers && l.car.drivers[0]) || {}; const name = `${d.firstName || ""} ${d.lastName || ""}`.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim() || d.shortName || ""; if (name) seasonDriverKeys.add(name.trim().toLowerCase()); } } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: skipped unreadable file ${item.file}`, e); } } const licencesSnap = await db.collection("licences").limit(3000).get(); const eligibleDocs = licencesSnap.docs.filter((doc) => { const p = doc.data() || {}; // ✅ CHANGED: no longer excluded for already holding a deal - see // staffRunSponsorshipLottery above for the full rationale. const existingCount = Array.isArray(p.sponsorships) ? p.sponsorships.length : (p.activeSponsorship ? 1 : 0); if (existingCount >= 50) return false; const aliases = (p.accDriverNames || []).map((n) => String(n).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean); if (!aliases.length) return false; return [...seasonDriverKeys].some((dk) => aliases.some((a) => dk.includes(a) || a.includes(dk))); }); // ✅ FIXED: same Support Staff bonus + real balance credit as // staffRunSponsorshipLottery above - see that block for the full // rationale. Kept in sync manually since this is a separate scheduled // function, not a shared code path. const shopCatalogSnapAuto = await db.doc("league_meta/careerShop").get(); const shopCatalogAuto = shopCatalogSnapAuto.exists ? (shopCatalogSnapAuto.data()?.items || []) : []; // careerShopSeasonKeyNow()/careerShopTxId() live inside rtGate's own // closure and aren't reachable from this separate scheduled function - // inlined here instead, same one-line logic as those two helpers. const seasonWindowSnapAuto = await db.doc("league_meta/seasonWindow").get(); const currentSeasonKeyAuto = String(seasonWindowSnapAuto.exists ? (seasonWindowSnapAuto.data()?.startDate || "no-season-set") : "no-season-set"); // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing / Sponsorship Contracts - a driver's active // backer(s) also take their agreed % of any Sponsorship Lottery win // landed while the contract is active (not just race earnings) - "a // backer can profit off any sponsors their driver lands during the // backing terms", not only the driver's on-track results. Same // fetch-once-group-by-driver shape as the equivalent block inside // ifwlCreditRaceEarningsCore, and the same deferred second pass for // backer writes (a backer could have multiple winning drivers in one // run, so their balance is only read/written once here, not once per // winner). const backingContractsSnapAuto = await db.collection("driverBackingContracts").limit(3000).get(); const activeContractsByDriverAuto = new Map(); backingContractsSnapAuto.docs.forEach((d) => { const c = { id: d.id, ...d.data() }; if (c.status !== "active") return; const list = activeContractsByDriverAuto.get(c.driverId) || []; list.push(c); activeContractsByDriverAuto.set(c.driverId, list); }); const backingBackerDeltasAuto = new Map(); // backerId -> { delta, txEntries: [] } const winners = []; const nowMs = Date.now(); for (const doc of eligibleDocs) { if (Math.random() >= 0.05) continue; const p = doc.data() || {}; const sponsor = sponsors[Math.floor(Math.random() * sponsors.length)]; let amount = Math.round(sponsor.minAmount + Math.random() * (sponsor.maxAmount - sponsor.minAmount)); const hadSponsorshipPerk = Array.isArray(p.careerPerks) && p.careerPerks.includes("sponsorshipNetwork"); if (hadSponsorshipPerk) amount = Math.round(amount * 1.08); const isCurrentSeasonAuto = p.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKeyAuto; const ownedItemIdsAuto = isCurrentSeasonAuto && Array.isArray(p.careerShopOwnedItemIds) ? p.careerShopOwnedItemIds : []; const supportStaffBonusPctAuto = shopCatalogAuto .filter((it) => it.category === "supportStaff" && ownedItemIdsAuto.includes(it.id)) .reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.bonusPct) || 0), 0); if (supportStaffBonusPctAuto > 0) amount = Math.round(amount * (1 + supportStaffBonusPctAuto / 100)); const deal = { id: `sponsor_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, companyName: sponsor.companyName, dealDescription: sponsor.dealDescription, amount, boostedBySponsorshipPerk: hadSponsorshipPerk, boostedBySupportStaffPct: supportStaffBonusPctAuto, awardedAtMs: nowMs, acknowledged: false }; const priorBalanceAuto = isCurrentSeasonAuto ? (Number(p.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; const priorTransactionsAuto = isCurrentSeasonAuto && Array.isArray(p.careerShopTransactions) ? p.careerShopTransactions : []; const ledgerTxAuto = { id: `cstx_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, type: "sponsorship", amount, description: `Sponsorship deal - ${sponsor.companyName}`, reason: `Sponsorship deal - ${sponsor.companyName}`, isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }; // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing - apply this driver's active backers' % // against the FULL gross sponsorship amount (a lottery win is never // negative, so unlike race settlement there's no "cover a loss" // direction here - it's always a cut taken out of the driver's win). const driverActiveContractsAuto = activeContractsByDriverAuto.get(doc.id) || []; const backingTxsAuto = []; let netAmountAfterBacking = amount; for (const contract of driverActiveContractsAuto) { const cutAmount = Math.round(amount * ((Number(contract.pctBack) || 0) / 100)); if (!cutAmount) continue; netAmountAfterBacking -= cutAmount; backingTxsAuto.push({ id: `bktx_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, type: "backingSponsorshipCut", amount: -cutAmount, contractId: contract.id, counterpartId: contract.backerId, counterpartName: contract.backerName || "a sponsor", description: `Driver Backing: paid ${contract.backerName || "a sponsor"} ${cutAmount} (${contract.pctBack}% of your ${sponsor.companyName} deal)`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - sponsorship deal cut", isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }); const backerEntryAuto = backingBackerDeltasAuto.get(contract.backerId) || { delta: 0, txEntries: [] }; backerEntryAuto.delta += cutAmount; backerEntryAuto.txEntries.push({ id: `bktx_${nowMs}_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`, type: "backingSponsorshipCut", amount: cutAmount, contractId: contract.id, counterpartId: doc.id, counterpartName: p.name || "a driver", description: `Driver Backing: received ${cutAmount} from ${p.name || "a driver"}'s ${sponsor.companyName} deal (${contract.pctBack}%)`, reason: "Driver Backing contract - sponsorship deal cut", isManual: false, dateMs: nowMs, dateLabel: new Date(nowMs).toISOString() }); backingBackerDeltasAuto.set(contract.backerId, backerEntryAuto); } await doc.ref.set({ sponsorships: admin.firestore.FieldValue.arrayUnion(deal), activeSponsorship: admin.firestore.FieldValue.delete(), careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKeyAuto, careerShopBalance: priorBalanceAuto + netAmountAfterBacking, careerShopOwnedItemIds: ownedItemIdsAuto, careerShopTransactions: [ledgerTxAuto, ...backingTxsAuto, ...priorTransactionsAuto].slice(0, 200), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); winners.push({ driverName: p.name || "Unnamed driver", ...deal }); } // ✅ ADDED: Driver Backing - second pass, once per backer touched in // this whole lottery run (not once per winning driver they back). for (const [backerIdAuto, entryAuto] of backingBackerDeltasAuto.entries()) { if (!entryAuto.delta && !entryAuto.txEntries.length) continue; try { const backerRefAuto = licenceRef(backerIdAuto); const backerSnapAuto = await backerRefAuto.get(); if (!backerSnapAuto.exists) { logger.warn(`ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: Driver Backing backer ${backerIdAuto} not found, skipping cut`); continue; } const bpAuto = backerSnapAuto.data() || {}; const backerIsCurrentSeasonAuto = bpAuto.careerShopSeasonKey === currentSeasonKeyAuto; const backerBalanceAuto = backerIsCurrentSeasonAuto ? (Number(bpAuto.careerShopBalance) || 0) : 0; const backerTransactionsAuto = backerIsCurrentSeasonAuto && Array.isArray(bpAuto.careerShopTransactions) ? bpAuto.careerShopTransactions : []; await backerRefAuto.set({ careerShopSeasonKey: currentSeasonKeyAuto, careerShopBalance: backerBalanceAuto + entryAuto.delta, careerShopTransactions: [...entryAuto.txEntries, ...backerTransactionsAuto].slice(0, 200), updatedAtMs: nowMs }, { merge: true }); } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: could not settle Driver Backing backer ${backerIdAuto}`, e); } } await db.doc("league_meta/sponsorshipSchedule").set({ lastRunDateStr: todayStr }, { merge: true }); logger.info(`ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: rolled against ${eligibleDocs.length} eligible driver(s), ${winners.length} won.`); if (winners.length) { try { const webhook = LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK.value(); const lines = winners.slice(0, 25).map((w) => `**${w.driverName}** - ${w.companyName} (£${w.amount.toLocaleString()})`).join("\n"); const more = winners.length > 25 ? `\n...and ${winners.length - 25} more` : ""; await publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord(webhook, `🤝 **Sponsorship Lottery Results** - ${winners.length} driver(s) landed a deal today:\n${lines}${more}`); } catch (e) { logger.warn("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery: Discord notification failed", e); } } } catch (e) { logger.error("ifwlAutoRunSponsorshipLottery failed", e); } } ); // ======================================================= // ✅ ADDED: MARKETPLACE DISCORD POSTS // Runs every minute, but only actually posts once a driver's marketplace // activity has gone quiet for 60 seconds - so listing (or buying) several // items back-to-back lands as ONE combined Discord post instead of one per // item. New listings are grouped/posted per seller; sales are grouped/ // posted per buyer. Docs are flagged `discordPosted: false` at creation // (see listPartsForSale / purchaseMarketplaceListing) and this job only // ever looks at `discordPosted == false` docs, so it's safe to run every // minute indefinitely without ever re-posting the same activity twice. // ======================================================= async function ifwlFetchDriverNamesFor(ids) { const names = {}; const uniqueIds = [...new Set((ids || []).filter(Boolean))]; if (!uniqueIds.length) return names; const BATCH = 450; for (let i = 0; i < uniqueIds.length; i += BATCH) { const chunk = uniqueIds.slice(i, i + BATCH); const snaps = await db.getAll(...chunk.map((id) => licenceRef(id))); snaps.forEach((s, idx) => { names[chunk[idx]] = (s.exists ? s.data()?.name : "") || "Unnamed driver"; }); } return names; } async function ifwlMarkMarketplaceDocsPosted(refs) { const BATCH_SIZE = 450; for (let i = 0; i < refs.length; i += BATCH_SIZE) { const batch = db.batch(); for (const ref of refs.slice(i, i + BATCH_SIZE)) batch.set(ref, { discordPosted: true }, { merge: true }); await batch.commit(); } } exports.ifwlPostMarketplaceActivityToDiscord = onSchedule( { schedule: "every 1 minutes", region: "europe-west2", timeoutSeconds: 120, memory: "256MiB", secrets: [LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK] }, async () => { try { const webhook = LICENCE_BOT_WEBHOOK.value(); const nowMs = Date.now(); const QUIET_MS = 60 * 1000; // ---------- New listings, batched per seller ---------- const listingsSnap = await db.collection("marketplaceListings").where("discordPosted", "==", false).limit(500).get(); if (!listingsSnap.empty) { const bySeller = new Map(); const malformedRefs = []; listingsSnap.docs.forEach((d) => { const l = d.data() || {}; if (!l.sellerId) { malformedRefs.push(d.ref); return; } if (!bySeller.has(l.sellerId)) bySeller.set(l.sellerId, []); bySeller.get(l.sellerId).push({ ref: d.ref, ...l }); }); if (malformedRefs.length) await ifwlMarkMarketplaceDocsPosted(malformedRefs); const readySellers = [...bySeller.entries()].filter(([, items]) => { const latest = Math.max(...items.map((it) => Number(it.createdAtMs) || 0)); return nowMs - latest >= QUIET_MS; }); if (readySellers.length) { const sellerNames = await ifwlFetchDriverNamesFor(readySellers.map(([sellerId]) => sellerId)); for (const [sellerId, items] of readySellers) { try { const lines = items.map((it) => { const qty = (it.partType === "tyre" || it.partType === "fuel") ? (Number(it.quantity) || 0) : (Array.isArray(it.parts) ? it.parts.length : 1); // ✅ ADDED: Level 1/2/3 badge on tiered parts, so the Discord // post matches what the Marketplace UI now shows - tyres // stay untiered. const tier = (it.partType === "tyre" || it.partType === "fuel") ? null : (() => { const t = Math.round(Number(it.parts?.[0]?.tier) || 1); return t >= 1 && t <= 3 ? t : 1; })(); const tierTag = tier ? ` [LVL ${tier}]` : ""; return `• ${MKT_PART_LABEL[it.partType] || it.partType}${tierTag} x${qty} - £${(Number(it.pricePerUnit) || 0).toLocaleString()} each`; }); await publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord( webhook, `🆕 **New Marketplace Listing${items.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}**\n**Seller:** ${discordSafe(sellerNames[sellerId])}\n${lines.join("\n")}\n[View the Marketplace](${CAREER_MARKETPLACE_URL})` ); await ifwlMarkMarketplaceDocsPosted(items.map((it) => it.ref)); } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlPostMarketplaceActivityToDiscord: failed posting listings for seller ${sellerId}`, e); } } } } // ---------- Sales, batched per buyer ---------- const txSnap = await db.collection("marketplaceTransactions").where("discordPosted", "==", false).limit(500).get(); if (!txSnap.empty) { const byBuyer = new Map(); const skipRefs = []; txSnap.docs.forEach((d) => { const t = d.data() || {}; if (!t.buyerId || t.voided) { skipRefs.push(d.ref); return; } if (!byBuyer.has(t.buyerId)) byBuyer.set(t.buyerId, []); byBuyer.get(t.buyerId).push({ ref: d.ref, ...t }); }); if (skipRefs.length) await ifwlMarkMarketplaceDocsPosted(skipRefs); const readyBuyers = [...byBuyer.entries()].filter(([, items]) => { const latest = Math.max(...items.map((it) => Number(it.dateMs) || 0)); return nowMs - latest >= QUIET_MS; }); if (readyBuyers.length) { const buyerIds = readyBuyers.map(([buyerId]) => buyerId); const sellerIds = readyBuyers.flatMap(([, items]) => items.map((it) => it.sellerId)); const names = await ifwlFetchDriverNamesFor([...buyerIds, ...sellerIds]); for (const [buyerId, items] of readyBuyers) { try { const lines = items.map((it) => { // ✅ ADDED: same Level 1/2/3 tag as the listing post - tier // is stored on the transaction itself now (see mktTxTier in // purchaseMarketplaceListing). const tier = [1, 2, 3].includes(Number(it.tier)) ? Number(it.tier) : null; const tierTag = tier ? ` [LVL ${tier}]` : ""; return `• ${MKT_PART_LABEL[it.partType] || it.partType}${tierTag} x${Number(it.quantity) || 1} - £${(Number(it.pricePerUnit) || 0).toLocaleString()} each (from ${discordSafe(names[it.sellerId])})`; }); const totalSpent = items.reduce((sum, it) => sum + (Number(it.totalPaid) || 0), 0); await publishLicenceUpdateToDiscord( webhook, `💰 **Marketplace Sale${items.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}**\n**Buyer:** ${discordSafe(names[buyerId])} (total spent: £${totalSpent.toLocaleString()})\n${lines.join("\n")}\n[View the Marketplace](${CAREER_MARKETPLACE_URL})` ); await ifwlMarkMarketplaceDocsPosted(items.map((it) => it.ref)); } catch (e) { logger.warn(`ifwlPostMarketplaceActivityToDiscord: failed posting sales for buyer ${buyerId}`, e); } } } } } catch (e) { logger.error("ifwlPostMarketplaceActivityToDiscord failed", e); } } );
